yāṉ aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka ērāḷamāṉa nūlkaḷil ārāyntu kaṇṭa muṭivukaḷi nūlkaḷāka eḻutiyirukkiṉṟēṉ.
pāṟkaṭalik kaṭintu amutam eṭuttatu pōla, uṇmi kāṇum uṇarcciyiṉāl naṭunili niṉṟu ārāyntirukkiṉṟēṉ. 
oruvar tam vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum kaṟṟālum aṟiyamuṭiyāta uṇmikaḷi ellām eḷitāka eḻuti vittirukkiṉṟēṉ.
ellārum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ yāvaṟṟiyum kaṟkum tolliyi mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya tilli, atu muṭiyavum muṭiyātu, tēviyumilli. 
ellā nūlkaḷilum uḷḷa ciṟanta uṇmikaḷi ellām piḻi cāṟāka yāṉ vaṭittut tantuḷḷēṉ.
eṉ nūlkaḷip paṭittāṟpōtum, ataṉāl, tamiḻ muḻutuṅ kaṟṟa payaṉi aṭiyalām.
- maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ



cāṉṟitaḻ
nāki - tiru. vētācalam piḷḷiyavarkaḷ orupōtu tiruvaṉantapurattil oruvāran taṅkiyirunta ñāṉṟu, avaruṭaṉ neruṅkip paḻakum vāyppuk kiṭittataṟkuc cālavum makiḻkiṉṟēm. varuṅkālat tamiḻ makkaḷukku maṭṭaṟṟa tamiḻppulami mikuntu varumeṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟāka ivar iḷamiyilēyē nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulam uṭiyarāyiruppatu kaḷippūṭṭukiṟatu. maṟṟum tāymoḻip paṟṟuk kuṟinta ikkālattil, ivar tamatu nuṇṇaṟivit tamiḻ nūlkaḷil muṟṟum celutti naṉkārāyntuḷḷatum pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatu. pattuppāṭṭu, kalittoki, cīvakacintāmaṇi mutaliya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil ārāycciyuṭaṉ kūṭiya ivartam aṟivuṭimiyē eṉṉi ivarpāl matippuṟumāṟu ceytatu. tamiḻāciriyarkaḷiṉ toki curuṅkivarum ikkālattil ivarukkuc ciṟanta ūkkamaḷittāl ivar oru ciṟanta tamiḻāciriyarāvāreṉṟu nām uṟutiyāka nampukiṉṟēm. muṉṉarē iraṇṭāntarak kallūrikaḷil tamiḻkaṟpikkap pōtumāṉa aḷavu ilakkaṇa ilakkiyattiṟ ciṟanta tērcci peṟṟavarāyirukkiṉṟāreṉak karutukiṉṟōm. Fiwªj msÉš v~¥, V., (F.A.) vakuppukkuriya āṅkila aṟivu peṟṟārāṉāl mēlnāṭṭu muṟippaṭi tamiḻ nūlkaḷi varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟuṭaṉ ārāyavum eḻutavum vallunarāvāreṉpatu emmuṭiya ciṟanta eṇṇam.

vaṇṇārappēṭṭi	maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntarampiḷḷi
tirunelvēli	tamiḻttāy vāḻttiṉ  āciriyar
2-12-1895
(inta naṟcāṉṟu tirunelvēli intuk kallūriyil cuntaram piḷḷiyavarkaḷ pērāciriyarāka iruntapōtu koṭukkap peṟṟatu.)

māṇpukaḻ oḷivīci niṟkum
tamiḻ moḻikkum civaneṟikkum aṭikaḷ aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka arumpaṇiyāṟṟi yuḷḷārkaḷ. tamiḻ moḻiyip pōlavē, aṭikaḷ āṅkilattiyum vaṭamoḻiyiyum naṉku kaṟṟavarkaḷ. cāntuṇiyum kaṟka vēṇṭum eṉṟa cātaṉi paṭittavarkaḷ aṭikaḷ. ākavē, tām tēṭiya poruḷiyellām oru periya nūl niliyamākat tikaḻumpaṭi ceytu vittārkaḷ. aṭikaḷiṉ tamiḻ uri naṭi nūlkaḷ tamiḻiṉ taṉittūymi nilikkuc cāṉṟāka miḷirkiṉṟaṉa. eṇṇaṟṟa coṉmārikaḷ poḻintu makkaḷukku uṇarcciyi ūṭṭivanta aṭikaḷ maṟintu viṭṭaṉar. tamiḻ uḷḷaḷavum maṟimaliyaṭikaḷiṉ māṇpukaḻum maṅkātu oḷivīci niṟkum.
- ṭākṭar e.ji. maṇavāḷarāmānucam
tuṇivēntar, aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakam.
(pak. 40)

maṟimaliya māṇpu
nuḻivuri
taṉittamiḻ eṉṟa aḷavil muṉ niṟkum muḻuturu eḻilvaṭivar
tavattiru. maṟimaliyaṭikaḷārē āvar!
munti aṭikaḷ ākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, `muttamiḻ aṭikaḷ eṉa muḻakkamiṭa niṉṟār!
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷō, taṉittamiḻt tanti eṉṉum takavārnta perumi peṟṟār.
tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi vaḻiyatu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻit tuṇiyiṉṟi iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, ulaka moḻikaḷuk kellām tāymoḻi vaṭamoḻiyē eṉṟum  tamiḻk kalvi valla peṟṟiyarum muṟṟum mayaṅki yurikka,
vaṭamoḻi ulaka moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻi eṉṟāl, ataṟkum maṟṟi moḻikaḷukkum tanti moḻi tamiḻē eṉṟu kūṟiya vaṭalūr aṭikaḷār pukaḻparappāḷarāyp potunilik kaḻakam kaṇṭa tava aṭikaḷār, taṉittamiḻt tanti eṉṉum perumi peṟṟa maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār āvar.
civaṉiyam cārnta perumakkaḷum, pēraṟivarkaḷum kūṭa, oppārāy, `taṉittamiḻ (taṉittu amiḻ) eṉṟu paḻikkavum iḻikkavum, tuṇintu niṉṟu, avar veḷka; veḷḷiṭi maliyākat `taṉittamiḻ eṉpati nili nāṭṭiya niṟimali, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷārē!
civaṉiya maṭattiṉarum, vaṭamoḻi vallārum, `tol kāppiyak  kaṭal eṉap pukaḻappaṭṭārumām cāmināta tēcikar, nāṇamiṉṟi, 
 ainteḻuttāl orupāṭi eṉṟu aṟiyavē nāṇuvar
  aṟivuṭiyōrē; yāmum atuvē
eṉṟum,
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭō?
eṉṟum - eḻutip parappiṉārāka,
oru col tāṉum vēṟṟuc col viravā nūl yām ceyvēm eṉṟu aimpāṉ nāṉku nūlkaḷ kalimali pēḻiyāy eḻutik kāṭcip paṭuttiya māṭciyar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār!
tolkāppiyak kāval
tamiḻ moḻikku vāytta tolkāppiya vaḷanūl,
 vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
  eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē	(884)
eṉṟum,
moḻi peyarppuc ceytalilum muṟimi pēṇa vēṇṭum eṉpati,
moḻipeyarttu atarppaṭa yāttal	(1597)
eṉṟum,
pēccu vaḻakkil oru kāl vēṟṟuccol vantu viṭiṉum,
 marapunili tirital ceyyuṭ killi 	(1590)
eṉṟum - varampāṇi ceytum, ac cevviyal vaḻittaṭam citintum cīraḻintum, cēṟum cakatiyum nāṟum ciṟumiyum kuṟumiyum vaḻivaḻiyē eyta mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷāy, ayalōr cūḻcciyālum tiṟavōr mayakkālum ēṟpaṭṭa aḻipāṭukaḷi ellām tāmē ōr iyakkamāy iruntu, viḻippum eḻucciyum ūṭṭiyavar maṟimali aṭikaḷē eṉpatāl,
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻi mīṭṭeṭuppa taṟkāka arum pāṭupaṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa marapōṭu yāṉ kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu eṉṉum pāvāṇar,
mūvā yiramāṇṭu mōtum vaṭamoḻiyāl
 cāvām takiniṉṟa taṇṭamiḻi - mēvāk
 kumarit taṉinilikkuk koṇṭuvanta  appar
 amarar maṟimali yār
eṉap pāṭip paraviṉār. `taṉit tamiḻt tantiyār eṉṟa pārāṭṭum ip pāṭṭiṉ talippil vaḻaṅkiṉār.
 nuṇṇiya nūlpalavum kaṟpatē nōṉpāka
 eṇṇiya vāḻnāḷ elāmaḷittup - paṇṇiya
 centamiḻnūl ovvoṉṟum ceppum kaṭalaṟivi
 pintamiḻ kākkum paṭi
eṉa aṭikaḷ kalvi mēmpāṭṭiyum kalit tiṟap paṭippukaḷiṉ mēmpāṭṭiyum pāvēntar pāratitācaṉār pārāṭṭukiṟār.
paltuṟi āṟṟal
aṭikaḷiṉ paltuṟi āṟṟali 1. pērāciriyar, 2. perum pulavar, 3. pāvalar, 4. ārāycciyāḷar, 5. mummoḻip pulavar, 6. moḻipeyarppāḷar, 7. coṟpoḻi vāḷar, 8. eḻuttāḷar, 9. palkalic celvar, 10. taṉittamiḻt tantiyār eṉṟu pāvāṇar pakuttuk kāṭṭukiṟār.
mēlum avar tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulatti
paṉimali mukaṭṭiṉ uyarvum, nīla āṟṟiṉ nīḷamum amiti vāriyiṉ āḻamum amintavar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eṉkiṟār. (paṉimali  - imayam; nīla āṟu - nil; amitivāri - pacipikku mākaṭal).
aṭikaḷār nūlkaḷ
aṭikaḷ paṉmāṇ māttamiḻ nūlkaḷi, nūlaka iyakkat tanti araṅka nātarum, innāḷ civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻaka āṭciyarum annāḷ nūlakarumākiya tirumiku irā. muttuk kumāracāmi avarkaḷum 14 vakimippaṭutti vittami perum pārāṭṭukku uriyatām avi,
1. maruttuvam  2. maṟiporuḷ  3. ilakkiyam 4. itaḻkaḷ  5. caṅka  ilakkiyam  6. pāṭal 7. nāṭakam  8. putiṉam 9. kaṭitam 10. kaṭṭuri 11. camayam  12. tattuvam 13. varalāṟu 14. camūka iyal eṉpaṉa.
mutaṟ kuṟaḷ vāta nirākaraṇam eṉṉum maṟuppu nūlē, aṭikaḷiṉ mutal nūlāka veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu (1898) annūṉ maṟuppu nayamē nāki aṭikaḷi - pallavapura aṭikaḷāka -taṉittamiḻ aṭikaḷāka ākka arum perum tūṇṭal āyiṟṟām.
aṭikaḷār varalāṟu: kīḻkaṭal cār nākappaṭṭiṉattic cārnta ciṟṟūr kāṭampāṭi, āṅku vāḻnta aruntavap peruntakiyar cokkanātar - ciṉṉammiyār! ivviṇiyar koṭiyāyt tamiḻaṉṉi ceyta tavap pēṟṟāl piṟantavar vētācalam. piṟanta peru maṅkala nāḷ ki.pi. 15.07.1876.
cokkanātar, cittamalam aṟuvitta cittamaruttuvar; aṟiva maruttuvam eṉpatum atu. attoḻil avarkku vaḷatti vaḻaṅkiyatu. ataṉāl vētācalam kuṟivaṟa vāḻntār.
nākappaṭṭiṉattu veciliyaṉ micaṉ paḷḷiyil aintu vakuppuvari payiṉṟār; ciṟanta māṇavarāka viḷaṅkiṉār. āṟām vakuppil cērum niliyil, arumit tantiyār iyaṟki eytiṉār. āyiṉum aṉṉiyār uṟutiyāl kalviyit toṭarntār. appaḷḷiyil āṅkilak kalvikku irunta mutaṉmi tamiḻukku illi. kiṟittava camayam paṟṟi aṉṟic civaṉiyam paṟṟi aṟiya vāyppilli eṉṉum iraṇṭu kuṟikaḷiyum iḷamiyilēyē uṇarntār.
vīṭṭilēyē tamiḻ nūlkaḷip payiṉṟu mutaṟ kuṟiyiyum, nākiyil irunta intumatāpimāṉa caṅkam vaḻi iraṇṭām kuṟiyiyum nīkkiṉār. paḷḷiyilum kuṟiyaṟak kaṟṟār. varuvāyk kuṟiyāl vāṭum aṉṉikkuc cumiyāy illāmal taṉiyē payilvatē iṉiyavaḻi eṉa eṇṇi, nākiyil puttaka vaṇikarāka viḷaṅkiya nārāyaṇa cāmiyār pulamiyālum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyavar. ātalāl avariṭam taṉiyē ilakkiya  - ilakkaṇa - camaya nūlkaḷik kaṟṟār. nārāyaṇariṉ muṉṉi māṇavar pērā. maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntaraṉār eṉpatiyum aṟintār. oṉpatām vakuppoṭu paḷḷik kalviyi niṟuttit taṉikkalviyē kaṟṟār. tolkāppiyam, toki nūlkaḷ, tirukkuṟaḷ, tirucciṟṟampalak kōvi, iṟiyaṉār akap poruḷ eṉakkaṟṟār.
aṭikaḷār iyaṟṟiya tiruvoṟṟi murukar mummaṇik kōvi mukappil, ematu patiṉintām āṇṭil muṟippaṭi tuvaṅkiya tamiḻp payiṟciyāṉatu ematu irupattōrām āṇṭiṟkuḷ tolkāppiyam, tirukkuṟaḷ, ciṟṟampalak kōviyār eṉṉum mūṉṟu nūlkaḷiyum muḻutum neṭṭuruc ceytu muṭittēm eṉṟum vēṟucila nūlkaḷiyum muḻuvatāka ōtiyatiyum kūṟi,
iṅṅaṉamāka, viḻumiya tamiḻppaḻa nūlkaḷil ematu karuttu īrppuṇṭu niṉṟu payiṉṟa payiṟciyiṉālēyē ceyyuḷum uriyum taṉic centamiḻ naṭiyil eḻutum tiṟam ematu iḷamip paruvattilēyē emakku vāyppa tāyiṟṟu eṉkiṟār. avvāṇṭu ki.pi. 1897. nāki intu matāpimāṉa caṅkattiṟkuc civa cittānta caṇṭa marutam eṉṉum virutu peṟṟa cōmacuntara nāyakar vantu coṟpoḻivāṟṟiṉār. avar poḻivil vanta karuttoṉṟaṉi maṟuttu oruvar eḻuta, ataṟku maṟuppāka murukavēḷ eṉṉum puṉipeyaril maṟuppuri eḻutiṉār. ataṉikkaṟṟa nāyakar, mīḷap poḻivukku varuṅkāl muruka vēḷic cantikka virumpiṉār. accantippiṉ payaṉē uṉṉi virivilē ceṉṉikku varuvippōm. nī antap pakkaṅkaḷil iruppatē nallatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. ituvē aṭikaḷārkkuc ceṉṉi vāḻvu amiya mutal tūṇṭal āyiṟṟu. aṭikaḷukkup patiṉēḻām vayatil (1893) tirumaṇam nikaḻntatu. tuṇivi tam māmaṉ makaḷ cavuntara valli eṉpār.
tiruvaṉantapuram
nārāyaṇacāmi avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉi māṇavarām pērā. cuntaraṉār tiruvaṉanta purattil iruntār. avariyaṟṟiya maṉōṉmaṇīya nāṭaka nūl kaṟṟu ataṉ arumi kaṇṭu, pērāciriyarik kāṇavirumpiṉār. ataṉāl tam āciriyarōṭu tiruvaṉantapuram ceṉṟu pērāciriyarōṭu uriyāṭiṉār. vētācalaṉār iḷam paruvattiyum mūtaṟiviyum aṟinta cuntaraṉār viyappuṟṟut takutic cāṉṟu oṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉār. ciṟitu kālam tiruvaṉanta purattil āciriyap paṇiyum ceytār. avviṭattiṉ paruvanili ovvāmiyāl nākikkē tirumpiṉār.
ceṉṉi: cōmacuntaraṉār muṉṉē kūṟiyapaṭi vētācalaric ceṉṉikku aḻittu aṟiñar nallucāmi eṉpār naṭāttiya cittānta tīpiki eṉṉum itaḻāciriyar ākkiṉār. piṉṉē ceṉṉi kiṟittavak kallūrikku oru tamiḻp pērāciriyar vēṇṭiyiruttal aṟinta cōmacuntaraṉār vētācalaṉā ra akkallūrip pērāciriyarākka muyaṉṟār. atukāl tamiḻttuṟit talivarāka iruntavar vi.kō cūriyanārāyaṇa cāttiriyār eṉṉum paritimāl kaliñar. kallūri mutalvar villiyam millar eṉpār.
9.3.1898 il vētācalaṉār paṇiyēṟṟār. aṅkup patiṉmūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ paṇiyāṟṟiyuḷḷār.
akkallūrik kālattē iḷaṅkali māṇavarkkup pāṭamāka irunta mullippāṭṭu (1903) paṭṭiṉappāli (1906) eṉpavaṟṟukku ārāycci uri eḻutiṉār.
avvuri nūli vāṅkik kaṟṟa māṇākkarum ēṉit tamiḻaṟiñarum putumuṟiyāka eḻutappaṭṭa avvurip pāṅkiṉip piṉpaṟṟip paḻiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷi ārāyvatil kiḷarcci peṟalāṉār. ipputumuṟi uri, tamiḻ nūlārāyum vakikaḷi iṉitu viḷakki aṟiñar palarkkum aka makiḻcci yiṉi viḷittami kaṇṭu iṉi, ittakiya urikaḷiyē eḻututal vēṇṭum eṉa ematuḷḷam peritum viḻintatu eṉkiṟār.
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattārāṅkilamum vaṭa moḻiyum kaṭṭāyamākap paṭittal vēṇṭum eṉṟum, tāymoḻip pāṭatti viruppap pāṭamākak kaṟpikkalām eṉṟum oru tīrmāṉam āṭcikkuḻuvil koṇṭu vantaṉar. oru pāṭam viruppappāṭam eṉappaṭṭa piṉṉar ataṉik kaṟka varuvār evar? tamiḻ kaṟkum māṇavar eṇṇikki kuṟiyavē āciriyap paṇiyiṭamum kuṟiya vēṇṭuvatāki, aṭikaḷār 31.1. 1911 ām nāḷuṭaṉ kallūrip paṇiyi muṭikka vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu.
māṇavar: ikkālattil aṭikaḷiṉ māṇavarāka iruntāruḷ, taṇikimaṇi ceṅkalvarāyar, iracikamaṇi ṭi.kē.ci, nāvalar ca.cō. pārati, pēraṟiñar ca. viyāpuriyār mutalōr cilar.
taṇikimaṇiyiṉ, tēvāra tiruvācakac collaṭivu oru piṟappaḷavil ceyyak kūṭiyatā?
nāvalar ca. cō. pāratiyāriṉ āyvut tuṇivu ettaṉi kacaṭukaḷi nīkkiyatu? viyāpuriyār, kāla āyvilum, vaṭamoḻip piṭivātattilum tammic cālpu āyvil iruntu vilakkiṉālum, caṅka ilakkiyap patippu, nikaṇṭuppatippu, palkalik kaḻaka akaramutalip patippu ivaṟṟic ceyta arumi eṉṉa? puṟattiraṭṭu, kaḷaviyal, kāriki āyavaṟṟiṉ patippuc ciṟappu eṉṉa?
ivarkaḷ paṇiyum aṭikaḷārit taḻuvutal uṇmi tāṉē!
camaraca caṉmārkkam
kallūrip paṇiyil iruntu vīṭupeṟṟa aṭikaḷār, pallavapuratti vāḻviṭamākak koṇṭār. camaraca caṉmārkka niliyam tōṟṟuvittār. 
ellāc cātiyārkkum ellāc camayattārkkum aṅkīkāramāṉa cīvakāruṇiya oḻukkattiyum, camaraca caṉmārk kattiyum piṟkālattil mika valiyuṟuttip pōtitta āciriyar srīmat irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷē āvār. accuvāmi kaḷ valiyuṟuttiya avviraṇṭu koḷkikaḷiyum eṅkum valiyuṟutti viḷakki aṉparkaḷi oruṅku kūṭṭa vēṇṭi accuvāmikaḷ iṭṭa peyarālēyē, `camaraca caṉmārkka niliyam eṉṉum itu tāpikkalāyiṟṟu. inniliyattiṟku muṉ āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār; piṉ āciriyar irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷ āvar eṉṟu peyarīṭu ceytu koḷkikaḷiyum vakukkiṟār.
kuṭumpam
1898il aṭikaḷār ceṉṉikku varumpōtu avartam aṉṉiyār ciṉṉammiyār, tuṇiviyār cavuntiram ammi yār, celvi cintāmaṇi, aṭikaḷār eṉṉum nālvarik koṇṭa kuṭumpamāka iruntatu.
1911il pallava purattil kuṭipukum pōtu nīlāmpiki, tiruñāṉa campantam, māṇikkavācakam, tirunāvukkaracu, cuntara mūrtti eṉṉum makkaḷum koṇṭa oṉpatiṉmar kuṭumpamāka iruntatu. pallavuram pukunta piṉ tiripura cuntari piṟakkap patiṉmaṉ kuṭumpam āyiṟṟu. inniliyilēyē 27.08.1911il civat tuṟaviyarkkuriya tuṟavāṭi (kāvi) koṇṭu cuvāmi vētācalam āṉār. cuvāmi vētācalam eṉpataṉ taṉittamiḻākkamē maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. muṉṉēyē avari aṭikaḷ eṉṟatu avartam māṇpu karutiyēyām.
mīḷpārvi
nām mīḷpārvi oṉṟupārkka vēṇṭum.
aṭikaḷ 21ām akaviyilēyē taṉittamiḻ āyvuttiṟam kivarap peṟṟār.
1903, 1907 ākiya āṇṭukaḷil mullippāṭṭu, paṭṭiṉappāli ākiya nūlkaḷukku āyvuri vaḻaṅkuṅkāl itiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ ittaṉi; ivaṟṟuḷ centamiḻccol ittaṉi; vēṟṟuc col ittaṉi - eṉa aṟutiyiṭukiṟār.
ātalāl, taṉittamiḻ eṉṉum tōṟṟam 1916 ām āṇṭil nīlāmpikiyār tūṇṭalāl ēṟpaṭṭami eṉpatu, akattē amintu kiṭanta vittu, muḷi viṭṭu veḷippaṭṭa niliyām. av veḷippaṭṭa niliyum uṭaṉē paṟṟip paṭaravilli! mēlum cila āṇṭukaḷiṉ piṉṉarēyē camacara caṉmārkka niliyam, potunilik kaḻakamākavum, ñāṉacākaram aṟivuk kaṭalākavum, cuvāmi vētācalam maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ ākavum paṭippaṭiyē kiḷarkiṉṟaṉavām!
ōr ariya amippu uruvākum niliyum, ōr ariya kaṇṭu piṭippu ulakavar aṟiya veḷippaṭalum, avi iyakkamākavum payaṉākavum ākiṉṟa niliyum paṟṟiya viḷakkam, aṭikaḷār taṉittamiḻ iyakka varalāṟṟil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷatām!
etiriṭit tūṇṭal: mēlum oṉṟu: oru nēriṭit tūṇṭaliṉum etiriṭit tūṇṭal valimi mikkatu eṉpatum, uḷḷattu uṟutiyi veḷḷap perukkāy veḷippaṭutta vallatu eṉpatum teḷivām.
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭā? eṉṟu kaṟṟaṟinta tamiḻar oruvarē viṉāvi nakittal, peṟṟa tāy moḻip piṟavikku uṟṟa takimi ākātu eṉpatum, ataṉi uṇarnta appiṟavi pāmpaṉ kurupāta aṭikaḷ pōl cēntaṉ centamiḻ eṉṉa oru nūliyaṟṟit tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkavalla taṉi uyar moḻi eṉṟu nilināṭṭiyiruppiṉ vaḻipaṭattakka piṟaviyāy amintirukkum! appēṟu ataṟku vāykkāmi,
 tuṟantārkkut tuppuravu vēṇṭi maṟantārkol 
  maṟṟi yavarkaḷ tavam	(kuṟaḷ 263)
eṉṉum vaḷḷuvar vāy moḻippaṭi,
taṉittamiḻt tantip pēṟu maṟimaliyaṭikaṭku vara iruppataṟkē nēriṭṭa nērcciyām eṉka.
iyakkam
aṭikaḷ iyakkam ālamarameṉak kālpala koṇṭu akala akala viriyat taṉittamiḻ iyakkamāy, ulakattamiḻk kaḻakamāy, pāvalarēṟu tamiḻk kaḷamāy, cērar koṟṟamāy, tamiḻkkāppuk kaḻakamāy, tamiḻp pātukāppuk kaḻakamāy, taṉittamiḻ ilakkiyak kaḻakamāy, tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakamāy, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ maṉṟamāy, tamiḻkkāval kuḻuvāy, iyaṟṟamiḻp payiṟṟakamāy, pāvāṇar tamiḻ iyakkamāy āṅkāṅku mukiḻttu muṉippoṭu kaṭaṉāṟṟa ēviyatām.
nūlakam
aṭikaḷ nūṟṟoki, arumaṇik kuviyaṉṉa nālāyiram nūlkaḷik koṇṭatu. etiratu uṇarntu, civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattiṟku muṟkoṭiyāka vaḻaṅkiyatāl, nālāyiram nāṟpatāyiramāy mēlum mēlum virintu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ nūlakap peyarāl, perumimiku vaḷḷalār mutaṟkaṇ poḻivu ceyta periyavar cōmu avarkaḷ illamē vāykka ulaka vaḷamākkiyatām! ataṉ vaḻiyākavē aṭikaḷ tāmē veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷum, veḷiyiṭāmal kaḻaka vaḻi veḷivanta nūlkaḷum, aṭikaḷ kālattiṟkup piṉ veḷivanta nūlkaḷum ākiya muḻumit tokutikaḷum ki.pi. 1898 mutal 1969 vari mutaṟpatippāka veḷivantavi. avaṟṟuḷ paṭikaḷ kiṭṭutaṟku ariyaviyum uṇṭu. aṭikaḷ nūlkaḷ nāṭṭuṭimi ākkappaṭṭa pēṟṟāl, iṉṟu vāytta nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum oṭṭumottamāka vaḻaṅkum peruvāyppit tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peṟṟatām.
veḷḷac cētam
aṇmiyil ēṟpaṭṭa veḷḷap perukku patippakattuḷ pukuntu accu aṭikkappaṭṭatum, aṭikkappaṭa iruntatum, kaṇiṉippaṭutti vitta kuṟuvaṭṭukaḷum,  mūlanūlkaḷum eṉa vāri aḷḷik koṇṭu pōṉami, kaḷattukku vanta katir maṇikaḷ, kaḷañciyattiṟku vārāmal pōya, pēraḻivu oppatām. ātalāl, nūlkaḷi mīḷat tēṭavum, kaṇiṉippaṭuttavum, kuṟuvaṭṭu ākki acciṭavum eṉa mummaṭaṅkup paṇiyum pēriḻappum perutta avalamum uṇṭākkiṟṟām. anniliyil,
ūḻiyum uppakkam kāṇpar uliviṉṟit
  tāḻā tuñaṟṟu pavar
eṉṉum vāymoḻiyi vāymi moḻiyākkiya tuṇivuc celvar iḷavaḻakaṉārum avar kuṭumpattavarum āvar!
muṉṉaṟivipput tiṭṭappaṭi nūlukku ārvamāyp paṇam celuttiyōrkkum nūl kālattil kiṭṭā uḷiccal illāmal pōkātē! avarkaḷum amintu tāṅkal, iyaṟki iyaṅkiyal vaḻiyatām.
veḷiyīṭukaḷ
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam tamiḻmoḻi iṉa maṇvaḷam - vaḷarcci mīṭṭeṭuppu - eṉpavaṟṟi muṉvittut tamiḻp pōrāḷi iḷavaḻakaṉārāl toṭaṅkappaṭṭatāl, pēriḻappukkām eṉat teḷivākat terintum, icik kalikku vāytta oppaṟṟa kaḷañciyamāy vāytta - karuṇāmirta cākaratti veḷiyiṭṭatām! muṉṉi vanta mutaṟpakuti pēriḻappākkiyum ataṉ piṉṉip pakutikaḷiyum aritiṉ muyaṉṟu veḷiyiṭṭatu! moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar; īḻattu aṟiñar na.ci. kantiyā;  varalāṟṟaṟiñar cāmināta carmā, urivēntar auvi cu.tu; nāvalar na.mu.vē; paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpālar; cāmi citamparaṉār; mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭa cāmiyār; pēraṟiñar ka. veḷḷi vāraṇaṉār; pāvalar kuḻantiyār; pāvalar muṭiyaracaṉār; pāvēntar, tol poruḷ kalveṭṭu āyñar catācivaṉār, cāttaṉkuḷam irākavaṉ  nūlkaḷiyum eḷiyēṉ nūlkaḷiyum muḻumi muḻumiyāka veḷiyiṭṭatu.
tolkāppiyam- pāṭṭu - toki - kāppiyam - kīḻkkaṇakku - tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu - āṅkilam valla aṟiñar irāma nātaṉār nūlkaḷiyum, yāḻppāṇa akarāti mutalām akarātit tokutikaḷiyum veḷiyiṭṭatu. patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutitoṭṭu, irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakuti vari veḷivanta - tamiḻukku ākkamām nāṭ ṭuṭimi ākkappaṭṭa nūlkaḷi ellām - ōriṭattup peṟṟu, oru taṉittamiḻ nūlakam amikkum aḷavukku acciṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya perumi iḷavaḻakaṉārkku uṇṭu. avarē aṉṟi avar tuṇiyiyum makkaḷiyum uṟaviyum aṉpiyum naṇpiyum oruṅkē iṇittu, aṟiñar perumakkaḷ palar aravaṇippiyum vāyppak koṇṭu vāḻum iyalpu avarkku vāyttalāl maṭṭumē, ivvāṟu ceyaṟkariya ceyya vāyttatām eṉpataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭākat tikaḻpavarum avar.
maṟimaliya māṇpu
maṟimaliyam, taṉittamiḻ iyakkam! tamiḻt taṉip peruvaḷam! muttamiḻc curappu! cīrtiruttac ceḻumi! vāḻviyal vaḷam! kalic collākkak kaḷañciyam! paṇṭit tamiḻar varalāṟṟup putiyal! āyviyal vaḻikāṭṭi! poḻivup polivu! eḻuttiṉ eḻil! moḻik kāval muṉiyam! muṭṭaṟukkum muḻutuṟu koṭi! mīṭṭeṭukkum mētakimi! aḷḷa aḷḷak kuṟiyā aṇimaṇik kuviyal! eṉal meymi viḷakkamām!
 puttakam ēṟṟup polivatē puttakam
 vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
tiruvaḷḷuvar niliyam,	iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
7, irāmaṉ teru,	irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ
tirunakar, maturi - 6
625006.

malikka vikkum maṟimaliyam!
patippuri
moḻiyē iṉamakkaḷ muṉṉēṟa nalla
vaḻiyām ataṉi vaḷarkka -paḻiyāyt
tamiḻayarat tāḻntāṉ tamiḻaṉ avaṉē
tamiḻuyarat tāṉuyarvāṉ tāṉ
moḻippēraṟiñar pāvāṇar avarkaḷ `moḻiyiṉ vaḻiyākavē moḻipēcum makkaḷ muṉṉēṟa muṭiyum eṉak kūṟukiṟār. ulakam muḻutum ivvuṇmi uṇarappaṭṭuḷḷati varalāṟu kāṭṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇari aṟivulakam cariyāka aṭiyāḷam kāṇpataṟkuk kāraṇamāṉatu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār tanta pārāṭṭu!
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ (15.7.1876 - 15.9.1950) tēvanēyap pāvāṇar (7.2.1902 - 16.1.1981) iruvarum taṉittamiḻiyakkattit tāṅki niṟkum tūṇkaḷākap pōṟṟappaṭupavarkaḷ.
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār 1916il tōṟṟuvitta taṉittamiḻ iyakkam, nūṟṟāṇṭi eṭṭiyuḷḷa makiḻvāṉa nēram itu!
taṉittamiḻiyakka nūṟṟāṇṭi niṉivu kūrum vakiyil, taṉittamiḻiyakkat tanti maṟimaliyaṭikaḷāriṉ eḻuttu, coṟpoḻivu, nāṭkuṟippu, kaṭitaṅkaḷ aṉittiyum `maṟimaliyam eṉum peruntokuppākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam vaḻi veḷikkoṇarat tiṭṭamiṭṭōm.
ulakam muḻutum paravi vāḻum uṇarvu kuṉṟāta tamiḻarkaḷ tanta ūkkuvippu, intap peruntiṭṭatti niṟivēṟṟit tīravēṇṭum eṉum uṟutiyi eṉakkut tantatu.
taṉittamiḻ iyakka aṟiñarkaḷum ārvalarkaḷum aṭikaḷāriṉ ariya paṭippukaḷ palavaṟṟiyum tēṭittēṭi vaḻaṅki vaḻikāṭṭiṉar; valimi yūṭṭiṉar.
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār 1898ām āṇṭiliruntu eḻuti vantuḷḷār. aṟivukkaṭal (ñāṉacākaram) itaḻi 1902ām āṇṭiliruntu veḷiyiṭṭu vantuḷḷār. avariṉ paṭippukaḷ peruveḷḷamāy veḷivantirukkiṉṟaṉa.
accēṟātavi, peyar kūṭak kēṭṭaṟiyātavi, peyar terintum pārvikkuk kiṭṭātavi - eṉap palavakiyāyk kiṭittaṉa aṭikaḷāriṉ arumpaṭippukaḷ! avaṟṟi `maṟimaliyam eṉum talippil 34 tokuppukaḷāka orucērap pārkkumpōtu, tamiḻulakam viyappaṭivatu uṟuti.
moḻinalam iṉanalam pēṇutal aṉṟō
muḻunalam peṟuvaḻi! nāmpeṟal eṉṟō?
ippaṭik kavaliyōṭu kēṭpār pāvalarēṟu peruñ cittiraṉār (10.3.1933 - 11.6.1995). avariṉ `teṉmoḻi itaḻ 1.8.1959 iliruntu veḷivarat toṭaṅkiya piṉputāṉ, taṉittamiḻt tici nōkki nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉōr naṭipōṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. atil nāṉum oruvaṉ.
peruñcittiraṉāriṉ eḻuttu kāṭṭiya aṭiyāḷattāl, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ eṉum aṟivupputiyal palar kikaḷilum kuṭiyēṟiyatu.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ valiyuṟuttip pēciyatāl, maṟimali yaṭikaḷārit tēṭippayilum ārvam tamiḻulakil puttuyir peṟṟatu.
āriyatti etirppatilum camaṟkirutatti vilakku vatilumē tamiḻiṉa mēmpāṭum tamiḻk kāppum aṭaṅkiyirukkiṟatu. avvuṇarcciyit tamiḻar neñcaṅkaḷil arumpa vittatu tirāviṭar iyakkam; malarntu viriyavittatu taṉittamiḻ iyakkam! kumuka, araciyal, paṇpāṭṭu malarccikku inta īriyakkaṅkaḷiṉ pēruḻippup payaṉpaṭṭatu.
tamiḻakattil taṉittamiḻ uṇarvu ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ taḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu; 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ cariyat toṭaṅkiyatu. vaṭamoḻi veḷḷattil tamiḻ iḻuttuc cellappaṭṭu viṭāmal kāppāṟṟiyōr kamparum pavaṇantiyum! piṉvantōrukku antak kāppuṇarcci illāmal, ayaṉ moḻip paṟṟukku aṭimiyāṉatāl tamiḻum tamiḻiṉamum peṟṟa iḻappukaḷ ērāḷam!
ayaṉmoḻic cēṟṟiliruntu tamiḻi mīṭkum muyaṟci āṅkilēyar varukiyiyoṭṭip puttuyir peṟṟatu. vaḷḷalār, pāmpaṉ cāmikaḷ, vaṇṇac carapam taṇṭapāṇi cuvāmikaḷ, cuppiramaṇiyacivā, paritimāṟ kaliñar mutaliya palarum toṭakka vitikaḷāyiṉar.
kōvi avināciyil piṟantu virutunakaril vāḻnta viruti civañāṉayōki 19.11.1908 ām nāḷ `tiruviṭar kaḻakam toṭaṅkit taṉittamiḻ uṇarccikku vēkaṅkūṭṭiṉār. avvamippil uṟuppiṉarāyiruntavar cuvāmi vētācalam (malimaliyaṭikaḷ).
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eṉat tam peyarik koṇṭu, 1916il avar toṭaṅkiyatē taṉittamiḻ iyakkam.
`tuṟimiku āṅkilam maṟivaṭa moḻiyoṭu
 tūytamiḻp pēraṟiñar - taṉittamiḻ
 tōṟṟiya ōraṟiñar
pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār pāṭiyataṟkēṟpa, maṟimali yaṭikaḷār tamiḻ, āṅkilam, camaṟkirutam mutaliya paṉmoḻiyilum pulami mikkavar.
paṉmoḻip pēraṟiñarāṉa maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum ātaravāka niṉṟatāl etirppukkum iḻappukkum āḷākkappaṭṭār. āṉālum uṟuti kuṉṟāmal tamiḻukku ātaravāy uḻikkum uḷḷattōṭiruntār. ataṉāl, tamiḻar uḷḷaṅkaḷil iṉṟaḷavum māṟāp perumiyōṭu avar vīṟṟirukkiṉṟār.
 vāḻttāta nāḷilli viyakam
  maṟimali yaṭikaḷ maṟiyāt tiruppeyar
  vāḻttāta nāḷilli viyakam
pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ avar māṇpiṉip pāṭi makiḻntatum, tam `kuṭumpa viḷakku nūliṉi maṟimali yaṭikaḷukkuk kāṇikkiyākki vaḻaṅkiyatum, avariṉ uṟuti taḷarāta uḷḷamum uḻippum kaṇṭu pōṟṟiya naṉṟik kaṭaṉ eṉpatil ayyamilli.
ceṉṉiyil kaṭanta irupatu āṇṭukaḷākac ceyalpaṭṭu varum tamiḻmaṇ patippakap paṇikaḷit tamiḻulakam aṟiyum.
vāḻum talimuṟikkum varuṅkālat talimuṟikkum vaḻikāṭṭavalla tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ palariṉ nūlkaḷi muḻumuḻut tokuppukaḷāka vaḻaṅkiyiruppatu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ muṭippiṉi (cātaṉi)yākap pōṟṟappaṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻ maṟumalarccikku vittiṭṭa aṟiñarkaḷik kaṇṭaṟintu, avarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷi muḻumiyāṉa tokuppukaḷākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṟatu. avi kālam muḻutum payaṉpaṭavalla karuvi nūlkaḷ eṉpati aṟiñar ulakam aṟiyum!
tamatu nalatti otukki vittu, tamiḻ nalattik kākka vāḻnta aṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṉittu nūlkaḷiyum oru cērat tokuttu muḻut tokuppāka vaḻaṅkuvatu perumpaṇi! ataṉit tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭarntu niṟivēṟṟi varukiṟatu. tokuppu nūlkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalip piṉṉiṇippil kāṇka.
tamiḻukkāka uḻikkum uṇarvi iḷamiyil emmiṭam ēṟpaṭuttiyōruḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉārum avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉārum kuṟippiṭattakkōr! em naṉṟikkuriyōr!
taṉittamiḻ ārvam, tirāviṭar iyakka īṭupāṭu eṉum iru ciṟakukaḷāl emmi eḻavittavar perumpulavar nakkīraṉār! avari eṇṇik kaṇpaṉikka naṉṟi kūṟukiṟatu em neñcam.
kālappaḻamiyālum parāmarippuk kuṟivālum aḻiya nērnta tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ aḷavaṟṟavi! avaṟṟil orupakutiyi yāvatu mīṭṭu nam kikaḷukku vaḻaṅkiyōr āṟumukanāvalar, ci.vi. tāmōtaraṉār, u.vē.cāminātar mutaliya cāṉṟōr perumakkaḷ!
tamiḻilakkiyap peruñcelvatti mīṇṭum mīṇṭum accākki, aṉivar kikaḷiyum eṭṭac ceyta perumi - civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, u.vē.cā. nūlakam, maṇivācakar patippakam mutaliya patippāḷarkaḷukku uriyatu.
accuttuṟi aṟimukamāṉa piṉpē, aṟivuttuṟi aṉivarukkum potuccottākum vāyppu vantatu. eṭṭāk kaṉiyāka ōlic cuvaṭikaḷil muṭaṅkik kiṭanta ilakkiyac celvaṅkaḷ, ellōrukkum eḷitāyk kiṭikkum paṇiyic ceyta patippāciriyarkaḷum, patippāḷarkaḷum nam naṉṟik kuriyōr!
iravu pakal pārātu mutumiyik karutātu uḻikkum maṉavuratti muṉṉōṭip patippāciriyarkaḷiṉ paṇiyē emakku vaḻaṅkiyatu.
aracum, palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷum, aṟaniṟuvaṉaṅkaḷum, camaya pīṭaṅkaḷum ,perum iyakkaṅkaḷum, perum celvarkaḷum ceyya vēṇṭiya perun tamiḻppaṇi itu! eḷiyavarkaḷāl iyaṅkum tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peruntamiḻp paṇikaḷi iṉṉalkaḷukku iṭiyil arumuyaṟciyōṭu ceytu varuvatu, uḷḷavuram oṉṟiṉāltāṉ!
vallami cērkkum valimiyuṇ ṭākkum!
  vaṇṭamiḻ nintiṭil etunammik kākkum?
kākkum tamiḻik kākkum uṇarvip pāvēntar pāṭal varikaḷ tantaṉa.
accukku eṭṭāmalum kikkuk kiṭṭāmalum irunta pēraṟiñar palariṉ nūlkaḷi nāṭṭuṭimiyākki tirāviṭar iyakka aracukaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. ataṉāl, aṟivu paravalākum vāyppukaḷ uruvāyiṉa.

1965il paḷḷi māṇavaṉāka iruntapōtē moḻip pōrāṭṭattil muṉippākap paṅkēṟṟut taḷippaṭuttap peṟṟu 48 nāḷkaḷ ciṟiyil iruntavar.
piṟanta ūrākiya uṟantirāyaṉkuṭikkāṭṭil `ūrnalaṉ vaḷarccik kaḻakam eṉum camūka amippil iruntu ūr nalappaṇi āṟṟiyavar.
urattanāṭṭil `tamiḻar urimik kaḻakam eṉṉum amippiyum, pāvāṇar paṭippakattiyum naṇparkaḷuṭaṉ iṇintu niṟuvit tamiḻmoḻi, tamiḻiṉa, tamiḻaka mēmpāṭṭiṟku uḻittavar. iḷam talimuṟikkut tamiḻt toṇṭāṟṟiyavar.
pēraṟiñar aṇṇāviṉ matuvilakkuk koḷkiyi neñcil ēnti urattanāṭu matuvilakkuk kuḻuviṉ mukkiya amippāḷarkaḷil oruvarāka iruntu ceyalāṟṟiyavar. 1975-il tamiḻnāṭu caṭṭamaṉṟattil `urattanāṭu tiṭṭam eṉṟu pārāṭṭap peṟṟa matuvilakkut tiṭṭam veṟṟi peṟa uḻittavar.
tamiḻmaṇ patippakatti niṟuvi puḻakkattil illāta  paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiyum, putiya paṭippu ilakkiyaṅkaḷiyum, 19  ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilum 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkat tilum vāḻnta tamiḻccāṉṟōrkaḷ eḻuti vittuc ceṉṟa tamiḻiṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷit tēṭi eṭuttu muḻumiyākac cērttu poruḷvaḻip pirittu, kāla niralil orē vīccil toṭar toṭarāka  veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻnūl patippil taṉi muttiri patittu varupavar.

mullippāṭṭu ārāycciyuri
1962il civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam  patippitta
nūliṉi mūlamākak koṇṭu innūl patippikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
innūlip paṟṟiya kuṟippuri...
iyaṟkip poruḷkaḷik kaṇṭaṟivatilum, kaṇṭavāṟē colvatilum nappūtaṉār koṇṭuḷḷa tiṟaṉ, iṉpam payakkum vakiyil viḻuppamuṟa viḷaṅkum poruṭkōvi ākiya vaṟṟi aṭikaḷār pārāṭṭukiṟār. poruḷkaḷukku ēṟpa aṭikaḷ metuvākavum virivākavum iṭiyiṭiyē teṟṟuppaṭṭum ceṉṟu, ōciyiṉpam māṟi māṟip payakkum vakiyil amintuḷḷatip pārāṭṭum aṭikaḷār, ēṉiya oṉpatu pāṭṭukaḷip pōla mullippāṭṭu mika uyarnta tīñcuvi naṭiyiṉip peṟātu kuṟipaṭutaliyum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
mullippāṭṭil poruḷ iṭiyaṟal paṭukiṟa iṭaṅkaḷ, poruḷ poruntumiṭaṅkaḷ, poruḷ muṟṟiyum muṟṟāmal viritalaṭikiṟa iṭaṅkaḷ, poruḷ mutircciyuṭaṉ muṭitaluṟumiṭaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi `mullippāṭṭil nīḷacceṉṟu poruntum coṟṟoṭar muṭivu eṉṟa talippil aṭikaḷār tam āyvuttiṟam veḷippaṭum vakiyil viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
paṇṭikkālat tamiḻariṉ vaḻakkavoḻukka varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukaḷi ārāyntu nimittam pārttal, kāṭṭil pāṭivīṭu, makaḷirōṭu pāṭi vīṭṭil taṅkal, yāṉikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil payiṟci peṟal, ayalavarkaḷ aracarkaḷatu paḷḷiṟik kāvalarāyiruntami. kamma vēli ceytal, ēḻaṭukku māḷikikaḷ, palvaki iṉpa arum paṇṭaṅkaḷ, tamiḻaka maṉṉar nāṟpaṭi vaḷam ākiya uṇmikaḷit tam ārāyccik kuṟippil amittuḷḷār.
mullippāṭṭārāycci mutaṉ mutalil eḻunta centamiḻc ceyyuḷ uṇmiyārāycci viḷakkamākum
- nā. ceyappirakācu
maṟimali aṭikaḷāriṉ  ilakkiya paṭippukaḷ
pak. 5,6


Preface to the first edition
 “Poetry is simply the most beautiful impressive and widely effective mode of saying things and hence its importance
 – Matthew Arnold.
To a mind brooding on the silent beauty exhibited in the varied phenomena of nature, to an interest diving deep into the mysteries that lie in the inmost coners of life, to a soul soaring high into ethereal regions of religion and philosophy, to a spirit seeking serene rest in moral sanctity and Divine grace, nothing appears so permanently beautiful, so certinly impressive and effective as the study of a fine piece of poetry. Not only does a fine poem delight us by presenting a faithful picture of nature’s charms and beauties but it also impresses us with the immediate presence of a benign principle that manifests itself in all that is bright and beautiful. While it thus kindles in man the thought about a supreme being of unlimited love and kindness, it calls forth also from him love and sympathy for all animate beings which like every one of us pursue certin lines of development and fulfill certin purposes in life each in its way. It is this love, this sympathy that marks him out as superior to lower animal kingdom; it is this special quality that refine and ennobles his entire being.
Further, the soothing power of poetry is great and its influence on our mind is mild and sweet. To hearts eaten away by sorrow and worldly cares the reading of poetry comes as a healing balm; to hearts chilled by the icy hand of cruelty it appears like the warning rays of the morning Sun; and to hearts stined with the blackest crime and the basest vice it comes as the washing water of a crystal rill. Ah:! How lovely are the changes wrought by poetry in the mind of man; and how profound  and permanent are the moral effects it brings into his inward nature:!
To me the study of poetry has been a welling fountin of delight and ever will it continue to be so to the very end of my life on this Earthly plane. For years together I have been devoting my time to a close, careful and diligent study of the ancient classical poems of the Tamil language and have been drinking deep the ineffable sweetness that was there strode in. The pure simplicity of thought, the close and minute observation of nature, the vivid and sublime portrits presented of the social, moral religious and intellectual conditions of the hoary Tamilian life, the energy and artistic beauty beaming through with a sterling freshness and last, but not least the great historic value which it possesses – all combine to invest Tamil Poetry with a peculiar charm and splendour that can hardly be surpassed by the poetry of any great language in the world. These characteristics of Tamil Poetry accord to Tamil quite a unique place in the history of cultivated languages in the civilized world.
But sad to relate that the Tamil poems prescribed as text books for the B.A and F.A examination in the university of Madras/ have attracted little or no attention of the students and that a study of them has even been looked upon as useless and tiresome by some who had been led to revel in stories of religious and mythological character and who had thereby lost all seriousness and all appreciation of natures charms. This has been due partly to the teaching of the Pandits and their bulky volumes of notes which minly consist in giving word for word meanings and turgid and trivial grammatical notes and partly to some prescribed Tamil texts in prose and poetry which/ for the most part contin silly and crude religious myths translated from Sanscrit Puranas and which by no means represent the true character of original Tamil classics. 
I do not mean, however, to deny the fact that one or two classical poems are occasionally prescribed as text – books by the scrupulous members of the Tamil Board, But I only intent to say that even these brilliant grms get buried deep in the heap of filthy poems forthcoming as text – books by their side. The sparkling glitter of the farmer is swallowed up in the intense darkness of the latter;; and the study of Tamil Poetry therefore appears to our gaze as though it were surrounded by an impenetrable gloom.
In spite of this disadvantage I had had an earnest desire to direct the attention of students to a sound critical study of at least the few prescribed texts of classical poems and see what effect would be produced on them when they are tught in the light of modern poetry – criticism in general. Although I felt incompetent for the task, yet I ventured to make the attempt which as I afterwards found to my great satisfaction, proved highly successful not only among the students of my college but also among those who stand outside the pale of university learning and study Tamil in private out of pure love. The benefits that had accrued to students from a critical and comparative study of poetry became patent and ever since the time that I attempted my critical methods of teaching poetry my scheme was put into practice only orally. But an occasion presented itself to come for bringing my critical methods of teaching into writing also. While I was engaged in teaching Mullippattu to my students of the senior B.A. class, they requested me to write a critical commentary on this beautiful idyll on the new lines and subscribed in advance to defray the cost of printing. In compliance with their request I undertook the task with pleasure and finished it. I take this opportunity to express my thanks to my students of the senior B.A. class for this and many other acts of courtesy and kindness they had extended to me.
Mullippattu is an ancient classical Tamil idyll of 103 lines sung in honour of the pandian king Nedunchelian by his court bard Napputhanar. How Nedunchelian, one having gone on a expedition to meet the combined forces of seven powerful kings in the field at Talayalanganam and routed them all in the fierce battle that ensued, returns with feat splendour and magnificence and with trophies of victory to kiss his loveliest wife and how this beautiful miden that had been left alone in a country villa with a deep sorrow at the separation of her beloved husband, is lying down on her couch her chaste bosom heaving up with a sigh of consolation at his shortly expected return, are all related most vividly and very picturesquely in the poem. The poem is remarkable alike for simplicity of thought, beauty of expression and noble sentiments. The imaginative hold which it has on the mind of the reader is strong and elevating. The interest of the plot is sustined thought out but the poet with great delicacy and subtlety that characterize a master mind. The rhythm and harmony of style vary every now and then and adhere closely to the dignified march of the sense, while the diction, though rugged at times, is one the whole, chaste and elegant replete with genuine elements of Tamil. The Sanscrit words have mingled in it at the rare of two per cent and hence the date of its composition falls about the first century after Christian era.
In commenting on this excellent poem I have followed the min lines of literary criticism inculcated by the able and profound critic professor William minto;; for his critical methods are authentic and help much towards a true and clear under standing of the work taken to be scrutinized. From this critical point of view, I have given a historical account of the age that gave birth to such a splendid poem as Mullippattu and of the indluence which that age had exerted on this poem. It must be remembered that the true nature of a work of art cannot be comprehended unless the nature and conditions of the times in which it had sprung are distinctly understood. I have also aviled myself of the grand views expressed on poetry by such great masters as Milton and Ruskin and have even translated freely into Tamil one or two passage from their writings.
In my analysis of the poem I have adhered closely to the original idea conceived and expressed by the poet, rather than follow the mangled and distorted meanings given by the old commentator Nachinarkkiniar. The old commentator, discarding the simple and natural order and setting in which the subject matter or the idyll is disposed, has torn the excellently woven fabric of the poet to pieces and glues them agin together with his own vapid thought is such a fantastic manner s to make the poem seem most unnatural and its structure highly ridiculous. And in elucidating the text I have, therefore, inserted, wherever necessary, short critical notes which would make the inaptitude of the commentators meaning at one intelligible to all reflective minds. Pandit V. Swamintha Aiyar of the Government College at Kumbakonam and pandit C. Tiruchitrambalam Pilli of coimbatore having been treaty misled by the commentary of Nachinarkkiniar, have in their editions, added notes which, without following the sense of the poem, simply mar the flow and natural setting of its poetic thoughts. But of the two I wish to recommend to students Mr. Tiruchitrambalam’s edition, since his comments and critical introduction seem to me more valuable for a clear understanding of the old commentary, though not of the poem that that of Mr. Swaminatha Aiyar. In the course of my criticism I need hardly say that I have differed as widely from the old commentator as from the two Pandits, But I have fully stated my reasons and authority for doing so.
I conclude these few prefatory remarks with a fervent hope that these Tamil students who take a deep interest in the study of this poem, will find in the following critical commentary a better means of getting at a true and appreciative knowledge of this only genuine relic of a great and old Tamil poet Napputhanar and will interest themselves by further devoting a few of their leisure hours to the study of Tamil classics and extend their knowledge of Tamil wider than it is at present.
Madras Christian college 
28th September 1903					Vedachalam
nāṉkām patippiṉ mukavuri
iṟṟikku irupatteṭṭāṇṭukaḷukku muṉ yām im mullip pāṭṭārāycci yuriyi eḻutiya kālattup paḻiya caṅkat tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷākiya akanāṉūṟu, naṟṟiṇi, kuṟuntoki mutalāyiṉa acciṭṭu veḷippaṭuttappaṭavilli. ataṉāl yām payiṉṟa tolkāppiya vuriyiliruntē ivvārāycciyurikku vēṇṭiya mēṟkōṭ ceyyuṭkaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭalāyiṉēm. appōtu am mēṟkōḷ uḷḷa nūlkaḷik kuṟippiṭutal iyalātiruntatu. maṟṟu, in nāḷilō eṭṭuttoki nūlkaḷ avvaḷavum veḷivarum pēṟu kiṭittiruttaliṉ, yām ippatippiṉkaṇ ovvoru mēṟ  kōḷukkum avi vantuḷḷa nūlkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷik kuṟittirukkiṉṟēm.
atuvēyumaṉṟi, muṟpatippukaḷil illāta cila pakutikaḷum viḷakka vurik kuṟippukaḷum ippatippiṉkaṭ putiyavāyc cērkkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
iṉṉum, innūliṉ urinaṭic cuviyi muṉṉiyilum mikutip paṭuttal vēṇṭip, palappala coṟṟiruttaṅkaḷum coṟṟoṭart tiruttaṅkaḷum āṅkāṅkuc ceytirukkiṉṟēm.
iviyallāmalum, muṟpatippukkaḷil iṭiyiṭiyē viraviyirunta ciṟcila vaṭacoṟkaḷiyuṅ kaḷinteṭuttu, ivvurinaṭiyic cālavun tūya taṉit tamiḻākkiyirukkiṉṟēm.
iṅṅaṉamāka urinaṭi vakiyilum urik kuṟippu vakiyiluñ ceyyappaṭṭa cila pala māṟutalkaḷit tavirap poruḷ vakiyil ētoru māṟutalum itaṉkaṭ ceyyappaṭavilli. ākavē, muṟpatippukkaḷip payiṉṟavarkaḷ ip patippiṉkaṇ tikippuṟattakka poruḷ māṟutal ētuñ ceyyappaṭavilli yeṉpatum, innūliṉ tamiḻc cuviyiṉi mēlum mikuti ceytaṟku vēṇṭiya aḷavē cila pala cīrtiruttaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa veṉpatum aṟiñarkaḷ aṟivārkaḷāka! in nūliṉ muṟpatippiṉip payiṉṟu makiḻntavarkaḷukku ip patippu am makiḻcciyiṉi mikuti ceyyumeṉṉum nampikkiyumuṭiyēm.
pallāvaram potunilik kaḻakam	iṅṅaṉam
	tiruvaḷḷuvar yāṇṭu, 1961	maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ
mūṉṟām patippiṉ mukavuri
 muruku porunāṟu pāṇiraṇṭu mulli
peruku vaḷamaturik kāñci - maruviṉiya
kōlaneṭu nalvāṭi kōlkuṟiñci paṭṭiṉap
pāli kaṭāttoṭum pattu
eṉṉum paḻiya pāṭṭiṉkaṭ kaṇṭa tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi mutal malipaṭukaṭām īṟāṉa aruntamiḻp pāṭṭukaḷ pattiṉuḷ aintāvatāy niṟpatu mullippāṭṭākum. taṉ āruyirkkaṇavaṉip pirintu varuntiyirunta taliviyiṉ nilimiyum, pakiyaracar mēṟpōrpuriyac ceṉṟa avaḷ koḻunaṉ kārkālat tuvakkattē ap pōrviṉiyi muṭittut taṉ maṉivipāṟ ṟirumpivarum vakimiyum ic ceyyuḷiṉ kaṇ mikavum aḻakāka eṭuttuc collappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ic ceyyuḷ iyaṟṟappaṭṭuc ciṟitēṟakkuṟiya iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ākiṉṟaṉa. itaṉi iyaṟṟiya āciriyar; kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattup poṉvāṇikaṉār makaṉār nappūtaṉār eṉṟu collappaṭuvar. ip pāṭṭittavira ivvāciriyarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉavāka vēṟu eviyum ituvariyum veḷippaṭa villi.
kaṟpōruṇarvik kavarun tiṟattatāy, aḻakiya iyaṟkip poruḷ nilikaḷi iruntavāṟē eṭuttu moḻivatāy, nūṟṟu mūṉṟaṭikaḷiṟ curuṅkiya pāvāy iruppiṉun tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ nalaṅkaḷi viḷakkamākat tēṟṟuvatāy uḷḷa im mullip pāṭṭu ki.pi. 1903 ām āṇṭu kalinūṟ pulamikkup payilum māṇākkarkkup pāṭamāka vantatu. ceṉṉik kiṟittuvak kallūriyil appōtu yām tamiḻāciriyarāy iruntu māṇākkarkkut tamiḻnūl aṟivuṟutti vantamiyāl, im mullip pāṭṭiṟkum urivirittu urikkalāṉēm. icceyyuṭku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutiya uriyiyuṅ kūṭavē viḷakki varukiyiṟ, ceyyuḷiyaṟṟiya āciriyar koṇṭa poruṇmuṟi oru pakkamākavum, urikārar koṇṭa poruṇmuṟi maṟṟoru pakkamākavum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu iṇaṅkātāy māṟupaṭṭu niṟṟal kaṇṭu māṇākkar peritum iṭarppaṭuvārāyiṉar. atuvēyumaṉṟi, ākkiyōṉ amitta coṟṟoṭar neṟiyic citittuc coṟkaḷiyum aṭimoḻi
kaḷiyum oru muṟiyumiṉṟip pirittukkūṭṭip poruḷurikkum nacciṉārkkiṉiyaruri ciṟitum ēlāvuriyēyāmeṉṟum avar karutuvārāyiṉar. muṉṉarē ākkiyōṉ karuttiyoṭṭi vēṟōr uriceytu vittirunta yām ataṉiyeṭuttu avarkku viḷakkikkāṭṭiṉēmāka, atu kaṇṭu avarellān tamaturi ākkiyōṉ karutiya poruḷi nērniṉṟu viḷakkuvatoṭu, paṇṭikkālattut taṇṭamiḻc ciṟappukaḷip piṉṟik kālattukkēṟṟapaṭi teṟṟeṉa naṉku terippatāyum iruttaliṉ tamatu virinta iv vārāycciyuriyiṉiyē patippiṭṭu emakkut tantaruḷal vēṇṭum eṉak kēṭṭu atu patippiṭutaṟkāñ celavum oruṅku cērntu muṉ utaviṉar. aṅṅaṉam utaviya am māṇākkarkku yām eṉṟum naṉṟiceluttak kaṭamip paṭṭirukkiṉṟēm.
uṭaṉē yām eḻutiya mullippāṭṭārāycciyuri acciṟ patippikkappaṭṭamiyiṉ, ataṉ accup puttakaṅkaḷ avvāṇṭiṉ māṇākkarkkum piṟarkkum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. avvuri nūli vāṅkik kaṟṟa māṇākkarum, ēṉit tamiḻaṟiñarum putumuṟiyāka eḻutappaṭṭa avvurip pāṅkiṉip peritum viyantu pārāṭṭi, ammuṟiyiṉip piṉpaṟṟip paḻiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷi ārāyvatiṟ kiḷarcci peṟalāṉār. ipputu muṟiyuri tamiḻ nūlārāyum vakikaḷi iṉitu viḷakki aṟiñar palarkkum aka makiḻcciyiṉi viḷittami kaṇṭu, iṉi ittakiya urikaḷiyē eḻututal vēṇṭumeṉa ematuḷḷam peritum viḻintatu.
ki.pi. 1906 ām āṇṭu kalinūṟ pulamikkup payilum māṇākkarkkup pattuppāṭṭukkaḷil oṉṟāṉa paṭṭiṉap pāli eṉṉum aruntamiḻc ceyyuḷ pāṭamāy vantatu. yām mullippāṭṭiṟ keḻutiya ārāycciyuri yiṉippaṟṟik kēḷviyuṟṟa avvāṇṭiṉ māṇākkarkaḷun tamakkup pāṭamāy vanta paṭṭiṉappālikkum ataṉip pōlavē or ārāycciyuri eḻutittarumpaṭi vēṇṭi, ataṉip patippiṭutaṟkāñ celaviṉ poruṭṭut tāmum oruṅkucērntu poruḷutavi ceytaṉar. am māṇākkar ceyta vutavikkum yām perituṅ kaṭamippaṭṭiruk kiṉṟēm.
tirumpavuṅ ki.pi.1910 ām āṇṭu kalinūṟ pulamikkup payilum māṇākkarkkum mullippāṭṭup pāṭamāka vantatu. itaṟkiṭiyil mutaṟ patippiṭṭa ematu mullip pāṭṭārāycciyurip puttakaṅkaḷ ellāñ celavaḻintu pōṉamiyiṉ, avvāṇṭiṉ māṇākkar kaḷuñ ceyta vēṇṭu kōṭ kiṇaṅki avar tāmum oruṅkucērntu muṉṉart tanta poruḷkoṇṭu aḵtiraṇṭām muṟiyum patippiṭappaṭṭatu. iraṇṭām patippup puttakaṅkaḷuñ celavāy viṭṭamiyāl, ippōtitaṉi mīṇṭum patikkalāyiṉēm. ippatippiṉkaṭ pala tiruttaṅkaḷum pala māṟutalkaḷuñ ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ematiṭamuḷḷa ōr ēṭṭuc cuvaṭiyiyum accup puttakattiyumoppiṭṭup pārttu mullippāṭṭuc ceyyuḷiṉ kaṇṇuñ cila tiruttaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
mullip pāṭṭup payilvōr piṟanūl utavi peritum vēṇṭāmal eḷitil ataṉik kaṟṟaṟitaṟ poruṭṭu muṉṉiraṇṭu patippukkaḷilum illāta pala viḷakka urik kuṟippukaḷ ippōtitaṉkaṭ putiyavāyc cērkkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. atuvēyumaṉṟi, ulaka vaḻakkattiluḷḷa coṟkaḷit tavirttu mullippāṭṭil vanta ēṉi ellāc coṟkaḷiyum akara varicippaṭutti avaṟṟiṟkellām poruḷkaḷ eḻutiyiruk kiṉṟēm. iv varuñcoṟporuḷ variciyiṉ utavi koṇṭu icceyyuṭporuḷ uṇarntu koḷvatu evarkkum eḷitēyām. iṉi, ivvaruñ coṟkaṭkup poruḷ variyuṅkāl im mullippāṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya kālattil ataṉkaṇ vanta coṟkaṭku vaḻaṅkiya poruḷkaḷiyum, akkālatti aṭuttut tōṉṟiya cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷil avaṟṟiṟku vaḻaṅkiya poruḷkaḷiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟēm. oru  ceyyuḷ vaḻaṅkiya kālattum ataṉi yaṭuttuvanta kālattum ac ceyyuṭ coṟkaṭkup poruṭeḷivatu acceyyuḷ ākkiyōṉ karutti naṉkaṟintu kōṭaṟkuk karuviyāmātaliṉ, iṅṅaṉañ coṟ poru ṭuṇivikkum muṟiyit tamiḻārāy vōr aṉivaruṅ kippaṟṟi oḻukuvarāyiṉ namatarumic centamiḻmoḻi cālavum viḷakkam uṭiyatākit tikaḻum.
iṉi, ivvārāycciyuriyiṉkaṇ maṟṟoru mutaṉmi yāṉa cīrtiruttamuñ ceytirukkiṉṟēm. toṉṟu toṭṭa ciṟappum, ilakkaṇa ilakkiya varampun, taṉakkeṉap paṉṉūṟāyirañ coṟkaḷum vāyntu, iṉṟukāṟum vaḻakku vīḻātu uyirōṭu ulāvip paṉṉūṟāyiram makkaṭkup peritu payaṉpaṭṭu varum namatu iṉiya centamiḻ moḻiyi ayalmoḻic coṟkaḷ viravāmaṟ pātukāttut tūyatāy vaḻaṅki ataṉi vaḷampeṟac ceyvatu tamiḻarāyiṉār ovvoru varkkum iṉṟiyamiyāta kaṭamiyām. cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāyt tōṉṟi ilakkaṇa ilakkiya varampillātu tamakkeṉac cila coṟkaḷēyuṭiya moḻikaḷiyum, ulaka vaḻakkiṟkuc ciṟitum payaṉpaṭāmal iṟantupaṭṭa moḻikaḷiyum avaṟṟiṟkuriyārum avaṟṟiṟku uriyārpōṟ ṟammi eṇṇikkoḷvārum avaṟṟit tūyavāy vaḻaṅkavum avaṟṟi uyirppikkavum ōvātu muyaṉṟuvara, ellā nalaṅkaḷum oruṅkuṭiya namatarumic centamiḻ moḻiyi nammaṉōr payilātum pātukāvātuṅ kiviṭṭiruttal nirampavum iraṅkaṟpālatoṉṟām. iṉiyēṉum avar aṅṅaṉam maṭintirāmip poruṭṭu, nammaṉōriṟ kaṟṟavarāyiruppōr āriyam āṅkilam mutalāṉa piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷik kalavāmaṟ ṟaṉittamiḻiṟ pēcavum eḻutavuṅ kaṭippiṭiyāyp paḻakivaral vēṇṭum. itaṉi muṉnaṭantu kāṭṭum poruṭṭu, itaṟkumuṉ yām eḻutiya nūlkaḷiṟ pukunta ciṟcila ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷiyum annūlkaḷit tirumpap patippiṭṭuvarum ippōtu muḻutuṅ kaḷintu viṭṭu, avi niṉṟa iṭattiṟ ṟūya tamiḻc coṟkaḷiyē nirappi varukiṉṟēm. im mullippāṭṭārāycci yuriyiṉ kaṇṇum muṟpatippukaḷilirunta ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷi nīkki avaṟṟiṟkīṭāṉa centamiḻc coṟkaḷiyē ippatippiṉkaṭ peytu vittirukkiṉṟēm. eṉṟāluṅ, kālanilikkum nākarika vaḷarccikkum iciyac cila ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷiyuñ cila coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyuṅ kuṟiyīṭukaḷiyum orōviṭaṅkaḷil mikac ciṟuka eṭuttāḷutal vaḻuvaṉ ṟeṉpatūum aṟiñarkku uṭaṉpāṭāmeṉka. it taṉmiyavāṉa ārāycciyurikaḷ eḻutuvataṟku nalvaḻi kāṭṭiṉa āṅkilamoḻi nallicip pulavarkku yām eḻumiyum naṉṟi pārāṭṭuṅ kaṭami uṭiyēm.

1. pāṭṭiṉiyalpu
mullippāṭṭu eṉpatip paṟṟit teriya vēṇṭuvaṉa ellām ārāyummuṉ, pāṭṭu eṉpatu ettaṉmiyatu? eṉṟu ārāyntu aṟintu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. piṉṟik kālattut tamiḻppulavar pāṭṭeṉpatu iṉṉateṉṟē aṟiyārāyp putupputu muṟiyāṟ coṟkaḷik kōttup poruḷ āḻamiṉṟic ceyyuḷ iyaṟṟukiṉṟār. paṇṭik kālattut taṇṭamiḻp pulavarō pāṭṭu eṉpataṉ iyalpi naṉkaṟintu nalamuṭiya ceyyuṭkaḷ palap pala iyaṟṟiṉār. iṅṅaṉam muṟkālattārāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa pāṭṭiṉ iyalpoṭu māṟupaṭṭup piṟkālattār uṇmi piṟaḻntu pāṭiya ceyyuṭkaḷik kaṇṭu māṇākkar pāṭṭiṉiyalpu aṟiyātu mayaṅkuvarākaliṟ, pāṭṭu eṉpatu iṉṉateṉpataṉi oru ciṟitu viḷakkuvām.
ulakiyaṟkiyiṟ kaṇ mutalāṉa pulaṉkaḷukku viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum aḻakiyellān taṉṉakattē neruṅkap potintuvittup, piṉ avaṟṟi nam aṟiviṉiṭattē pulappaṭumvaṇṇan tōṟṟuvittup, poruḷ nikaḻcciyoṭu māṟupāṭutal illā iṉiya ōciyuṭaṉ icintu naṭipeṟum iyalpiṉi uṭiyatutāṉ pāṭṭeṉṟu aṟital vēṇṭum. iṉṉum eṅkeṅku nam aṟivit tamvayappaṭuttukiṉṟa pēraḻakum pēroḷiyum peruntaṉmiyum viḷaṅkit tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉavō aṅkellām pāṭṭu uṇṭeṉṟē aṟital vēṇṭum. itaṉi viḷakkik kāṭṭumiṭattup, pēraḻakāṟ ciṟanta or araci tāṉ mēṟpōrttirunta nīlap paṭṭu āṭiyiṉic ciṟitu ciṟitāka nīkkip, piṉ ataṉic curuṭṭik kīḻē eṟintu viṭṭut tuyil oḻintu, oḷiviḷaṅku taṉ naḷimukaṅkāṭṭi eḻuntati yoppa, iruṭkūṭṭañ curuṇṭu maṭaṅki alikaṭaliṟ ceṉṟu aṭaṅkiviṭumāṟu iḷiya ñāyiṟu urukkit tiraṭṭiya pacumpoṟ ṟiraḷipōlat taḷataḷaveṉat kīḻt ticiyil tōṉṟavum, atticiyiṉ parappellām poṉ uritta kaṟpōṟ polintu tikaḻavum, pacumi poṉmi nīlam civappu veṇmi mutalāṉa niṟavēṟupāṭuḷḷa poṉ veḷḷikaḷ uruki ōṭukiṉṟa nilam pōla vāṉ iṭamellām pala vaṇṇamāy virintu viḷaṅkavum, kariyamukilka ḷellāñ cevvarakku vaḻitta akaṉṟa tiriccīlikaḷ pōlavum veḷiyē mukilkaḷellām poṟpaṭṭut tukil pōlavum āṅkāṅkuc collutaṟ kariya pēroḷiyoṭu tikaḻavum ulakamaṅki nakittāṟpōlap putumiyuṟṟut tōṉṟum viṭiyaṟkāla aḻakellām pāṭṭeṉṟē aṟital vēṇṭum. ā! iṅṅaṉan tōṉṟum avviṭiyaṟkāla aḻakiṉik kaṇṭu viyanta vaṇṇamāy mīṉvaliyoṭu kaṭaṟkariyil niṟkuñ cempaṭavaṉik kāṭṭiṉuñ ciṟanta pulavaṉ yār?
av viṭiyaṟkāliyilē mulli nilattu mēypparkaḷ āṉkaṉṟu kaḷit toḻuvattilē tāmpiṉāṟ kaṭṭivittu āṉnirikaḷi aṭuttuḷḷa maliccāraliṟ koṇṭupōyp paciya pul mēyaviṭṭut, tām maraniḻaliṟ cāyntiruntu koṇṭu, tamakketirē paccilip pōrvi mēṟkoṇṭu kariya mukilkaḷ neṟṟi taḻuvik kiṭappap peruntaṉmiyoṭu vāṉ aḷāvit tōṉṟum maliyiṉi aṇṇāntu pārttavarāy avarkaḷ accamum makiḻcciyum aṭiyumpōtu aṅkum pāṭṭu uṇṭeṉṟē aṟital vēṇṭum.
kātaliṉāṟ kaṭṭuṇṭa iḷiñarum makaḷirum nekiḻāta kātalaṉpiṉ mikutiyāl tōḷoṭu tōḷpiṇiyat taḻuvikkoṇṭu, maliyaṭi vārattil uḷḷa pūñcōlikaḷiṟ kaḷippāy ulavuntōṟun, tūṅkaṇaṅ kuruvikaḷ marakkiḷikaḷil viyappāṉa kūṭu kaṭṭutaliyum; marap pontukaḷiliruntu maṇippuṟākkaḷ kūvutaliyum; āṇmayilkaḷ tam aḻakiya tōkiyiṉi virittup peṭimayil kaṇṭukaḷippa orupuṟam āṭutaliyum; maliyilirun toḻukum aruvinīr kūḻāṅkaṟ paṭiyiṉ mēṟ ciluciluveṉṟu ōṭivantu ac cōliyiṉ oru pakkattuḷḷa āḻnta kuṭṭattil nirampit tuḷumpa, ataṉkaṇ uḷḷa centāmari mukiḻkaḷ akaṉṟa ilikaḷiṉ mēl itaḻkaḷi virittu mikac civappāy alartaliyum virumpik kaṇṭu, naṟumaṇaṅ kamaḻum pūkkaḷi maraṅkaḷiṉiṉṟun tāvip paṟittuk kariya kūntalil māṟi māṟi aṇintuñ, civakkap paḻutta kovvikkaṉi pōṉṟa tam itaḻkaḷ aḻunta muttam vittuk koṇṭun, tēṉ oḻukiṉāleṉa iṉiya nēyamoḻikaḷ pēcik koṇṭum avarkaḷ cellumiṭattu aṅkum pāṭṭu uṇṭeṉṟē aṟital vēṇṭum.
curuṅkac colluṅkāl eṅkellām namatuṇarvik kavarkiṉṟa pēraḻaku ulaka iyaṟkiyiṟ kāṇappaṭumō aṅkellām pāṭṭu uṇṭeṉpatu teḷiyappaṭum. āyiṉum, oru nallicip pulavaṉāl iyaṟṟappaṭukiṉṟa pāṭṭuppōla atu nūliṉiṭattē kāṇap paṭuvatilliyēyeṉiṉ; naṉku viṉāyiṉāy, oru nūliṉ kaṇ eḻutappaṭṭu, ulaka iyaṟkiyiṉ aḻaki namatuḷḷattiṟ ṟōṉṟak kāṭṭi namakku uvappuṇarvu payakkuñ colliṉ tokutiyāṉa pāṭṭu nūliṉkaṇ eḻutappaṭukiṉṟa vaṭivuṭiya parupporuḷākum; ulaka iyaṟkiyiṉ aḻakōṭu oruṅkottu niṉṟu, kaṇ mutalāṉa pulaṉvaḻip pukuntu namakku uvappuṇarvu mikutiyiṉi varuvikkum pāṭṭu, vaṭivam illāta nuṇporuḷākum. iṅṅaṉamākaliṉ ulaka iyaṟkiyilellām pāṭṭu uṇṭeṉpatu tuṇipēyā meṉka.
allāmalum, uyir vāḻnāḷil ovvoru nāḷum nammuṭiya niṉivukaḷellām uṇavu tēṭutalilum, poruḷ tokuppatilum, maṉivimakkaṭku vēṇṭuvaṉa tiraṭṭik koṭuttalilum, piṟar iṭṭa ūḻiyañ ceytalilumākap palavāṟu citaṟi arumi perumiyiṉṟik koṉṉē kaḻintu pōkiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu kaḻintu pōkum makkaḷuṭiya niṉivu kaḷuñ coṟkaḷuñ ceyalkaḷum namakku iṉpantarāvākaliṉ avaṟṟi aṟiya vēṇṭumeṉa virumpuvārum ulakil yārum ilar. iṉi, ivvāṟu kaḻiyum nāṭkaḷil orōvorukāl avar aṟivu vaṟiya niṉivukaḷiṉ vēṟākap pirintu, ulaka iyaṟkiyaḻakiṟ paṭintu ataṉ vaṇṇamāyt tirintu teḷivuṟṟu viḷaṅkumpōtu, avvaṟiviṟ curantu perukum ariya periya karuttukkaḷiyē nām aṟitaṟku mikaviḻikiṉṟōm. iṅṅaṉan tōṉṟum ariya periya karuttukkaḷiṉ kōvi oḻuṅkiṉiyē pāṭṭeṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
iṉṉum, makkaḷ vāḻnāḷ eṉkiṉṟa nīrōṭiyilē varuniṉivu kaḷāṉa kalaṅkaṟ perunīr perukic cellumpōtu, ulaka iyaṟkiyeṉṉum malik kukikaḷilē arittu eṭuttuvanta aruṅkaruttukkaḷāṉa poṟṟukaḷ iṭiyiṭiyē āḻntu avvōṭiyiṉ aṭinilattiṟ citarntu miṉṉikkiṭappa, nallicippulavaṉ eṉṉum arippukkāraṉ mika viḻintu muyaṉṟu appoṟcitarkaḷiyellām oṉṟākap poṟukki eṭuttut taṉ matinuṭpa neruppiliṭṭu urukkip pacumpoṟ piṇṭamākat tiraṭṭit taruvatē pāṭṭu eṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
iṉṉum, makkaḷ aṟivu eṉkiṉṟa tittippāṉa ariya amiḻtam palavakiyāṉa kuṟṟaṅkaḷoṭuṅ kalappuṟṟut tūyataṉṟāyp pōka, nallicippulavaṉ taṉ pēraṟiviṉāl ataṉit teḷiya vaṭittu ataṉ iṉcuviyiṉi mikutip paṭutti, nāmellām ataṉip parukip periyatōr āṟutalaṭiyak koṭuppaṉ; aṅṅaṉaṅ koṭukkappaṭun tūya iṉiya aṟiviṉ viḷakkamum pāṭṭeṉṟē aṟital vēṇṭum. ikkaruttup paṟṟiyē miliṭṭaṉ eṉṉum āṅkilamoḻi valla nallicip pulavarum, pāṭṭeṉpatu makkaḷ maṉa aṟiviṉiṉṟum vaṭittu iṟakkappaṭṭa tūya amiḻtam ām1 eṉṟu uri kūṟiṉār. itu niṟka.
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam iyaṟṟappaṭukiṉṟa pāṭṭu ulaka iyaṟkiyaḻakuṭaṉ peritum porunti naṭattal vēṇṭum. iṉṉum itaṉi nuṇuki nōkkumiṭattup pāṭṭup pāṭutalil vallavaṉāṉa nallicip pulavaṉukkum ulaka iyaṟkiyiṉip palavaki vaṇṇaṅkaḷāṟ kuḻittu varintu kāṭṭukiṉṟa ōviyakkāraṉukkum oṟṟumi mika uṇṭeṉpatu teḷḷitiṟ pulappaṭum. āyiṉum, ōviyakkāraṉ varikiṉṟa ōviyaṅ kaṭpulaṉukku maṭṭumē tōṉṟuvatākum; nallicip pulavaṉ amikkiṉṟa pāṭṭō kaṇ mutalāṉa pulaṉkaḷiṉ akattē viḷaṅkum uḷḷattilē ceṉṟu tōṉṟu vatākum. ōviyakkāraṉ tāṉ eḻuta eṭuttukkoṇṭa poruṭṭōṟṟattip paṉmuṟiyum nuṇṇitāka aḷantaḷantu pārttup piṉ ataṉit tiṟampaṭa varintāl maṭṭum aṅṅaṉam varinta ōviyattik kaṇṭu viyakkiṉṟōm; tāṉ virittu viḷakkamāy eḻutavēṇṭum pakutikaḷil avaṉ oru ciṟitu vaḻuvi viṭṭāṉāyiṉum avvōviyattiṉkaṇ namakku viyapput tōṉṟā toḻiyum. nallicippulavaṉō aṅṅaṉam avaṉippōl ovvoṉṟaṉiyum virivāka viḷakkik kāṭṭa vēṇṭum varuttam uṭiyāṉ allaṉ. ōviyakkāraṉ pulaṉ aṟivip paṟṟi niṟpavaṉ; pulavaṉō maṉa aṟivip paṟṟi niṟpavaṉ.
pulaṉaṟivō parupporuḷkaḷi virittaṟiyum iyalpi luḷḷatu; maṉavaṟivō appulaṉaṟiviṉ akattē niṉṟu nuṇṇitām poruḷiyun tāṉē oru noṭiyil virittaṟiyum āṟṟal vāyntatu! ammamma! maṉavaṟiviṉ āṟṟali yām eṉṉeṉṟu eṭutturippēm! aṇuvi oru noṭiyil malipōṟ perukac ceyyum, maliyi maṟunoṭiyil or aṇuviṉuṅ kuṟukacceyyum. iṅṅaṉam viyappāṉa iyalpuṭiya maṉavaṟiviṉi nallicip pulavaṉ eṉṉum mantirakkāraṉ taṉ matinuṭpamākiya māttirik kōlāl toṭṭa aḷavāṉē atu tiṭukkeṉṟeḻuntu avaṉ virumpiya vaṇṇamellāñ cuḻaṉṟu cuḻaṉṟāṭum.
iṉṉum itaṉic ciṟitu viḷakkuvām. ōviyakkāraṉ accuṟuttum akaṉṟatoru kariya periya kāṭṭiṉi eḻutal vēṇṭumāyiṟ pala nāḷum palakālum ataṉ iyaṟkiyiṉi aṟintaṟintu pārttup, pariya maraṅkaḷ aṭarntu ōṅki oṉṟōṭoṉṟu piṇintu veḷiccam pukāmal taṭi ceytu niṟṟaliyum, akkāṭṭiṉ veḷittōṟṟa amippiṉiyum, maraṅkaḷiṉ iṭiyiluḷḷa iṭukku veḷikaḷil namatu pārvi nuḻiyuṅkāl avi tōṉṟun taṉmiyiṉiyum, uḷḷē iruḷ tiṇintu paraviyiruttaliyum aṅkuḷḷavāṟē ciṟanta pala vaṇṇaṅkaḷik kuḻittu iraṭṭut tuṇiyiṉmēl mika varunti muyaṉṟu eḻutikkāṭṭal vēṇṭum; iḵtu avaṉukkup pala nāḷ viṉiyāka muṭiyum. nallicip pulavaṉō, pariya maraṅkaḷ aṭarntōṅkip piṇintu niṟkum iruṇṭa kāṭu eṉṟu cila coṟkaḷit tiṟampaṭac cērttuk kūṟutal oṉṟiṉālēyē oru noṭippoḻutil avvōviyakkāraṉāluṅ kāṭṭa muṭiyāta oruperuviyap puṇarviṉi nam maṉattakattē viḷivikkum āṟṟa  luṭiyaṉāvaṉ. iḵtu ivaṉukku mika eḷitilē muṭivatoṉṟām. iṅṅaṉam maṉavuṇarviṉi eḻupputal mika eḷitilē ceyyak kūṭiyatoṉṟāyiṉum, ammaṉaviyalpiṉ nuṭpam uṇarntu avvāṟu ceyya vallarāṉa naṟperum pulavar ulakiṟ cilarēyāvar. pulavaṉuṭiya tiṟamiyellāñ cilvakiyeḻuttiṟ palvakip poruḷik kāṭṭukiṉṟa arumperuñ ceykiyiṉālētāṉ aṟiyappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam pāṭṭuvaḻakkiṉ nuṭpa muṇarntu piṟa moḻikaḷiṟ pukaḻpeṟṟu viḷaṅkiya nallicip pulavarkaḷ ōmar2, tāntē3, cekappiriyar4, miliṭṭaṉār5, kītē6, kāḷitācar mutaliyōrum, nañcen tamiḻil tiruvaḷḷuvar, nakkīraṉār, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, kūlavāṇikaṉ cāttaṉār, māṅkuṭimarutaṉār, kapilar, cēkkiḻār mutalāṉōrum paṇṭikkālattu ēṉi nallicip pulavarumēyāvar. iṉṉum itaṉi virippiṟ perukumeṉṟañci ittuṇiyiṉ niṟuttukiṉṟōm.



aṭikkuṟippukaḷ

2. paḻantamiḻp pāṭṭiṉ ciṟappiyalpu
iṉi, paṇṭik kālattuc centamiḻp pulavarellārum ulaka iyaṟkittiṟam piṟaḻāmal, ataṉi nuṇuki ārāyntu pāṭṭuppāṭum maṉavuṟuti mikutiyumuṭiyarāyiruntaṉar. ulaka iyaṟkiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum oḷiviḷakkattiyum eḻiliyum mika viyantaṉar. tam maṉaṉuṇarviṟku icinta vaṇṇamellām ulaka iyaṟkiyiṉit tirittuk kūṟāmal, avvulaka iyaṟkiyiṉ aḻakiṉ vaḻiyē tamataṟiviṉip porunta vittut tam niṉiviṉi virivuceytu viḷakki makiḻntaṉar. im muṟimi naṟperum pulavarkku iṉṟiyamiyāc ciṟappiṉatām eṉṉuṅ karuttup paṟṟiyē iracikar1 eṉṉum āṅkila moḻi urivalla āciriyar, kāṭṭu eṉṟa pulavarip paṟṟic collavanta viṭattu avar tamatuṇarviṉ vaḻiyē ulaka iyaṟkiyiṉi niṟuttik koḷḷāmal, avvulaka iyaṟkiyiṉ vaḻiyē tamatuṇar viṉi niṟutti niṉṟār eṉṟu pukaḻnteṭuttuk kūṟiṉār. ākavē, ulaka iyaṟkiyiṉ vaḻi niṉṟu pāṭṭup pāṭutalē arumiyāmeṉpatum, atuvē nallicip pulavarkku aṭi yāḷamām eṉpatum itaṉāl naṉku peṟappaṭum. paḻantamiḻp pulavarkaḷellārum in nuṇukkam iṉitaṟintu viḷaṅkiṉār kaḷeṉpataṟkup paḻiya tamiḻp pāṭṭukkaḷē cāṉṟākum. eṉiṉum, itaṉi oru paḻiya ceyyuḷ mukattāṉum oru ciṟitu viḷakkik kāṭṭuvām.
 muḷitayir picinta kāntaḷ melviral
kaḻuvuṟu kaliṅkaṅ kaḻāa tuṭīik
kuvaḷi yuṇkaṇ kuyppuki kamaḻat
tāṉtuḻan taṭṭa tīmpuḷip pākar
iṉiteṉak kaṇavaṉ uṇṭaliṉ
nuṇṇitiṉ makiḻntaṉṟu oṇṇutal mukaṉē.
eṉpatu kuṟuntoki2 eṉṉum paḻiya tamiḻnūlil uḷḷa oru pāṭṭu. taṉ makaḷ taṉ kātaṟkaṇavaṉ vīṭṭil eppaṭiyirukkiṉṟāḷ eṉpatik kaṇṭaṟiyum poruṭṭuc ceṉṟa cevilittāy, avviruvarum mikka nēyamuṭiyarāy vāḻvatu kaṇṭu, taṉṉuḷḷē makiḻntu colliyatāka itu pāṭappaṭṭiruk
kiṉṟatu. eṉ makaḷ vaṟṟakkāycciṉa kaṭṭit tayirip picinta kāntaḷ malarmukiḻpōṟ civanta melliya viralkaḷāl, naṉku kaḻuvi veṇmiyāṉa uyarnta āṭi camiyal ceyyum viriviṉāl iṭuppiṉiṉṟum aviḻntu kaḻala ataṉik kikaḻuvāmalē uṭuttukkoṇṭu, kuvaḷip pūppōṉṟa mi tīṭṭiya taṉ kaṇkaḷilē tāḷippuc ceyyum puki paṭṭu maṇakkavum ataṉiyum pārātu, tāṉ tuṭuppiṉāl tuḻāvi mikka aṉpoṭu camitta cuvi mikunta puḷippākiṉit taṉ kaṇavaṉ mikavum iṉitāyirukkiṉṟa teṉṟu collik koṇṭē uṇṇutalip pārttu oḷimikunta neṟṟiyiṉi yuṭiya eṉ makaḷiṉ mukam uḷḷukkuḷḷē nuṭpamāy makiḻcci aṭintatu eṉpatu tāṉ ippāṭṭiṉ poruḷ. pāruṅkaḷ! icceyyuḷiṉ iyaṟkiyaḻakum, itaṉkaṭ kāṭṭappaṭṭirukkum maṉavuṇarviṉ iyalpum evvaḷavu arumiyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa! kātaṟkaṇavaṉum maṉiviyuṅ keḻumi iruntu illaṟam nikaḻttum oḻukkam mulli eṉappaṭumākaliṉ, ivvoḻukkam naṭipeṟukiṉṟa mulli nilattiṟku ēṟpa muḷitayir picinta eṉṟār; eṉṉi? tayir pāl mutaliyaṉa āṉirimikka mulli nilattiṟkē uriyavākaliṉ. taṉ kaṇavaṉ mēluḷḷa kātal mikutiyiṉāl ēvalarum piṟaruñ camiyal ceyvataṟku oruppaṭātu tāṉē taṉ melliya civanta viralkaḷāl tayirip picitalum, kaṇavaṉ pacittiruppāṉē eṉṉum niṉivāl virintu camiyal ceyyumpōtu iṭuppiṟkaṭṭiya uyarnta āṭi kaḻalavum picinta kiyiṉik kaḻuviviṭṭu uṭuppataṟkuṭ kālam nīṇṭu appuḷippākiṉ pataṅkeṭumeṉa uṇarntu akkiyuṭaṉēyē avvuyarnta āṭiyiṉik kaṭṭikkoḷḷutalum, aṅṅaṉam picintu tiruttiya puḷippākiṉit tāḷikkumpōtu mēl eḻum puki taṉ kuvaḷippūvaṉṉa kaṇṇiṟpaṭavum appuṟan tirumpiṉāl atu pataṅkeṭumē eṉṟu mukantirumpāmal ataṉi virintu tuḻāvalum, aṅṅaṉa
mellān taṉ varuttattiṉiyum pārātu camitta cuvimikka appuḷippākiṉik kaṇavaṉ makiḻntuṇṇal kaṇṭu taṉ makiḻcci veḷiyē teriyāmal avaḷ mukam malarntu kāṭṭutalum, iyaṟkiyē taṉakkuḷḷa nāṇattāl avaḷ mukañ ciṟitu kaviḻntu niṟka avaḷatu oḷimikka neṟṟiyē am makiḻccik kuṟippup pulaṉāka muṉ viḷaṅkit tōṉṟutaluñ cila collil mika viḷaṅkakkūṟiya nuṇmi peritum viyakkaṟpālatoṉṟām. uḷḷamuvakkum mulli nilattiṟ kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum nēyamāy maruvivāḻum iyaṟki ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ ōviyam eḻutik kāṭṭiṉāṟpōl evvaḷavu uṇmiyākavum iṉitākavuñ collappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu! ipporuḷarumiyōṭu ic ceyyuḷil uḷḷa coṟkaḷ ellām nīrmaṭiyil teḷinīr moḻumoḻuveṉṟu ōṭuvatupōl ōciyiṉpam uṭiyavāy oḻukutalum, oru collāyiṉum poruḷiṉṟi vēṇṭā kūṟalākāmal muṉṉum piṉṉumuḷḷa poruṭṭoṭarpukku ēṟpa iṭiyē muḻumuḻuc coṟkaḷāy amintu niṟṟalum mikavum pārāṭṭaṟ pālaṉavākum eṉpatu.
iṉṉum paṇṭik kālattut taṇṭamiḻp pulavar ulaka iyaṟkip poruḷkaḷi āṅkāṅkut tirintu kaṇṭu peruṅ kaḷippum perukiya maṉaveḻucciyum uṭiyarāy, varuttamiṉṟi iṉitākap pāṭṭukaḷ pāṭiṉār eṉpatu avar tām virittuc collum poruḷkaḷukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭum uvamikaḷāl naṉku pulaṉām. ōriṭattil māṉ kompip paṟṟic collavantuḻi, irumpi muṟukkiṉāṟ pōluṅ kariya periya kompu eṉṉum poruḷpaṭa irumpu tirittaṉṉamāyiru maruppu (akanāṉūṟu, 4) eṉṟum, ōriṭattil iruvar nēya oṟṟumiyiṉic colla vantapōtu kattiyuṟi ceyyuñ ciṟiya toḻilāḷaṉ arakkoṭu cērtta kallip pōlap piriyōm eṉṉum poruḷpaṭac ciṟukārōṭaṉ3 payiṉoṭu cērttiya kaṟpōṟ piriyalam (akanāṉūṟu,1) eṉṟum, ōriṭattiṟ puṟanturuttiya naṇṭiṉ kaṇkaḷukku vēppampū mukiyiṉi uvamiyāka eṭuttu vēppunaṉiyaṉṉa neṭuṅkaṭkaḷvaṉ (aiṅkuṟunūṟu, 30) eṉṟum, ōriṭattil vayal nel putitu īṉṟa paciya katirukkuc celvarkaḷ tamatu kutiriyiṉ ucciyil tūkkik kaṭṭiya tiyal mūṭṭuḷḷa kavarimayiri uvamiyāka eṭuttu muracuṭiccelvar puraviccūṭṭum, mūṭṭuṟu kavari tūkkiyaṉṉa, ceḻuñcey nelliṉ cēyarip puṉiṟṟuk katir (akanāṉūṟu, 156) eṉṟum ōriṭattil maḻiyillāta vāṉam pūttatu pōla ili neruṅkiya mucuṇṭic ceṭikaḷ veḷḷiya malarkaḷip pūkka eṉṉum poruḷ pōtara maḻiyil vāṉam mīṉ aṇintaṉṉa, kuḻiyamal mucuṇṭi vāliya malara (akanāṉūṟu, 264) eṉṟum, ōriṭattil pañciṉ ṟoṭar nuṉipōlun taliyiṉiyuṭiya putarkaḷiṉ mēl ēṟippaṭarum iṇṭik koṭikaḷiṉ nīril naṉinta taḷirkaḷ neyyil tōyttaṉapōla viḷaṅki iraṇṭāka iruḷik kūṟupaṭuttiṉāṟpōl ovvoru taḷirum iraṇṭu kūṟupaṭṭaṉavāyk kariyaniṟattuṭaṉ aciya eṉṉum poruḷpaṭat tuyttalippūviṉ putalivar īṅki, neytōyttaṉṉa nīrnaṉi antaḷir, iruvakiri iruḷiṉ īriya tuyalvara (akanāṉūṟu, 294) eṉṟum, ōriṭattiṟ pacci mañcaḷiṉ paciya mutukip pōl cuṟṟilum poruttuṭampu uṭiyaṉavāyk kaḻiyiṟ kiṭakkum iṟā mīṉ eṉpatu viḷaṅka muṟṟā mañcaṭ pacumpuṟaṅ kaṭuppac, cuṟṟiya piṇara cūḻkaḻi yiṟavu (naṟṟiṇi, 101) eṉṟum, ōriṭattil mayiliṉ aṭipōl mūṉṟu piḷavāy irukkum ili kaḷiyuṭiya periya katiruḷḷa nocci eṉpatu tōṉṟa mayilaṭi iliyamākkural nocci eṉṟum, ōriṭattiṟ katir arintuviṭṭa tiṉippayiriṉ tāḷpōṉṟa ciṟiya pacuṅkāli yuṭiyavāy ōṭumnīril āralmīṉip pārttuk koṇṭirukkum nāri eṉpatu pulappaṭat tiṉittāḷ aṉṉa ciṟupacuṅ kāla, oḻukunīr āral pārkkuṅ kuruku (kuṟuntoki, 25) eṉṟum pōnta taṉmi naviṟciyaṇic coṟṟoṭarkaḷāṉē paḻiya tamiḻp pulavariṉ viḻumiya ulakiyaṟ poruḷ aṟiviṉi iṉitu aṟintu koḷḷalām. iṉṉum ivipōṉṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ nūṟunūṟākap perukkalāmēṉum, iṅku ataṟku iṭam
peṟutal kūṭāmiyiṉ itaṉi ivvaḷavil niṟuttukiṉṟōm.
ittuṇi nuṭpamāṉa ulakiyaṟ poruḷ aṟivu paṇṭik
kālattut taṇṭamiḻp pulavariṭaṅ kāṇappaṭutal pōla, maṟṟimoḻikaḷil vallarāy viḷaṅkiya ēṉip paḻampulavariṭattuṅ kāṇappaṭutal aritu. iṉṉum ivvāṟē paḻiya tamiḻppulavar ulaka iyaṟporuṭkāṭcikaḷip puṉinturitta muṟiyum, avvulaka iyaṟkikkum makkaḷiyaṟkikkum uḷḷa poruttam paṟṟi avar veḷiyiṭṭa ariya karuttukkaḷiṉ viḻuppamum pattuppāṭṭu, eṭṭut toki, patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷiṟ parakkak kāṇalām. āṇṭuk kaṇṭu koḷka.
ti.mu. nāṉūṟu āṇṭu mutal ti.pi. nūṟāṇṭu variyil toṭarpuṟṟu viḷaṅkiya centamiḻ ilakkiya kālattil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa nūlkaḷiṉ iyaṟkiyum, annūlkaḷukkum mullippāṭṭiṟkum uḷḷa iyipum, akkāla varalāṟum.
iṉi, tiruvaḷḷuvar piṟappataṟku muṟceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭu kaḷilē mikavum pukaḻpeṟṟu viḷaṅkiya pulavar kālamum, avar piṟanta piṉnūṟṟāṇṭilē avvāṟu viḷaṅkiya pulavar kālamuñ centamiḻmoḻi mika uyarnta niliyilē iruntu tikaḻnta kālamākumeṉṟu aṟital vēṇṭum. tiruvaḷḷuvar piṟappataṟkumuṉ oru nāṉūṟu āṇṭum, avar piṟantapiṉ oru nūṟu āṇṭum cērntu muṭinta ōr ainnūṟāṇṭun tamiḻmoḻi maṟuvaṟṟa matipōṟ kalinirampi viḷaṅkiya kālamākum. ikkālattilē ciṟanta pulavar palartōṉṟip palavakiyāṉa ariya periya centamiḻ nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟiṉār. ip pulavarkaḷip pōṟṟit tamiḻi vaḷampaṭuttaṟku āvalmikka aracar palarum vaḷḷal palarum āṅkāṅku mikkiruntaṉar. tamiḻ aracarkaḷ palar kalvi vaḷattāluñ celvavaḷattālum mēmpaṭṭum, pōr vallamiyilum perumiyaṭintu, tamiḻmoḻiyiṉip palaviṭaṅkaḷilum perukac ceyvatiṟ karuttūṉṟiṉarāy iruntār.
ikkālattilētāṉ, taṉakku oppum uyarvum iṉṟiviḷaṅ kāniṟkun tirukkuṟaḷ eṉṉum arumperunūl eḻutappaṭṭatu; cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali mutalāṉa ciṟanta tamiḻk kāppiyaṅkaḷum, paḻamoḻi, nāṉmaṇikkaṭiki mutalāṉa aṟanūlkaḷun tōṟṟamuṟṟu eḻuntaṉa ivvinnūṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉumpiṉṉumirunta tamiḻppulavarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭuc citaṟikkiṭanta aruntamiḻp pāṭṭukkaḷellām oruṅku tokukkap paṭṭu, akanāṉūṟu, puṟanāṉūṟu, kalittoki mutaliya vakivakit toki nūlkaḷāka ikkālattilētāṉ oḻuṅku paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. paṇṭik kālattilē ceyyappaṭṭa tolkāppiyam eṉṉum ariyaperiya  tamiḻilakkaṇattil mikac ciṟanta pakutiyāṉa akapporuḷiṉ viriviyellāñ curukki ataṉi vaṭitta piḻivāka iyaṟṟappaṭṭa iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷ eṉṉum maṉaviyaṟkinūl paṉṉeṭuṅ kālamāka maṟintukiṭantu piṉṉar ikkālattilētāṉ veḷivantu lāvalāyiṟṟu.
iṉṉum ikkālattilē iṉṟiyamiyātu aṟiyaṟpālatāñ ciṟappiyalpu oṉṟuṇṭu. itaṟku muṉṉellān tamiḻ perumpāluñ ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilēyē perukivantatu; maṟṟu ikkālattilō ataṉōṭu uri vaḻakkum viravip paravat toṭaṅkiṟṟu; col viḻuppamum poruḷ viḻuppamum potinta mika iṉiyatōr uri mika nuṇukkamāṉa aṟiviṉiyuṭiya nakkīrar eṉṉum nallicip pulavarāl iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷ eṉṉum nūlukku varintu tarappaṭṭatu. ivvuri cūttirapporuḷi iṉitu viḷakkum poruṭṭē eḻutappaṭṭatāyiṉum, maṟṟi urikaḷpōṟ curuṅkātu mika virintu iṉṟiyamiyātu uṇaraṟpālaṉavām. arum peruntamiḻ nuṭpaṅkaḷellām oruṅku nirampip polikiṉṟatu. ākavē, ikkālattil mikac ciṟanta uriyāciriyarāyt tōṉṟit, tamiḻ moḻiyiṟ pala vakiyāṉa nalla cīrtiruttaṅkaḷellāñ ceytu, tamiḻp payiṟciyip perukac ceytuvanta naṟperum pulavar āciriyar nakkīraṉār tāmeṉṟu aṟiyaṟpāṟṟu. itaṟkup piṟkālattē vaṭamoḻik kalappāṟputitu tōṉṟiya viruttappā eṉpatu, ivvinnūṟāṇṭukaḷum virintu peruki vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷil eṭṭuṇiyuṅ kāṇappaṭāmi peritum niṉivukūraṟpāṟṟu.
puttacamayat tōṟṟamum perukkamum
iṉi, iṅṅaṉan tamiḻ perukkamuṟṟu viḷaṅkutaṟku oru perun tuṇikkāraṇamāy iruntatu yātu? eṉṟu ārāyap pukuvārkkup putta camayam āṅkāṅku viḷakka muṟṟup paravi vantamiyēyā meṉpatu pulappaṭum.
paṇṭik kālattē āciyāk kaṇṭattiṉ vaṭaticip pakkaṅkaḷil irunta āriyar, kuḷirnaṉi mikunta avviṭaṅ kaḷi viṭṭu, intiya nāṭṭiṟ kuṭipukutaṟkumuṉ, iv vintiyanāṭu muḻutum paraviyirunta makkaḷ tamiḻarē yāvar.4 tamiḻar irunta ivvintiya nāṭu perumpālum veppam mikunta nāṭātalāl itilirunta avarellāṅ kuḷir mikunta āciyāk kaṇṭattiṉ vaṭakkēyuḷḷa āriya makkaḷip pōl attuṇi uṭal valimi yuṭiyarāka iruntilar. uṭampil uraṅkuṉṟiyirun tamiyāl tamiḻar tamakkuḷḷē kalām viḷittu oṟṟumi kulitaṟku iṭampeṟutaliṉṟip perumpālum orumippāṭu uṭiyarāy nāṭ kaḻittaṉar. uṭalvaliviṉ kuṟivāl avar maṉa aṭakkam peṟṟu etaṉiyum āḻntaṟiyum iyalpuṭiya rāyiruntaṉar. ulaka iyaṟki yiluḷḷa aḻakiṉik kaṇṭu viyantu avvaḷavil amintu viṭāmal, avviyaṟkiyiṉ uḷḷē nuḻintu aṅkellām, piṟaḻāta ōr orumippāṭum ataṉi aṅkē nilipeṟutti maṟintu kiṭakkum ōr uyirp poruḷiṉiruppuṅ kaṇṭaṟintu kaḷippaṭintaṉar. aṅṅaṉam ivvulaka iyaṟkiyil maṟintu ūṭuruvik kiṭakkum avvuyirpporuḷiṉiyē kaṭavuḷ eṉṟu tuṇintu, ataṉi maṉattāl niṉintu vāyāl vāḻtti meyyāl vaṇaṅki vaḻipaṭṭu vāḻntaṉar. avar tamatu uṭalvaliviṉ kuṟi pāṭṭāṟ palappala vakiyāṉa caṭaṅkukaḷ iyaṟṟi vaḻipaṭutaṟku oruppaṭārāyt taṉiyē ōr iṭattil maṉa amitiyōṭu iruntu akkaṭavuṭporuḷi maṉattāṟ palakāl uṟittu niṉintu, ataṉāl aṟivāḻa muṭiyarāyt tulaṅku vārāyiṉar.
ivar ivvāṟu iruppa, āciyāviṉ vaṭapakutikaḷil irunta āriyarō kuḷirāl uṭampu iṟuki mikka valivuñ curucuruppum uṭiyarāyiruntaṉar; uṭampu valivu mikutiyum uṭimiyāluṅ, kuḷirum paṉiyum mikunta avvaṭapakutikaḷil uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷ vēṇṭum aḷavu kiṭittilāmiyālum avarkaḷ oriṭattil amitiyāy irukkappeṟāmal, tokuti tokutiyākap pala ticikaḷiṟ pirintupōy aṅkaṅkuḷḷā roṭu pōr purintum allātavarkkuk kīḻaṭaṅkiyum āṅkāṅkuk kuṭiyēṟi vāḻntuvaralāyiṉar. avar maṟṟiyōruṭaṉ pōr iyaṟṟappōṉa kālaṅkaḷilellān tāmē veṟṟi peṟum poruṭṭu ataṉip peṟuvikkum uyirttuṇiyi nāṭat talippaṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl, tam muṉṉōril iṟantupaṭṭa varkaḷāṉa intiraṉ varuṇaṉ mittiraṉ mutaliyavariṉ āvikaḷiyē teyvaṅkaḷeṉat tuṇintu vaḻipaṭalāṉār; vaḻipaṭum kālaṅkaḷilellān tām uṇavāka ayiṉṟuvanta palavakiyāṉa vilaṅkukaḷik koṉṟu, avaṟṟiṉ iṟiccikaḷit tēvarkku ūṇeṉa ūṭṭi vēḷviceytum, vēḷvic caṭaṅkukaḷip palavēṟu vakiyavāyp perukki iyaṟṟiyum vantaṉar.
(irukku vētattiṉ mutaṉ maṇṭilattil uḷḷa, 33)
	intiraṉē, ellāmvallavaṉē, mikuntaporuṭ kaḷañciyaṅkaḷi oruṅku cērttukkoṇṭu emmuṭaṉ vāṇikañ ceyyātē!	(3)
	celvattiṉ mikka tayuvi nī taṉiyākavē niṉ kulicap paṭiyāṟ koṉṟu, intiraṉē, nī niṉ tuṇivaruṭaṉ ēkukiṉṟāy!
	toṉṟutoṭṭē caṭaṅkukaḷ ceyyārāṉa avarkaḷ, vāṉveḷikkuc cēyarāyp, palamukamāyt tappiyōṭi aḻintaṉar.	(4)
(51)
	āriyariyun tayukkaḷiyum naṉṟāy vēṟupirittaṟintu koḷka! caṭaṅkukaḷ iyaṟṟāta avarkaḷit taruppippuṟ parappuvōṉpāl oppuvittiṭuka!	(8)
(53)
	irijivāṉ kīḻppaṭiyāṉāy avarkaḷi muṟṟuki ceyta annāḷil, vaṅkiritaṉuṭiya nūṟu kōṭṭi kaḷiyum nī aḻittaṉiyaṉṟō!	(8)
	tuṇivarillāta cucuravarkaḷuṭaṉ pōrpuriyum poruṭṭu, aṟupatiṉā yirattut toṇṇuṟṟoṉpatu kālāṭkaḷuṭaṉ paṭiyeṭuttu vanta makkaḷuḷ aracarāṉa irupatiṉmariyum, ō intiraṉē, paranta pukaḻuṭiyāy, nī ellāvaṟṟiyum mēṟkaṭanta tēr uruḷikaḷāl aḻittuḷiyaṉṟō!					(9)
(103)
	intiraṉē, tayuvitterintu avaṉmēl niṉkaṇiyi ēvuka! āriyaṉuṭiya āṟṟaliyuñ ciṟappiyum mikutip paṭuttuka!	(3)
(163)
	teyvatti nōkki niṉitta maṉattiṉatāy valiya kutiri veṭṭappaṭutaṟku muṉvantu niṟkiṉṟatu.
	ataṟku uṟaviṉatāka veḷḷāṭum ataṟkumuṉ ōṭṭap paṭṭu vantirukkiṉṟatu; iruṭiyarum pāṭakarum ataṉpiṉ  varukiṉṟaṉar.						(12)
	akkutiri mikacciṟanta koṭṭilukku vantirukkiṉṟatu, taṉ tāy taṉ tantiyiṉpāl vantirukkiṉṟatu.
	naṉku varavēṟkappaṭṭu iṉṟu atu tēvarkaḷpāṟ cellum; ataṉip paliyākak koṭuppavaṉukku atu pala naṉkoṭi yiṉit tarum	(13)
eṉṉum ivipōṉṟa pāṭṭukkaḷāl avar avviyalpu uṭiyarātal tuṇiyappaṭum. ivviyalpuḷḷa āriyar intiyāviṉuṭ pukuntapōtu aṅkē tamakku muṉṉirunta tamiḻariṟ ciṟitu karuntōṟṟam uṭiyarāy iruntavar tamakkellān tayukkaḷ, tācarkaḷ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ iṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvārāyiṉar. kirēkkar maṟṟi nāṭṭavarip pārpēriyar eṉṟum, tamiḻar ēṉiyōri milēccar eṉṟum aḻittal pōla āriyarun tamiḻariṟ cilari avvāṟu peyariṭṭaḻittaṉar. tamiḻar muṉṉāḷil āriyariyellām milēccareṉṟu aḻittami milēccar āriyar eṉṉun tivākara piṅkalantic cūttirattāl naṉkaṟiyappaṭum. uṭal valimi mikavum uṭiya āriyar intiyāviṉuṭ pukutalun tamiḻariṟ cilar avaroṭu pōr purintu tōlviyaṭintaṉar; cilar amitiyiṉ poruṭṭu malikaḷiluṅ kāṭukaḷilum pōy iruntaṉar. cilar kaṭum pōr malintu āriyari veṉṟaṉar; cilar tāntām irunta iṭamviṭṭup peyarāmal āriyari viruntāka ēṟṟu avaruṭaṉ uṟavāṭi avar vaḻakka oḻukkaṅkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟit tān taḻuviyun tam vaḻakka  oḻukkaṅkaḷi avar taḻuvumāṟu ceyvittum avarōṭu orumiyuṟṟu vāḻat toṭaṅkiṉar. ivviruvaki iṉattārum oruvarōṭu oruvar maruvi vāḻum nāṭkaḷil avaravar tattamakkē uriya vaḻakkavoḻukkaṅkaḷi muḻuvatun tirittup piṟaḻtti viṭāmal, avitammiṟ perumpāṉmiya vaṟṟi muṉṉiruntapaṭiyē vittukkoṇṭu kaṭippiṭittu oḻukiṉar.
ikkālattil āriyaruṭ kurukkaḷmār palar tōṉṟip palavakiyāṉa vēḷvikaḷ ceytal vēṇṭum eṉṟu vaṟpuṟutti avaṟṟit tamiḻa aracar utaviyāṟ ceytu vantaṉar; appōtutāṉ av vēḷvic caṭaṅkukaḷ ceyya vēṇṭum muṟikaḷi mika viritteḻutip pirāmaṇaṅkaḷ iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. elli illāta ēḻi vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷik koli ceytu iyaṟṟappaṭum vēḷvikaḷum vēḷvic caṭaṅkukaḷum āriyak kurukkaḷmārkaḷiṉ piṟaḻcci aṟivāl eṅkum mikunta varavē, uyirkkolikku iyaṟkiyilē uṭampaṭāta tamiḻaril aṟivāṉmikka cāṉṟōrkaḷ ‘iṅṅaṉan tītaṟṟa uyirkaḷiṉ uṭampic citittu vēḷvikaḷ ceytalāṟ pōtarum payaṉ eṉṉi? eṉṟu tam āriya naṇparuṭaṉ nayamāyk kalantu vaḻakkiṭṭu avariṟ cilarit tamvaḻippaṭuttik koṇṭaṉar.
iṅṅaṉat tamiḻariṉ aṟivāḻa ārāycciyiṉi āriya naṉmakkaḷ cilar tāmun taḻuvi oḻukap pukunta kālattilētāṉ upaniṭataṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. ivvupaniṭataṅkaḷ āriyarkku eṭṭātirunta aṟaṅkaḷi aṟivuṟutti, avar ceytupōnta uyirkkoliyiṉi niṟuttutaṟ poruṭṭākat tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉavāmeṉpataṟku av vupaniṭantaṅ kaḷilēyē maṟukkappaṭāta cāṉṟukaḷ pala virukkiṉṟaṉa5 iṅṅaṉam uṭaniṭataṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭa piṉṉarum vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷip paliyiṭṭuc ceyyum vēḷvikaḷ ciṟituṅ kuṟipaṭāmal āriyarkkuḷ mikuntu vantamiyāṉum, āriyak kurukkaḷmār taṅkoḷkikku iṇaṅkāta  tamiḻariyum atu ceyyumpaṭi valintu varuttiṉamiyāṉum āriyarkkun tamiḻarkkum itaṉporuṭṭu vaḻakkukaḷum etirvaḻakkukaḷum nēra, avvamayattil vaṭa nāṭṭilirunta tamiḻa aracakulattiṟ kutamacākkiyar eṉpār tōṉṟip paḻiya tamiḻmakkaḷ ārāyntu vanta ariya nalloḻukka muṟikaḷi eṭuttu virittuc collap pukuntār. kallāta makkaḷ maṉamuṅ karintu urukumpaṭi mikka irakkattuṭaṉ nalloḻukkaṅkaḷiṉ viḻuppatti eṭuttu virittu, in nalloḻukkaṅkaḷi oruvaṉ vaḻuvāmaṟ ṟaḻuvi naṭappaṉāyiṉ avaṉukku ellāt tuṉpaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum viṭupaṭum niruvāṇam eṉṉun tūyanili tāṉē varumeṉṟum, aṟivillāta ēḻi uyirkaḷi āyiramāyiramākak koṉṟu vēḷvi vēṭṭalāl mēlumēlun tīviṉiyē viḷiyumallatu nalviṉi eytāteṉṟuṅ kutamar aruḷkaṉintu aṟivuṟuppārāyiṉar. makkaḷ uḷḷatti eḷitilē kavarntu urukkumpaṭiyāṉa kutamar koḷkikaḷ cila nāḷilē eṅkum paravalāyiṉa. makkaḷellārum āriyak kurukkaḷmār coṟkaḷil aiyuṟavu koṇṭu tam aṟivāl nallaṉa palavum ārāyappukuntaṉar. eṅkum  avaravar tattaṅkaruttukkaḷiṟ ṟōṉṟum nuṭpaṅkaḷit tārāḷamāy veḷiyiṭat tuṇintaṉar. pirāñcu tēyattiṟ6 ṟōṉṟiyati yotta oru periya māṟutal eṅkum uṇṭāva tāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam oru periya māṟutal intiya nāṭu muḻuvatuñ cuḻaṉṟuvarumpōtu, teṉṉāṭṭiluḷḷa tamiḻarun tām tamatuḷḷattē ārāyntu vitta ariya periya nuṇporuḷkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭut tamatu paṇṭit tamiḻmoḻiyiṉip paṇṭināḷiṟ pōlavē peritum vaḷampaṭuttum ariyamuyaṟciyil tali niṉṟār. iṅṅaṉan tiruvaḷḷuvar piṟappataṟkumuṉ oru nāṉūṟu āṇṭum piṉ oru nūṟāṇṭum mika virintu perukiya camaya viḷakkamē akkālattiṟ ṟamiḻmoḻiyiṉkaṇ arumperuñ centamiḻ nūlkaḷ pala tōṉṟutaṟku oru peruṅkāraṇamāyiṟṟu eṉṟu teḷivuṟa aṟital vēṇṭum. āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār iyaṟṟiya ariyaperiya tirukkuṟaḷ eṉṉum nūliṟ kollāmi pulāluṇṇāmi, oḻukkamuṭimi eṉṉun tamiḻark kuriya aṟivāḻa nuṭpap poruḷkaḷ palakālum palaviṭattum eṭuttu vaṟpuṟuttappaṭutal kāṇka.
iṉi, ip poruḷkaḷellām putta camaya nūlkaḷi
lirun teṭuttuc collappaṭṭaṉa eṉpārun, tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār puttarē eṉpārum uḷar. iyaṟkiyilē tamiḻarkkuriya oḻukkaṅkaḷiṉ viḻuppattiyē kutamar eṉṉun tamiḻpperiyār viḷakka vantamiyāl avvoḻukka varicikaḷ avar coṉṉapiṉ eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu poruntātu. tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār mutaliya cāṉṟōr, tamakkun tammiṉattārkkum iyaṟkiyilē tōṉṟiya arumperuṅ karuttuk kaḷiyē putta camayam yāṇṭum virintu paranta kālattil taṭiyiṉṟic collutaṟku iṭampeṟṟārākaliṉ, akkaruttukaḷ tiruvaḷḷuvaṉārkkuṅ kutamacākkiyarkkum potuvāvaṉavēyām eṉṟu tuṇika.
 avicorintu āyiram vēṭṭaliṉ oṉṟaṉ
uyircekuttu uṇṇāmi naṉṟu	(kuṟaḷ. 259)
eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷil āciriyar āriyamakkaḷ ceytu pōnta vēḷvi viṉiyi maṟuttuk kollāmiyiṉ ciṟappi valiyuṟuttik kūṟiyatuṅ kāṇka. iṉṉum ivvāṟē āciriyar āṅkāṅku āriyamakkaḷ ceytu pōnta maṟṟi viṉiccaṭaṅkukaḷiyum maṟuttuk kūṟutal kaṇṭu koḷka. īṇṭu aviyellām eṭutturippiṟ perukum.
iṉi, ivvāṟu oru kālattil naṭipeṟum oḻukkaṅkaḷukkum ak kālattiṟ ṟōṉṟum nūlkaḷukkum periyatōr iyipu uṇṭeṉpataṉi viḷakkutaṟ poruṭṭē ivvōr ainnūṟṟāṇṭiṉkaṇ naṭipeṟṟa nikaḻcciyiṉi oru ciṟitu virittuk kūṟiṉēm. oru nūliṉ iyalpi uḷḷavāṟu uṇartaṟku annūl eḻutappaṭṭa kālattiṉ iyaṟki iṉṟiyamiyātu aṟiyaṟpālatākum. itupaṟṟiyē āṅkilamoḻiyil nuṭpavārāyccikaḷ pala eḻutiya uviliyam miṇṭō7 eṉṉum ārāycci urikārar, kālappōkku eṉpatu iṉṉateṉṟu toṭṭu aṟiyappaṭāta ōr iyaṟki vāyntatu; aḵtu akkālattu makkaḷ iyaṟṟum nūlkaḷiluṅ, kottu vēlikaḷilum, uṭikaḷilum, avarkaḷ naṭāttum vāṇika vāḻkkiyilum, avarkaḷ amikkun toḻiṟ kaḷaṅkaḷilum ellān taṉ aṭiyāḷattip patiya iṭukiṉṟatu. ***oru pulavaṉum oru kāla iyaṟkiyiṉ vaḻiniṉṟē nūl eḻutupavaṉāvaṉ; akkāla iyaṟki allatu am makkaḷ oppuravu avaṉ eḻutuvaṉavaṟṟi ellān taṉ uruvākki avaṟṟiṟkut taṉ niṟatti ūṭṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉi nam kaṇṭaṟivataṟku ak kālattiṉ potu iyaṟkiyum, ataṉkaṇ avaṉ kuṟippiṭṭa makkaḷ naṭiyiṉiyaṟkiyum, avaṉ irukkum iṭattiṉ iyaṟkiyum iṉṟiyami yātu ārāyaṟpālaṉa vākum8 eṉṟu mika nuṭpamāka eṭuttu moḻintiṭṭār. atu kiṭakka.
iṉi, ivvōr ainnūṟāṇṭiṟ ṟōṉṟiya nūlkaḷellām perumpālum akkāla iyaṟki taṅkaṇ etirtōṉṟi viḷaṅkap peṟum oru taṉmiyuṭiyavākumeṉṟu terital vēṇṭum. aṟivu āḻamiṉṟi āriya makkaḷ ceytupōnta vīṇāṉa veṟuñcaṭaṅkukaḷiṟ kaṭṭuppaṭāmal taṉiyē pirintuniṉṟa tamiḻ makkaḷ, tam paṇṭiyāciriyarkaḷ ceṉṟa muṟiyē ulaka iyaṟkiyiṉiyum, makkaḷiyaṟkiyiṉiyum uḷḷuruvi nuḻintu ārāyntu tāṅkaṇṭa ariya poruḷ nuṭpaṅkaḷi amittu nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟiṉār. ākavē, ulaka iyaṟkiyiṉiyum, makkaḷ iyaṟkiyiṉiyum ārāyum ārāycci ikkālattut tōṉṟiya nūlkaṭkellām potutn taṉmiyāku meṉṟuṇarntu koḷka.
iṉi, ivvulaka iyaṟkiyiṉi ārāyum nūlkaḷ ellām puṟapporuḷn eṉavum, makkaḷiyaṟkiyiṉi ārāyvaṉavellām akapporuḷ eṉavun tollāciriyarāl vakukkap paṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ akapporuḷ eṉpatu āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupālāriyuñ ceṟiyap poruttuvatāy, maṟṟellā uṇarvukaḷiyum niṉivukaḷiyun taṉakkuk kīḻāka niṟuttit tāṉ avaṟṟiṉmēl amarntu taṉakku nikariṉṟip perumiyuṭaṉ tōṉṟuvatāy, iṉpamun tuṉpamumellān tōṉṟu taṟkut tāṉ nilikkaḷaṉāy, ellā ulakaṅkaḷum ellāp poruḷkaḷun taṉṉic cuṟṟic cuḻaṉṟu cellat tāṉ avaṟṟiṉ iṭiyē ciṟitun tiripiṉṟi nilipeṟṟu viḷaṅkuvatāy uḷḷa aṉpu allatu kātal9 eṉpataṉi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu makkaḷiyaṟki muḻuvatūum oruṅkē ārāyvatākum. iṉip, puṟapporuḷ eṉpatu makkaḷ ulaka iyaṟkiyuṭaṉ poruntit tamakku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavāṉa palvaki muyaṟcikaḷiyum muṟṟup peṟuvittaṟ poruṭṭuc ceyyun toḻil vēṟupāṭukaḷum piṟavun teḷḷitiṉ ārāyvatām.
iṉip potuvāka ellā māntarkkum uriya iyaṟki yiṉip pakutturippatākaliṉ, akapporuḷ oḻukkam payiṉṟuvarum pāṭṭukkaḷiṟ ciṟappāka or āṇmakaṉiyum oru peṇ makaḷiyum eṭuttu vittu, aṉṉavar tamakkuriya peyarcolli avi tām eḻutap paṭutal illi. ellā makkaṭkum potuvāy varukiṉṟa aṉpiṉāl nikaḻum aintiṇi oḻukkatti oruvar iruvarkku variyaṟuttuk kūṟutal, avvaṉpiṉ aintiṇiyoḻukkam ēṉiyōrkku illiyām pōlum eṉa malivu tōṟṟuvittu vaḻuvāy muṭintiṭumākaliṉ, appāṭṭukkaḷ ellāṅ kuṟittu oruvar peyar collāmalē variyappaṭum eṉpatu teḷintu koḷka. itu kaṭippiṭiyāy uṇarttutaṟkē āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār,
 makkaḷ nutaliya akaṉin tiṇiyuñ
cuṭṭi oruvar peyarkoḷap  peṟā ar?10
eṉṟu kiḷantu kūṟiṉār.
iṉi, itupōṟ potuvākavaṉṟi, makkaḷuḷ ovvoruvarun tattam muyaṟci vēṟupāṭukaḷukku ēṟpap palvakippaṭṭa uṇarvum palvakippaṭṭa ceyalum uṭiyarāy ulakanaṭiyaṟintu oḻukuvarākaliṉ, iṅṅaṉamāṉa avartam puṟapporuḷ oḻukkam payiṉṟuvarum pāṭṭukkaḷil avvavarkkē uriya peyar paṇpu ceyal mutaliyaṉa ellāṅ kiḷanteṭuttuk kūṟi maṟṟu avi eḻutappaṭum eṉka. oruvar paṇpuñ ceyalum ēṉiyoruvar paṇpuñ ceyalum pōlaṉṟi ulakanaṭiyiṟ perumpāṉmiyum vēṟupaṭṭu veḷiyē tōṉṟik kiṭattalāl, aṅṅaṉam veḷippaṭṭut tōṉṟum paṇpu ceyalkaḷik kūṟum puṟattiṇip pāṭṭukkaḷil avvappaṇpu ceyalkaṭku uriyār peyar kūṟal vēṇṭuvatu iṉṟiyamiyātatēyām eṉka. inneṟi aṟivuṟuttutaṟ poruṭṭē āciriyar tolkāppi yaṉār,
 puṟattiṇi maruṅkiṟ porunti ṉallatu
akattiṇi maruṅkiṉ aḷavuta lilavē11
 eṉṟu kūṟiṉār
iṉi, akam puṟam eṉṉum ivviruvaki oḻukkamuṅkalantu varum pāṭṭukkaḷil akavoḻukkamē perumpālum muṉṉum piṉṉun toṭarpuṟṟuccella, ataṉ iṭiyē oru puṟavoḻukkañ ciṟukivaru māyiṉ, avaṟṟuḷḷum oruvar peyar kuṟittuc collappaṭuvatilli. avvāṟaṉṟi, avaṟṟuḷ muṉṉum piṉṉum oru puṟavoḻukkamē toṭarpuṟṟuc cella iṭiyē or akavoḻukkaṅ kuṟuki varumāyiṉ avaṟṟuḷ avvoḻukkam uṭiyār peyar paṇpu mutalāyiṉa kiḷantu collappaṭum. ivvāṟaṉṟi akappuṟavoḻukkaṅkaḷ iraṇṭum iṇintu oppa varumāyiṉ aṅkum ammakkaḷ peyar paṇpu mutalāyiṉa kiḷantu collappaṭum eṉpatu aṟika. iṅkuc collappaṭṭa ivvilakkaṇaṅkaḷ ivvinnūṟāṇṭiṟ piṟanta nūlkaḷilellām iṉitu kāṇappaṭum.
