	kāḻūṉṟiya kavikiṭukiṉ
	mēlūṉṟiya tukiṟkoṭiyum
	palkēḷvit tuṟipōkiya
170.	tollāṇi nallāciriya
	ruṟaḻkuṟit teṭutta vurukeḻu koṭiyum
	veḷiliḷakkuṅ kaḷiṟupōlat
	tīmpukārt tirimuṉ ṟurit
	tūṅkunāvāy tuvaṉṟirukki
175.	micikkūmpi ṉacikkoṭiyu
	mīṉṟaṭintu viṭakkaṟut
	tūṉporikku molimuṉṟiṉ
	maṇaṟkuvii malarcitaṟip
	palarpukumaṉip palipputavi
180.	ṉaṟavu noṭik koṭiyōṭu
	piṟapiṟavu naṉiviriip
	palvē ṟuruviṟ patāki nīḻaṟ
	celkatir nuḻiyāc ceḻunakar varippiṟ
	cellā nallici yamarar kāppi
185.	ṉīriṉ vanta nimirparip puraviyuṅ
	kāliṉ vanta karuṅkaṟi mūṭiyum
	vaṭamamip piṟanta maṇiyum poṉṉuṅ
	kuṭamalip piṟanta vāramu makilun
	teṉkaṭaṉ muttuṅ kuṇakuṭaṟ ṟukiruṅ
190.	kaṅki vāriyuṅ kāvirip payaṉu
	mīḻat tuṇavuṅ kāḻakat tākkamu
	mariyavum periyavu neḷiya vīṇṭi
	vaḷantali mayaṅkiya naṉantali maṟukiṉ
	nīrnāp paṇṇu nilattiṉ mēlu
195.	mēmāppa viṉitutuñcik
	kiḷikalittup pakipēṇātu
	valiñarmuṉṟiṉ mīṉpiṟaḻavum
	viliñarkurampi māvīṇṭavuṅ
	kolikaṭintuṅ kaḷavunīkkiyu
200.	mamararp pēṇiyu māvuti yaruttiyu
	nallāṉoṭu pakaṭōmpiyu
	nāṉmaṟiyōr pukaḻparappiyum
	paṇṇiya maṭṭiyum pacumpataṅ koṭuttum
	puṇṇiya muṭṭāt taṇṇiḻal vāḻkkik
205.	koṭumēḻi naciyuḻavar
	neṭunukattup pakalpōla
	naṭuvuniṉṟa naṉṉeñciṉōr
	vaṭuvañci vāymoḻintu
	tamavum piṟavu moppa nāṭik
210.	koḷvatūu mikikoḷātu koṭuppatūuṅ kuṟipaṭātu
	palpaṇṭam pakarntu vīcun
	tolkoṇṭit tuvaṉṟirukkip
	pallāyamoṭu patipaḻaki
	vēṟuvē ṟuyarnta mutuvā yokkaṟ
215.	cāṟayar mūtūr ceṉṟutok kāṅku
	moḻipala perukiya paḻitīr tēettup
	pulampeyar mākkaḷ kalantiṉi tuṟiyu
	muṭṭāc ciṟappiṟ paṭṭiṉam peṟiṉum
	vāriruṅ kūntal vayaṅkiḻi yoḻiya
220.	vārēṉ vāḻiya neñcē kūrukirk
	koṭuvarik kuruḷi kūṭṭuḷ vaḷarntāṅkup
	piṟarpiṇi yakattiruntu pīṭukāḻ muṟṟi
	yaruṅkari kaviyak kuttik kuḻikoṉṟu
	peruṅki yāṉi piṭippuk kāṅku
225.	nuṇṇiti ṉuṇara nāṭi naṇṇār
	ceṟivuṭit tiṇkāp pēṟi vāḷkaḻit
	turukeḻu tāya mūḻi ṉeytip
	peṟṟavi makiḻtal ceyyāṉ ceṟṟōr
	kaṭiyaraṇ ṭolitta katavukoṉ maruppiṉ
230.	muṭiyuṭik karuntali puraṭṭu muṉṟā
	ḷukiruṭi yaṭiya vōṅkeḻil yāṉi
	vaṭimaṇip puraviyoṭu vayavar vīḻap
	perunal vāṉattup paruntulāy naṭappat
	tūṟivar tuṟukaṟ pōlap pōrvēṭṭu
235.	vēṟupal pūḷiyō ṭuḻiñi cūṭip
	pēykka ṇaṉṉa piḷiṟukaṭi muraca
	mākka ṇakalaṟi yatirvaṉa muḻaṅka
	muṉikeṭac ceṉṟu muṉcama murukkit
	talitavac ceṉṟu taṇpaṇi yeṭuppi
240.	veṇpūk karumpoṭu cenne ṉīṭi
	māyitaḻk kuvaḷiyoṭu neytalu mayaṅkik
	karāaṅ kalitta kaṇṇakaṉ poykik
	koḻuṅkāṟ putavamoṭu cerunti nīṭic
	ceṟuvum vāviyu mayaṅki nīraṟ
245.	ṟaṟukōṭṭi ṭiraliyoṭu māṉpiṇi yukaḷavuṅ
	koṇṭi makaḷi ruṇṭuṟi mūḻki
	yanti māṭṭiya nantā viḷakkiṉ
	malaraṇi meḻukka mēṟip palartoḻa
	vampalar cēkkuṅ kalantuṭip potiyiṟ
250.	parunili neṭuntū ṇolkat tīṇṭip 
	perunal yāṉiyoṭu piṭipuṇarnta tuṟiyavu
	maruvili naṟumpūt tūuyt teruviṉ
	mutuvāyk kōṭiyar muḻavoṭu puṇarnta
	tiripura narampiṉ ṟīntoṭi yōrkkum
255.	peruviḻāk kaḻinta pēemutir maṉṟattuc
	ciṟupū neruñciyō ṭaṟuki pampi
	yaḻalvā yōri yañcuvarak katirppavu
	maḻukuraṟ kūkiyō ṭāṇṭali viḷippavuṅ
	kaṇaṅkoḷ kūḷiyoṭu katuppikut taciip
260.	piṇantiṉ yākkip pēymaka ṭuvaṉṟavuṅ
	koṭuṅkāṉ māṭattu neṭuṅkaṭit tuvaṉṟi
	viruntuṇ ṭāṉāp peruñcōṟ ṟaṭṭi
	loṇcuvar nalli luyartiṇi yiruntu
	piṅkiḷi miḻaṟṟum pālār ceḻunakart
265.	toṭutō laṭiyar tuṭipaṭak kuḻīik
	koṭuvi leyiṉar koḷḷi yuṇṭa
	vuṇavil vaṟuṅkūṭ ṭuḷḷakat tiruntu
	vaḷivāyk kūki naṇpakaṟ kuḻaṟavu
	maruṅkaṭi varippi ṉūrkavi ṉaḻiyap
270.	perumpāḻ ceytu mamiyāṉ maruṅkaṟa
	maliyakaḻk kuvaṉē kaṭaṟūrk kuvaṉē
	vāṉvīḻk kuvaṉē vaḷimāṟ ṟuvaṉeṉat
	tāṉmuṉṉiya tuṟipōkaliṟ
	palloḷiyar paṇipoṭuṅkat
275.	tollaruvāḷar toḻilkēṭpa
	vaṭavar vāṭak kuṭavar kūmpat
	teṉṉavaṉ ṟiṟalkeṭac cīṟi maṉṉar
	maṉṉeyil katuvu mataṉuṭi nōṉṟāṇ
	māttāṉi maṟamoympiṟ
280.	ceṅkaṇṇāṟ ceyirttu nōkkip
	puṉpotuvar vaḻipoṉṟa
	viruṅkōvēṇ maruṅkucāyak
	kāṭukoṉṟu nāṭākkik
	kuḷaṅtoṭṭu vaḷamperukkip
285.	piṟaṅkunili māṭat tuṟanti pōkkik
	kōyiloṭu kuṭiniṟīi
	vāyiloṭu puḻiyamittu
	ñāyiṟoṟum putiniṟīip
	poruvēmeṉap peyarkoṭut
290.	toruvēmeṉap puṟakkoṭātu
	tiruniliiya perumaṉṉeyiṉ
	miṉṉoḷi yeṟippat tammoḷi maḻuṅki
	vicipiṇi muḻaviṉ vēntar cūṭiya
	pacumaṇi poruta parēreṟuḻk kaḻaṟkāṟ
295.	poṟṟoṭip putalva rōṭi yāṭavu
	muṟṟiḻi makaḷir mukiḻmuli tiḷippavuñ
	ceñcāntu citinta mārpi ṉoṇpū
	ṇarimā vaṉṉa vaṇaṅkuṭit tuppiṟ
	ṟirumā vaḷavaṉ ṟevvark kōkkiya
300.	vēliṉum veyya kāṉamavaṉ
	kōliṉun taṇṇiya taṭameṉ ṟōḷē.



aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	 tītteṟutaliṉ.
2.	vilakkum.
3.	`karuntoḻuti.   
4.	vaṟaḷaṭumpiṉ
5.	piṟakkoṭāatu
6.	makiḻntum
7.	kāyciṉak katirccelvaṉ
8.	pēriṭikaḻi
9.	miñciki
10.	taṇpaṇṇiyattu
3. poruṭpākupāṭu
(1-8) kāviriyāṟṟiṉ ciṟappu
kuṭakamaliyiṉiṭattē tōṉṟi eṉṟum nīr aṟātu oḻukum iyalpiṉatāyc cōḻanāṭṭi vaḷampaṭuttu varukiṉṟa kāviriyāṟṟiṉ ciṟappu mutaṟkaṭ collappaṭukiṉṟatu. maḻi vaṟakkuṅ kālattil cukkiraṉ eṉappaṭum vāṉmīṉ kīḻttici yiṉiṉṟu mēṟṟiciyiṟ cellāmal teṉṟicip pakkamāyc cellumeṉpatum, maḻittuḷiyip paruki uyirvāḻkiṉṟa vāṉampāṭippuḷ atu peṟāmiyiṉ appōtu mika vāṭumeṉpatum, ittaṉmiyāka maḻipeyyātu māṟippōṉa av vaṟkaṭa kālattilum kāviriyāṟu nīrvaṟṟātu oḻukippōy vayal nilaṅkaḷi niṟittu aṅkē poṟṟukaḷiyum tiraṭṭit tokukku meṉpatum collappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
(9-19) cōḻanāṭṭu marutanilavaḷam
vayalkaḷ eṉṟum māṟāmal viḷintu koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. aṅkē karuppañcāṟṟik kāyccukiṉṟa koṭṭilkaḷ pala irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟil ataṉik kāyccumpōtu uṇṭākum tīppukipaṭṭup pakkattē vayalkaḷil malarnta neytaṟpūkkaḷ aḻakukeṭṭu vāṭukiṉṟaṉa. kāytta cenneṟkatiri nirampa uṇṭu vayiṟu parutta muṟṟiṉa erumikkaṉṟukaḷ āṅkāṅkuḷḷa neṟkūṭukaḷiṉ nīḻalilē uṟaṅkik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. kuli neruṅkiṉa teṉṉi kulivāḻi pākkukkāyi yuṭiya kamuku mā paṉi mutaliya maraṅkaḷum mañcaḷ cēmpu iñci mutaliya ceṭikaḷum aṭarntu vaḷarntirukkiṉṟaṉa; eṉpatu collap paṭṭatu. itu kāṟuṅ kāviripāyuñ cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ vaḷam potuvakiyāṟ kūṟi iṉip pākkam kaḻi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ ciṟappi virittuk kūṟappukukiṉṟār.
(20-27) pākkam
pākkaṅkaḷilē akaṉṟa vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. av vīṭṭu muṟṟaṅkaḷilē ularavittirukkum nellukkuk kāvalāy irukkuñ ciṟu peṇkaḷ oḷi viḷaṅku neṟṟiyum kaḷḷam aṟiyāta pārviyum tiruntiya aṇikalaṉ kaḷum uṭiyar; ivarkaḷ an nellit tiṉṉavaruṅ kōḻikaḷi veruṭṭum poruṭṭu eṟinta kaṟavuk kuḻikaḷ1 am muṟṟattiṟ citaṟikkiṭantu, aṅkē ciṟu piyaṉkaḷ mūṉṟu uruḷ uṭiya ciṟiya tērik kutiriyiṉṟi iḻuttuk koṇṭu varukiyil, ataṉ uruḷkaḷi iṭaṟi acciṟutēr pōkāmal vaḻimuṉpi vilakkukiṉṟaṉa.2 tām maṉaṅkalaṅkutaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa paki tamakkuc ciṟitumillāmiyāl maṉakkoḻumiyiṉiyuṭiya pala kuṭikaḷ niṟiyap peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa appākkaṅkaḷellām.
(28 - 39) paṭappi mutaliyaṉa
iṉi oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu arukiluḷḷa pala ūrkaḷiyuṭiya nīṇṭa cōḻaṉāṭṭil eṅkuñceṉṟu veḷḷiya uppi viliceytu nelli ēṟṟik koṇṭuvanta valiya paṭakukaḷ pantiyilē niṟkuṅ kutirikaḷippōlat taṟika ṭōṟuṅ kaṭṭappeṟṟirukkum uppaṅkaḻikaḷ cūḻnta neytal nilattut tōṭṭaṅkaḷum, maṉaveḻucci tarutaṟ kētuvāṉa putuvaruvāyiṉiyuṭiya tōppukaḷum, attōppukaḷukkup puṟampēyuḷḷa pūñcōlikaḷum, mukiṟkaṟi ciṟitumillātu teḷinta vāṉattil tiṅkaḷic cūḻntu makam eṉṉum vāṉmīṉ viḷaṅkiṉāṟ pōṉṟa kariyiṉi allatu kōyiliyuṭittāy maṇaṅkamaḻum pala vaṇṇap pūkkaḷ nirampiyiruttalāṟ palaniṟamuṭittāyt tōṉṟum poykikaḷum, immiyilum maṟumiyilum kāmaviṉpa nukarcciyiṉip payakkum iṇinta ērikaḷum, ak kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉam eṅkum irukkiṉṟaṉa.
(40-50) cōṟiṭum aṭṭiṟ cālikaḷ
katavukaḷellām puliyurukkaḷ cetukkappaṭṭaṉavāyt tamvāy poruntap peṟṟiruppat, tirumakaḷi eḻutiya tiṇṇiya matilkaḷ amintu immiyiṟ pukaḻ paramputaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa naṟcol vaḷaravum maṟumiyiṉit tarutaṟkuriya aṟavoḻukkam nilipeṟavuñ ciṟanta aṭukkaḷikaḷil mikutiyākac camitta cōṟṟi vaṭittalāl oḻukuṅ koḻuṅ kañciyāṉatu yāṟṟil veḷḷam vantāṟpōla eṅkum paravi ōṭuvatāyiṟṟu; ak kañciyik kaṇṭu parukutaṟkuc ceṉṟa eruttumāṭukaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu caṇṭiyiṭutalāl atu maṇṇoṭu kalantu cēṟāyp piṉ tērkaḷ oṭutalāṟ puḻutiyāy mēleḻuntu pala vēṟuvakiyāṉa cittiraṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭirunta veḷḷiya araṇmaṉiyiṉmēṟ paṭintu veṇṇīṟṟiṟ puraṇṭa kaḷiṟṟiyāṉipōl aḻukkēṟap paṇṇuvatāyiṟṟu.
(51 - 53) putta, camaṇap paḷḷikaḷ
iṉṉum, kuḷirnta kiṇaṟukaḷ poruntap peṟṟaṉa vāyp periya eruttu māṭukaḷ niṟkum palacālikaḷum, tava oḻukkam naṭipeṟum amaṇar puttaruṭiya paḷḷikaḷum āṅkāṅkuḷḷaṉa.
(53 - 58) kāḷikōṭṭam
tām taṅkum iḷamarakkāvilē viḷaṅkiya caṭimuṭiyuṭiya rāṉa muṉivar neruppiliṭṭu vēṭkum neyp pukiyik kaṇṭu puyaleṉa añciya kuyiṟcēvalkaḷ ac cōliyiliruttali veṟuttut tampeṭikaḷōṭum av viṭatti viṭṭuppōyp pūtaṅkaḷ kāvalāyiruttalāṟ pukutaṟku ariya kāḷikōṭṭattiṟ ceṉṟu tūtuṇam puṟākkaḷōṭum ōr otukkiṭattē taṅkiyirukkum.
(59 - 74) cempaṭavar kuppam
neṭunāṭpaṭṭa maraṅkaḷi yuṭiyavāyc caṇṭi yiṭutaṟkuc camitta iṭaṅkaḷum nīr aritta kariya maṇalum uḷḷa akaṉṟa mēṭṭuk kuppaṅkaḷil kuṭiyirukkum periya kaṟṟat tiṉiyum iṉamāṉa uṟaviṉiyumuṭiya valiya toḻilāḷa rāṉa cempaṭavar kaṭal iṟāmīṉiṉ cuṭṭa taciyit tiṉṟum, vayal āmiyip puḻukkiṉa iṟicciyi uṇṭum, vayaliṟ paṭarnta aṭappampūvit taliyiṟ kaṭṭiyum, nīril niṉṟa āmpalmalarip paṟittuk cūṭiyum, nīlaniṟat tiṉiyuṭiya vāṉattil valampuṟamāy eḻuntu cuḻaṉṟu tiriyum naṭcattiraṅ kaḷōṭu kūṭiya kirakaṅkaḷip pōlac caṇṭiyiṭāniṉṟa av vakaṉṟa maṉṟattiṟ palarum oruṅku tiraṇṭu kiyoṭu kipiṇintum paṭikkalaṅkaḷōṭu paṭikkalan tākkiyum uṭampōṭu uṭampupaṭa uriciyum mikka ciṉattōṭum oruvar oruvarkkup piṉṉiṭiyātu peruñcaṇṭi ceyta ataṉāṉum valiyaṭaṅkārāy oruvarōṭoruvar māṟupaṭṭuk kalli vīcukiṉṟaṉar. aṅṅaṉam avar eṟiyuṅ kavaṇkalliṟku mika añcip pacceṉṟa paṉimarattiṉmēlirunta paṟavikaḷ paṟantu pōkiṉṟaṉa.
(75 -77) puṟaccēriyiṉ taṉmi
kuṭṭikaḷiyuṭiya paṉṟiyum pala kōḻikaḷum uṟi vitta ciṟiya kiṇaṟukaḷum irukkiṉṟa ac cempaṭavar puṟaccēriyilē cemmaṟi āṭṭuk kiṭāykaḷōṭu kutārip paṟavikaḷ viḷiyāṭā niṟkum.
(78 - 105) kāvirittuṟic ciṟappu
naṭṭakalliṟ ṟeyvamāy niṉṟavaṉukkuk kēṭakattivariciyāvittu vēli niṟuttiyavāṟupōla nīṇṭa tūṇṭiyaṟkōlic cārtti vittirukkum kuṟukiṉa kūriyiyuṭiya kuṭiyiruppukaḷiṉ naṭuvil, nilaviṉ iṭiyilē cērnta iruḷippōla mīṉvali yularum veḷḷiya maṇalparanta muṟṟattiṉkaṇ, viḻutiyuṭiya tāḻañceṭiyiṉ kīḻ vaḷarnta veṇkūtāḷa malarāṟ ceyta māliyi yaṇintavarāyc ciṉi koṇṭa cuṟāmīṉ kompi naṭṭu ataṉiyē irukkumiṭamāka ēṟṟiya valiya kaṭaṟṟeyvattiṉ poruṭṭāka maṭaliyuṭiya tāḻimalarik cūṭiyum, porinta marattiṉiyuṭiya paṉiyiṉ kaḷḷik kuṭittum, pulliya taliyiṉiyuṭiya cempaṭavar paciya taḻiyiṉi yuṭutta tampeṇṭi rōṭum paranta paṉikkaṭalil mīṉ piṭikkavuñ cellātu attoḻiliviṭṭu muḻumati viḷaṅkum uvānāḷilē tām vēṇṭuvaṉa uṇṭu viḷiyāṭiyum, piṉ pulālnāṟṟam vīcukiṉṟa maṇaliyuṭiya pūṅkāṉaṟcōliyil kariyamaliyip peruntikkiṭanta cekkarmēkam pōlavum tāyiṉ koṅkiyit taḻuvik kiṭanta makavip pōlavum teḷinta nīriyuṭiya kaṭaloṭu kāviriyāṟu ceṉṟu kalakkum mikak kuḷirntu muḻaṅkā niṉṟa pukār mukattil tīviṉipōkak kaṭal muḻukkāṭiyum, piṉ avvuppunīṅka vēṟunīrilē kuḷittum, naṇṭukaḷip piṭittu āṭṭiyum, ulavukiṉṟa aliyiṟpōy ataṉi uḻakkiyum, pāvi ceytum, aimpoṟikaḷāṉum nukarappaṭuvaṉa vellām nukarntu mayaṅkiyum, avarkaḷ oruvari yoruvar nīṅkāta kātaṟkiḻami yuṭiyarāy, ivvāṟu pakaṟpoḻutellām pōkkutaṟkuk kāraṇamāvatāyp peṟutaṟkariya paḻañ ciṟappukaḷiyuṭiya cuvarkka nāṭṭi oppatāy eṉṟum poyyāmal nīr varutaliyuṭiyatāyp pūkkaḷ niṟinta kāvirittuṟi viḷaṅkumeṉka.
(106 - 115) kaṭiyāmac ciṟappu
taṅkoḻunarip puṇarnta aḻakiya maṅkiyar paṭṭāṭikaḷik kaḷinteṟintu veṇṭukil uṭuttavarāyt tēṉuṇ ṭali viṭuttuk kāmaviṉpac cuvittēṉi niṟiya uṇṇā niṉṟu taṅkātalar cūṭiya māliyit tamateṉṟeṭuttuc cūṭikkoḷḷavum, taṅ kātalimār malinta kōtiyit tamateṉṟu avar kātalar aṇintu koḷḷavumāka avar kāmaviṉpac cuvi nukarkiṉṟa uyarnta māṭaṅkaḷil eriyum viḷakkaṅkaḷip pārttu muṉvaḷinta kaṭṭumarattik kaṭaliṭattē koṇṭu ceṉṟa paratavar avaṟṟi eṇṇā niṟpavum, an nakarattiluḷḷār iravil muṉ yāmattup pāṭṭuk kēṭṭum nāṭakam pārttum veṇṇilaviṟ peṟum payaṉkaḷit tuyttum kaṇṇuṟakkam peṟṟa kaṭiyāma meṉka.
(116 - 125) cuṅkaṅkoḷvōr nili
av viyāmattilē, periya kāviriyāṟu palavayiṉirunta pūkkaḷiṉ maṇamellām tiraṭṭikkoṇṭu vantu cērkkum tūya ekkar maṇalil ayarntu uṟaṅkiya vaṇṇamāy, veḷḷiya pūṅkottum maṭalum uḷḷa tāḻiyiṉiyum kariyiṉiyumuṭiya akaṉṟa paṇṭacālit teruvil nalla aracaṉatu poruḷip piṟar kavarāmaṟ kākkum aṭippaṭṭa pukaḻiṉi
yuṭiya kāvaṟ ṟoḻilāḷar kāyuñ ciṉattiyuṭiya veṅkatirc celvaṉāṉa pakalavaṉatu tēriṟ kaṭṭiya puravi oru noṭippoḻutāyiṉum maṭintirātu iyaṅkutal pōla nāṭōṟum iḷippuṟātu cuṅkaṅkoḷḷum paṭiyāka iruppareṉka.
(126 - 141) paṇṭacāli muṟṟam
kuṟiviṉṟi mukanta nīri mukil maliyilē poḻiyavum, maliyiṟ poḻinta nīr kaṭaliṟ ceṉṟu paravavum mārikkālattē peytalpōlap piṟanāṭukaḷiliruntu kaṭaṉmēl vantaṉa kariyi lēṟavum, tamiḻ nāṭukaḷiliruntu vantu kariyiṟ kiṭappaṉa piṟa nāṭukaḷukkup pōkalvēṇṭi nīrmēl marakkalaṅkaḷiṟ cēravumāka, aṟivāṉ aḷantaṟitaṟ kariyavākavuḷḷa pala paṇṭaṅkaḷum varampaṟiyappaṭāvāṟu vantu toka, ariya kāvalaminta periya cuṅkaccāvaṭiyilē kāvaṟkāraṉ cōḻa maṉṉar aṭiyāḷa muttiriyāṉa puliyi ilacciṉiyāka iṭṭup piṉ avaṟṟi veḷiyē pōkkiṉamiyāl, matittaṟiyappaṭṭa ap pala paṇṭaṅkaḷum potintu vikkappaṭṭa mūṭṭip pōrmēl ēṟi mukil ulāvap peṟuṅ kuvaṭṭiṉiyuṭiya periya malip pakkaṅkaḷilē viḷiyāṭum varuṭi māṉkaḷiṉ ṟōṟṟam pōlak kūriya nakaṅkaḷiyuṭiya āṇ nāykaḷ āṭṭuk kiṭāykaḷōṭu kutikkum paṇṭacāli muṟṟameṉka.
(142-158) aṅkāṭit teru
aṇuka aṇukap paṭikaḷ toṭukkappaṭṭu aminta nīṇṭa ēṇikaḷ cārttiyirukkum cuṟṟut tiṇṇikaḷum, pala kaṭṭukaḷum, ciṟuvāyil peruvāyilkaḷum, periya iṭi kaḻikaḷum3 poruntac camikkap peṟṟaṉavāy, nīr mukilkaḷ urāyum uyarnta māṭattil civanta aṭiyiṉiyum neruṅkiya toṭikaḷiyum paciya aṇikalaṅkaḷiyum periya ariyiṉiyum nuṇṇiya āṭiyiṉiyum pavaḷam pōl niṟattiṉiyum mayilpōṟ ṟōṟṟattiṉiyum māṉ pōl nōkkiṉiyum kiḷipōlum maḻalimoḻiyiṉiyum melleṉṟa cāyaliṉiyum uṭiyapeṇkaḷ teṉṟaṟ kāṟṟu nuḻiyum cāḷarattip porunti niṉṟu, uyarnta malippakkattil nuṇṇiya makarantappoṭiyic coriyum kāntaṭceṭiyiṉ kaṇukkaḷiliruntu kiḷikkum kavinta mukik kuliyip pōlum tamatu toṭi neruṅkiya kiyi mēleṭuttut toḻā niṟpa, murukap pirāṉukku veṟiyāṭṭu eṭukkum makaḷirkkup poruntap parampi vēyṅkuḻal icikkavum yāḻ olikkavum muḻā muḻakkañ ceyyavum muracam olippavum tiruviḻā iṭiyaṟātu naṭipeṟum aṅkāṭit teruveṉka.
(159 - 183) nakariṟ koṭic ciṟappu
kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya teyvatti ēṟṟuvitta malarkaḷaṇinta kōyil vāyililē palarun toḻutu celluṅ kōḻikkoṭiyum, uṇavup poruḷkaḷ vikkappaṭṭirukkuṅ koḻuviya pēḻiyiṉit tāḻiṭuvittup pōṟṟiya kuḷirnta palapaṇṭaṅkaḷiṉmēl veḷḷiya ariciyip paliyākac citaṟip pākākak kāycci vārtta paciya meḻukkip pūciya kōlkaḷi nāṭṭi avaṟṟiṉ mēṟ kavittiṭṭa caṭṭattiṉmēl, varukiṉṟa nīrāṟ ṟiraṭṭic cērkkappaṭṭa veṇmaṇal nirampiya kāṭṭiyāṟṟiṉ kari maruṅkil niṉṟa aḻakiya karumpiṉ oṇpūppōlat tōṉṟumpaṭiyāka naṭṭa tukiṟkoṭiyum, palavākiya nūṟ kēḷvikaḷil tuṟipōkak kaṟṟamiyāṉē toṉṟu toṭṭup piṟari ēvuvataṟku urimivāynta nal āciriyarāyuḷḷār vaḻakkiṭṭup piṟarkku meypporuḷ teruṭṭutaṟ poruṭṭu nāṭṭiya accantaruṅ koṭikaḷum, kaṭṭut taṟiyi acikkuṅ kaḷiṟupōlak kāṇpārkaṭkiṉiya kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉak kaṭaṟṟuri mukattil neruṅki niṉṟu aciyum marakkalaṅkaḷiṉmēṟ kaṭṭiya koṭikaḷum, mīṉiyum iṟicciyiyum aṟuttu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉi neyyiṟ porikkiṉṟamiyāl āravāram niṟinta muṟṟattilē maṇalik kuvittu malaric citaṟik kaḷḷuṇpār palarum nuḻitaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa kaḷḷuk kaṭiyiṉ palitarappeṟṟa vāyililē kaḷḷuviṟṟali yaṟivittaṟku aṟikuṟiyākak kaṭṭiya koṭiyum, ākiya ivaṟṟōṭu iṉṉum vēṟu vēṟu koṭikaḷum mikka kalavāniṟpap palvēṟu uruvāṟ ciṟanta peruṅkoṭikaḷum niṟintamiyāl avaṟṟiṉ nīḻalilē ñāyiṟṟiṉ kiraṇaṅkaḷum nuḻiyak kūṭātaṉavāṉa vaḷam poruntiya nakar eṉka.
(183 - 193) terukkaḷiluḷḷa poruḷvaḷam
ippeṟṟittāṉa nakarattiṉ elliyil keṭāta naṟpukaḻiyuṭiya tēvarkaḷ kāvalāy niṟkiṉṟamiyāl iṭiyūṟu ciṟitumiṉṟip piṟanāṭṭiliruntu kaṭal  vaḻiyē nāvāyiṟ koṇṭuvanta nimirntu virivāyc celluṅ kutirikaḷum, vaṭṭiyilē koṇṭuvanta kariya miḷakup potikaḷum, vaṭakkē imayam mēru mutaliya malikaḷiṉiṉṟuṅ koṇṭu vanta palatiṟappaṭṭa maṇikaḷum poṉṉurukkuk kaṭṭikaḷum, kuṭakumaliyiṉiṉṟum vanta cantaṉakkaṭṭi akiṟkaṭṭikaḷum, teṉkaṭaliṟkuḷittu eṭuttu vanta muḻumuttukaḷum, kīḻkaṭaliṟ ṟuruvik koṇṭu vanta cempavaḷaṅkaḷum, kaṅkiyāṟṟiṟ piṟanta poruḷkaḷum, kāviriyāṟṟiṉ poruḷkaḷum, ilaṅkit tīviṉiṉṟum pōnta uṇāp poruḷkaḷum, kaṭārattiṉiṉṟu koṇarnta nukarccip poruḷkaḷum, iviyēyaṉṟi yiṉṉum pala ariya periya paṇṭaṅkaḷum nilan tāṅkamāṭṭāmal neḷiyumpaṭi oruṅku tokkamiyiṉālē palavakippaṭṭa poruḷvaḷaṅkaḷum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu aḷāya akaṉṟa terukkaḷeṉka.
(193 - 212) vēḷāḷar kuṭicciṟappu
it taṉmittākiya teruviṉum, tuṟimukattu naṭuviṉum, kaṭaṟkari maruṅkiṉum makiḻntu iṉitu uṟaṅkic cuṟṟam taḻittut tamakkup pakiyāvār ivareṉṟu eṇṇātu valiñar muṉṟililē mīṉ pāyavum ūṉviṟpār kuṭiciyilē āṭu mutalāṉa vilaṅkukaḷ tiraṇṭu niṟkavum āka ivvāṟu kolipurivāri ataṉiṉiṉṟu nīkkiyum, kaḷavu kāṇpāri ataṉiṉiṉṟu nīkkiyum, tēvarip pōṟṟiyum, āriyar uvakkumpaṭi avar vēṇṭum vēḷvikaḷi vēṭṭu avari nukarvittum, nalla ākkaḷōṭu erutukaḷip pātukāttum, nāṉmaṟi vallōrkkum uṇṭām pukaḻi nilipeṟac ceytum, varum viruntiṉarkku nukartaṟkuriya pala paṇṭaṅkaḷ koṭuttum paciya cōṟu koṭuttum aṟavoḻukkam piṟaḻātu yārkkum kuḷirppakkūṟi aṉpu curakkum vāḻkkiyiṉkaṇ nilipeṟṟa vaḷinta kalappiyiṉ uḻavu toḻiliyē naccukiṉṟa vēḷāḷar tamatu nīṇṭa nukattiṟ ṟitta pakalāṇi pōl naṭunili tiṟampātu niṉṟa nalla neñciṉiyuṭiyarāyt tamakkun taṅkuṭikkum vaṭuvāmeṉṟuṇarntu poyyi yoḻittu meymmiyē kūṟit tammuṭiya paṇṭaṅkaḷiyum piṟaruṭiya paṇṭaṅkaḷiyum oru peṟṟiyākakkaṇṭu, tām piṟariṭattu ap paṇṭaṅkaḷi vilikoḷḷumpōtu mikutiyākak koḷḷāmalum, tam paṇṭaṅkaḷip piṟarkku viliyiṭuṅkāṟ kuṟiyak koṭāmalum ūtiyatti veḷippaṭiyākac colli viṟṟu in neṟiyē toṉṟutoṭṭu varampiṉṟip poruḷ tokuttu avar neruṅkiyirukkuṅ kuṭiyiruppu eṉka.
(213-218) kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉac ciṟappu muṭipu
vēṟuvēṟākac ciṟanta aṟivu vāytta cuṟṟattiṉiyuṭiya naṉmakkaḷ tiruviḻākkoṇṭāṭum paḻiya ūrilē ceṉṟu kūṭiṉāṟpōlap, palvēṟu vakippaṭṭa moḻikaḷ vaḻaṅkuvōrāyk kuṟṟamaṟṟa palanāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum vanta makkaḷ ellārum ceṉṟu taṉkaṇ uḷḷa naṉmakkaḷ kūṭṭam palavōṭum paḻakikkalantu uṟiyum vaḻuvāta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉa meṉka.
(218- 220) pāṭṭiṟ karukkoṇṭa poruḷ
ivviyalpiṉatāyc ciṟanta kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattiyē eṉakku urimipporuḷākap peṟuvēṉāyiṉum ē neñcamē! nīṇṭa karuṅ kūntaliṉiyum viḷaṅkum pūṇiṉiyumuṭiya eṉ kātali īṇṭut taṉiyaḷāyiruppa ivaṭaṉṉip pirintu varutaṟku oruppaṭamāṭṭēṉ kaṇṭāy eṉka.
(220 - 239) karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉ, taṉṉic ciṟiceyta
(pakivarit tappippōy avari veṉṟami.)
kūriya nakattiṉiyuṭiya pulikkuṭṭiyāṉatu kūṭṭil aṭipaṭṭu vaḷarntāṟpōlap piṟariṭṭa ciṟik kāvaliṟ ṟaṅkiyiruntu ataṉāṟ ṟaṉ perumikkuṇam vayiramēṟi muṟṟutalāṉē, kuḻiyil vīḻnta kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi ataṉ kariyik kompāṟ kuttit tūrttut taṉ piṭiyiṉiṭattē ceṉṟu cērntāṟpōlat taṉṉuṇarvāṟ kūrttaṟintu pārttup pakivaratu kāvalaminta ciṟikkaḷattu valiya matilit tāṇṭip puṟattē niṉṟa vāṭpaṭi vīrariyellām oṭṭippōyp pakivarkku accatti viḷikkun taṉṉaraca vurimiyi muṟiyē peṟṟum, aṅṅaṉam peṟṟa avvaḷavil uḷḷam makiḻtal peṟāṉāyp piṉṉum maṇkoḷal vēṇṭu meṉṟeḻunta naciyāl, pakivaruṭiya kāvalaminta araṇkaḷi aḻittuk katavik kutti yēntiṉa maruppiṉa
tāyp pakivaratu muṭicūṭiya peruntaliyi uruṭṭum muṉṟāḷil nakaṅkaḷ poruntap peṟṟa aṭikaḷiyuṭiya yāṉiyōṭum taṭittamaṇikaḷ kaṭṭiya kutiriyōṭum paṭiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟu māṟṟārtam paṭimaṟavar pōrk
kaḷattu viḻavum, periya vāṉattiṉkaṭ paruntukaḷ ulāvavum, tūṟu paṭarnta karuṅkaṟ pāṟipōṟ pōrvēṇṭi yeḻuntamikku aṭiyāḷamākap pūḷippūvum uḻiñippūvuñ cūṭip pēyiṉkaṇṇip pōṉṟa muḻaṅkum pōrmuracam akaṉṟa pācaṟiyilē atirntu muḻaṅkāniṟpac ceṉṟu pakivar iṭaṅkaḷ pāḻākumpaṭi avari mutaṟpōrilē tōlvi peṟacceytu, avvaḷaviṟ ciṉam māṟātu mēlum pōy avar araṇkaḷi aḻittu naṭantu kuḷirnta maruta nilattuk kuṭikaḷit turatti eṉka.
(240 - 245) marutanilam pāḻpaṭṭami
aṅṅaṉamellām ap pakivarūrip pāḻpaṭuttiṉamiyāl muṉṉē veḷḷiyapūviṉiyuṭiya karumpum cennellum nīṇṭu kariya itaḻkkuvaḷiyoṭu neytaṟpūvuṅ kalantu mutalikaḷ cerukkittirinta iṭam akaṉṟa ceḻum poykikaḷellām, ippōtu koḻuntaṇṭukaḷi yuṭiya aṟukum kōrippuṟkaḷum aṭarntu nīr aṟa, ivvāṟē vayalum vāviyum vēṟupāṭiṉṟit tantaṉmi tirintiṭa, aṟuppuḷḷa kompukaḷiyuṭiya pulvāykkalikaḷoṭu māṉ piṇikaḷ āṇṭut tuḷḷiviḷiyāṭum pāḻ iṭamāyiṉa.
(246 - 251) ampalaṅkaḷ pāḻpaṭṭami
tām tam pakivar makaḷiric ciṟiyākak koṇṭu vantamiyiṉ, am makaḷir nīruṇṇum tuṟiyilē muḻuki vantu mālippoḻutilē koḷuttiya viḷakkiṉi yuṭiyatāy malar aṇiyappaṭṭa meḻukiṉa iṭattilē ēṟicceṉṟu uḷḷūrār palar toḻutu cellavum, veḷiyūriṉiṉṟu putiyarāy vantār toḻututaṅkavum amikkappaṭṭa aruṭkuṟi niṟuttiya ampalaṅkaḷellām taṅkaṇ niṉṟa parutta tūṇkaḷ cāyumpaṭiyāka urāyntu kaḷiṟṟi yāṉiyoṭu piṭiyāṉiyuṅ kūṭiyirukkum iṭamāyiṉa eṉka.
(252 - 260) maṉṟam pāḻpaṭṭami
mikka viliyiṉiyuṭiya naṟiyamalarkaḷit tūvit teruvil niṉṟu aṟivuvāyttaliyuṭiya kūttar muḻavukku iciya muṟukkiṉa narampiyuṭiya yāḻici kēṭkum periya tiruviḻākkaḷ illi yāyppōṉa accam mikunta maṉṟaṅkaḷ ciṟiya pūkkaḷiyuṭiya neruñciṟ pūṇṭum aṟukampullum pampa aḻaṉṟa vāyiṉiyuṭiya narikaḷ ūḷi yiṭavum, aḻuṅkura lāyk kūppiṭum kūkikaḷoṭu kōṭṭāṉkaḷumiruntu etir kūvavum, tokuti koṇṭa āṇpēykaḷoṭu mayiri aviḻttuk toṅka viṭṭu iruntu piṇattit tiṉṉum peṇpēykaḷ kūṭavum āyiṉa eṉka.
(261 - 268) nakar pāḻpaṭṭami
vaḷinta kālkaḷāyk kaṭṭappeṟṟa nīṇṭa māṭattiṉ ṟalikkaṭiyiṟ ceṉṟu neruṅki viruntiṉar iṭiyaṟā tuṇṭum kuṟiyāta peruñcōṟṟu vaḷamnirampiya aṭukkaḷi vāyntu oḷiporuntiya cuvarkaḷamintavāṉa nalla illaṅkaḷiṉ uyarnta tiṇṇikaḷiṉ mēliruntu piṅkiḷi iṉcol miḻaṟṟutaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa pāl niṟinta ceḻuviya nakaram, ceruppiṭṭa aṭiyiṉiyuṭiyarāyt tuṭiyolippat tiraṇṭaceṉṟu vaḷinta villiyuṭiya vēṭar koḷḷikoṇṭu uṇṭu  viṭṭamiyiṉālē uṇāviṉṟi vaṟitāyppōṉa neṟkūṭukaḷiṉ uḷḷiruntu vaḷinta vāyiṉiyuṭiya kūkikaḷ naṇpakaṟ kālattuṅ kuḻaṟumpaṭi yāyiṟṟu.
(269 - 299) karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉ veṟṟittiru
iṅṅaṉamellām pukutaṟku ariya kāvalaminta matilcūḻnta pakivar ūrkaḷiyellām perumpāḻ ceytum ataṉālum avarmēṟkoṇṭa ceṟṟam taṇiyap peṟāṉāyt teyvattaṉmi poruntiyavaṉātaliṉ ivaṉ malikaḷi yellām kalluvaṉ, kaṭalit tūrppaṉ, vāṉi viḻacceyvaṉ, kāṟṟi iyaṅkāmal vilakkuvaṉ eṉṟu ivvāṟellām ulakattārellārum mīkkūṟumpaṭi tāṉ niṉittavaṟṟi an niṉittavāṟē tuṟipōka muṭittal vallavaṉākaliṉ, oḷināṭṭār palarum vantu tāḻntu oṭuṅkavum, paḻiya aruvāḷa nāṭṭiṉaracar avaṉ ēviya toḻilic ceytaṟku avaṉcoṟ kēṭpavum, vaṭa nāṭṭiluḷḷa āriya aracar vāṭavum, kuṭa nāṭṭiluḷḷār maṉaveḻucci kuṉṟavum, pāṇṭiyaṉ valikuṟiyavuñ cīṟi, maṉṉartam nilipeṟṟa matilik kippaṟṟum matamuṭittāy valiya tāḷiṉiyuṭiya yāṉippaṭiyum maṟattāṟ ciṟanta valimiyum vāyntavaṉāyc ciṉattāṟ civanta kaṇkaḷāl vekuṇṭu pārttup, pulliya iṭiya aracaratu kāl aḻintupaṭavum, iruṅkōvēḷ cuṟṟattār keṭavum, kāṭṭiyellām aḻittu nāṭākkik kuḷaṅkaḷ akaḻvittup palavaḷaṅkaḷum perukac ceytu, viḷaṅkum māṭattiṉiyuṭiya uṟiyūri viṭuttu, āṇṭuk kōyilkaḷiyuṅ kuṭikaḷiyum nilipeṟac ceytu ciṟuvāyilum peruvāyilum amittu, matilkaḷil ampeyyum kuruvittalikaṭōṟum ampuk kaṭṭukaḷip porunta vittup, pakivar yāvarāyiṉum avaroṭu porutaṟku nērntuḷḷēmeṉa vañciṉaṅ kūṟip, piṉ ataṉiṉiṉṟum piṟaḻamāṭṭēmeṉṉum uṟutiyuṭimi yiṉālē mutukukāṭṭip pōkātu vīrattirumakaḷ eṉṟum nilipeṟṟa periya matiḷ, miṉpōl oḷivīcutalāl, ivaṉip paṇitaṟku vanta vāriṟukkiṉa muḻaviṉiyuṭiya maṟṟi aracar tam oḷimaḻuṅkap peṟṟārāyp, piṉ ivaṉ aruṇōkkat tiṟkut takkārākalvēṇṭi ivaṉip paṇitalāl avar muṭikaḷiṟkuyiṟṟiya maṇikaḷ uriñciya mikka valiyiṉiyuṭiya vīrakkaḻal kaṭṭiṉa kāliṉiyum, poṟṟoṭi yaṇinta taṉputalvar ōṭi viḷiyāṭavum toḻil muṟṟup peṟṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇinta taṉ maṉivimāriṉ mukiḻnta koṅkikaḷ taḻuvavum ceñcantaṉak kuḻampu kalinta mārpiṉiyum, oḷḷiya pūṇkaḷiyum uṭiyaṉāyc ciṅkavēṟṟi yottup pakivarkku varuttatti viḷikkum valiyiṉaṉāka, karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉ eṉka.
(299 - 300) muṉ karukkoṇṭa
(ip pāṭṭiṉ poruḷ muṭivu)
ippeṟṟiyaṉāṉa av vēntaṉ pakivarmēl ōcciya vēlik kāṭṭiṉuṅ koṭiyatāyiruntatu yām pōtaṟku eḻunta kāṉakam; ivaḷ akaṉṟa melliya tōḷkaḷō avaṉ ceṅkōliṉuṅ kuḷirnta vāyirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉka.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1. 	ikkālattil itaṉic `cimikki eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar.
2.	poṟciṟu tērmicip pimpoṟ pōtakam
  naṟciṟā rūrtali ṉaṅkimār virīi
  uṟṟavar kōḻimē leṟinta voṇkuḻi
  maṟṟattē ruruḷkōṭā vaḷami cāṉṟavē.
eṉṟār cintāmaṇiyiṉum,
3.	ikkālattitaṉi rēḻi eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar.
4. pāli
pāliyeṉpatu piritalum pirital nimittamum uṇarttum akoḻukkamām. taṉ maṉakkiṉiya kātaliyi maṇantu koṇṭu talimakaṉ illiruntu illaṟam naṭāttuṅkāl, vēṟṟunāṭṭiṟ ceṉṟu poruṭēṭalik karutit taṉ maṉikkiḻattiyip pirintu pōtaṟkuk kuṟittavaḻi, ak kuṟippiṉi aṟinta avaṉ kātali avaṉip piritaṟku āṟṟāḷāy mika varuntāniṉṟaṉaḷ. av varuttattik kaṇṭu avaḷip piriyap peṟāṉāyt taṉneñci nōkki, muṭṭāc ciṟappiṟ paṭṭiṉam peṟiṉum, vāriruṅ kūntal vayaṅkiḻi yoḻiya, vārēṉ vāḻiya neñcē eṉṟu kūṟit tāṉ cellutal tavirntāṉ. iṅṅaṉañ celavu tavirntatu, ippōtu āṟṟāḷāṉa ivaḷip palatiṟattāṉum aṟivuṟutti āṟṟip piṉ piritaṟ poruṭṭēyām. ākavē, ippōtu tāṉ piritali yoḻintatu piṉ pirintu pōtaṟkuk kāraṇamākiyāl ippāṭṭup pirital nimittat tiṉkaṇ vanta pāliyoḻukkatti viḷakkuvatāyiṟṟeṉka. itaṉi āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār,
 celaviṭi yaḻuṅkal cellāmi yaṉṟē1
vaṉpuṟi kuṟitta ṟavircci yākum.
eṉṟu kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉuṅ kāṇka.
aṭikkuṟippu
1.	poruḷatikāram, kaṟpiyal, 44.
5. vāki
pāli eṉṉum makkaḷ aka oḻukkattik kūṟukiṉṟa ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ avaratu puṟoḻukkamuṅ kūṟal vēṇṭiṉ appāliyeṉṉum akavoḻukkattōṭu iyipuṭiya vāki eṉṉum puṟavoḻukkamē kūṟaṟpāṟṟām. eṉṉi? vāki tāṉē pāliyatu puṟaṉē eṉṟār āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārākaliṉ eṉka. avi iraṇṭum aṅṅaṉam iyipuṭiyavā ṟeṉṉiyeṉiṉ; vāki eṉpatu oruvar tam tiṟattiṉi ēṉiyōriṉ mikkut tōṉṟa viḷakkutalām; eṉavē, iḵtu oruvarkku uḷatām veṟṟi eṉṉum puṟavoḻukkatti uṇarttuvateṉpatu iṉitu peṟappaṭum. pāli eṉpatuṅ kātaṟ kiḻamiyiṟ ciṟanta taṉ kātalipāṟ ceṉṟa aṉpiṉ vaḻiyē oḻuki avaḷiṭattē taṅkiyirātu, illaṟa vāḻkkiyiṉi iṉitu naṭappittaṟku iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭum naṉporuḷ tiraṭṭutaṟporuṭṭu avaḷmēṟ ceṉṟa aṉpiṉi aṭakki veṟṟikaṇṭu talimakaṉ pirintu pōtali uṇarttuvatām. ākavē, akattē nikaḻum aṉpi veṟṟikāṇum pāliyum, puṟattē nikaḻum pakivar maṟatti veṟṟikāṇum vākiyun tammuḷ oppumi yuṭiyavātal teṟṟeṉap pulappaṭum.
iṉit taṉkātalimāṭṭuc ceṉṟa kātali aṭakkip piritaṟku eḻunta talivaṉ avaḷ mika varuntutal kaṇṭu iraṅki avaḷi āṟṟutaṟkut taṅki pāli oḻukkattik kūṟum ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ vāki eṉṉum puṟoḻukkam yāṇṭuk kūṟappaṭṭateṉiṟ kāṭṭutum; karikāṟcōḻaṉ taṉ iḷam paruvattē pakivar iṭṭa ciṟikkaḷattil iruntapōtu, avari vellum vakiyellām niṉintu pārttup piṉ acciṟikkaḷattiṉiṉṟum añcātu tappippōyt taṉ aracavurimiyiṉik kippaṟṟik koṇṭu ap pakivarmēṟ ceṉṟu avari yellām veṉṟu avar nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷi aḻittu veṟṟi vēntaṉāyc ceṅkōl ōcciṉami irunūṟṟirupat tōrām aṭimutaṟkoṇṭu irunūṟṟut toṇṇuṟ ṟoṉpatām aṭikāṟum iṉitu virittuk kāṭṭiṉarākaliṉ pāliyōṭu iyipuṭiya vāki eṉṉum puṟattiṇiyum uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār eṉṉum nallicip pulavarāl īṇṭuc collappaṭṭa teṉka.
6. pāṭṭiṉ varalāṟu
iṉi, ip pāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟip piṉvarumāṟu uyttuṇarntukāṭṭal iḻukkātu. itaṉi yiyaṟṟiya nallicip pulavarāṉa uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār tam āruyirk kātaliyoṭu maruviyiruntu illaṟam naṭāttukiṉṟuḻi avar tamakkuḷḷa poruḷvaḷañ curuṅka vaṟumi vantu nalivatāyiṟṟu. av vaṟumi nōyiṉik kaḷintu viruntōmpi vāḻtaṟku iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭum poruḷ peṟaṟ poruṭṭuk karikāṟcōḻaṉiṭattup pōtaṟkuk karutiṉār. ak karuttaṟinta avar arumi maṉiviyār avarip piritaṟkuc ciṟitum poruntārāy varuntiṉār. av varuttaṅkaṇṭu piritalit tavirnta avvāciriyar tām karikāṟcōḻaṉpāṟ pōtaṟkuk karutiṉamiyum, atu terintu taṉ maṉivi varuntiṉamiyum, avaḷ āṟṟāmiyiṉit taṇittup piṉ orukāṟ piritaṟporuṭṭup pōkātu tavirntamiyum iṉitu viḷaṅka nalamuṟavurittut, tām cōḻa vēntaṉiṭattup paricil peṟavēṇṭiṉamiyum muṭṭāc ciṟappiṟ paṭṭiṉam peṟiṉum eṉak kuṟippāl aṟivittu, akkarikāṟcōḻa maṉṉaṟkuriya ariya periya veṟṟit tiṟaṅkaḷellām naṭantavāṟē moḻintu ivvarumit tiruppāṭṭi aruḷic ceytaṉar. iṅṅaṉam iyaṟṟiya ivvaruntamiḻc ceyyuḷip piṉṉorukāl ivar ac cōḻa vēntaṉpāṟ ceṉṟu kāṭṭa avaṉ ivar ceyyuḷiṉ coṟcuvi poruṭcuvikaḷi mika viyantu ivarkkup patiṉāṟu nūṟāyiram poṉ paricilāka vaḻaṅkic ciṟappuc ceytāṉ; itu taḻuvu centamiḻp paricil vāṇarpoṉ pattoṭāṟu nūṟāyiram peṟap, paṇṭu paṭṭiṉap pālikoṇṭatum eṉṉuṅ kaliṅkattupparaṇic ceyyuḷāl aṟiyappaṭum.
7. ākkiyōṉ varalāṟu
iṉi ivvarumit tiruppāṭṭi aruḷic ceyta nallicip pulavarāṉa uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār kaṭiyalūriṟ piṟantavareṉpatu ivar peyar avvūrp peyaroṭu puṇarttuk kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār eṉṟu vaḻaṅkumāṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum. ivar paṇṭikkālan tamiḻmakkaḷil antaṇar kuṭikku uriyāreṉpatu, tolkāppiyam marapiyaliṟ pērāciriyar  ūrum peyarumuṭit toḻiṟ karuviyum, yāḻuñ cārtti yaviyavi peṟumē eṉṉuñ cūttiravuriyil uṟiyūr ēṇiccēri muṭamōci, peruṅkuṉṟūrp peruṅkucikaṉ, kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉ eṉpaṉa antaṇarkkuriyaṉa eṉṟu uri kūṟiyavaṟṟāl teḷiyappaṭum.
iṉi, ivar iṉṉa camayattik kikkoṇṭu oḻukiṉā
reṉpatu cevvaṉē peṟappaṭavilli. ivvāciriyar tām iyaṟṟiya perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭiyil kāntaḷañ cilampiṟ kaḷiṟupaṭin tāṅkup, pāmpaṇip paḷḷi yamarntō ṉāṅkaṇ eṉṟu kūṟiṉārākaliṉ ivar viṇava camayattip piṉpaṟṟi oḻukiṉā reṉṉāmō eṉiṉ; kāñci nakarattiṟ ceṅkōl occiya toṇṭimāṉ iḷantiriyaṉ eṉṉum vēntaṉpāṟ ceṉṟu paricil peṟumpaṭi pāṇari ēvip pāṭumiṭattu, avaṉ aracu vīṟṟirunta ak kacci nakarattil tirukkōyil koṇṭa tirumāliyum vaḻipaṭṭuc ceṉmiṉ eṉṟu appāṇari nōkkik kūṟiyataṉṟi, ivar tirumāli vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉa reṉpatu ataṉāṟ ciṟitum peṟappaṭāmiyiṉ aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟutal poruntā vuriyāmeṉka. ak kakci nakarattiṟ civapirāṉ kōyilum iruppa ataṉi viṭuttut tirumāl kōyiliyē ivar mikkeṭuttuk kūṟiyavāṟeṉṉi yeṉiṉ; toṇṭimāṉ iḷantiriyaṉ eṉṉum ac cōḻa maṉṉaṉ tirumāl kuṭiyiṟṟōṉṟiṉōṉeṉa irunilaṅ kaṭanta tirumaṟu mārpiṉ, munnīr vaṇṇaṉ puṟaṅkaṭi yannīrt, tiritaru marapiṉuravō ṉumpal eṉum perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭi yaṭikaḷiṟ kūṟiṉārākaliṉ, av vēntaṉpāṟ pōm pāṇarkku avaṉ taṉ kuṭit teyvamāy vaḻipaṭun tirumālit toḻutu celkaveṉa aṟivuṟuttaruḷiṉārākaliṉ atu kaṭāvaṉṟeṉa maṟukka. iṅṅaṉamaṉṟit tirumālik kūṟiṉamiyāṉē ivar viṇavarā meṉiṉ, karuvi lōcciya kaṇṇakaṉ eṟuḻttōṭ, kaṭampamar neṭuvēḷ eṉṟu murukak kaṭavuḷiyuṅ kūṟiṉārākaliṉ ivar civa camayattiṟkuriyarām pōlumeṉa etirmaṟutturip pārkku iṟukkalākāmiyiṉ aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟutal vaḻuvuriyā meṉka. ivvāṟṟāl ivar iṉṉa camayattavar eṉat tuṇipuri virittaṟku valiya cāṉṟiṉmi kāṭṭappaṭṭatākaliṉ, ivar camayam ituveṉpatu tuṇiyappaṭātu pōlumeṉka.
aṟṟāyiṉum, ivaratu peyariṉ ṟaṉmiyi uṟṟu nōkkumiṭattu ivar civacamayattiṟkuriyār eṉpatu pulappaṭu kiṉṟatu. uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭar uruttiraṉukkuk kaṇpōṟ ciṟanta iḷiya piḷḷiyārāṉa murukak kaṭavuḷik kuṟippatoṉṟām. civacamayan taḻīi yoḻukuṅ kuṭiyiluḷḷārē appeyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkutal marapāyp pōtarak kāṇṭaliṉ, ivvāciriyar civacamayattiṟ kuriyarām pōlumeṉak kūṟal iḻukkātu.
8. pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉ
iṉi ippāṭṭuṭit talivaṉāṉa cōḻaṉ karikāṟ peruvaḷavaṉ iṟṟikku āyirattuttoḷāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ cōḻanāṭṭiṉ talinakarāṉa kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattiṟ ceṅkōlōcciṉāṉ. ivaṉ kiṟittu piṟanta mutal nūṟṟāṇṭiṉkaṇ aracurimi peṟalāyiṉāṉeṉpatum, maturi mānakariṟ kaṭiccaṅkamum ivaṉ kālattētāṉ nirampap polivu peṟṟirunta teṉpatum yām eḻutiya māṇikkavācakar kālam eṉṉum nūlil iṉitu viḷaṅka virittuk kāṭṭap paṭṭaṉa. aviyellām īṇṭeṭuttuk kāṭṭaluṟiṉ itu varampiṉṟi viriyumākaliṉ āṇṭuk kaṇṭukoḷka. kaṭic caṅkattārāṟ ṟokukkappaṭṭa pattuppāṭṭukaḷil iraṇṭu pāṭṭukaḷ iyaṟṟiya āciriyar uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉārum āyirattut toḷāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa vareṉpatum itaṉāl viḷaṅkaṟ pālatē yām.
iṉi, ic cōḻamaṉṉaṉ tāṉ iḷiñaṉā yirunta kālattup pakivarāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭuc ciṟikkaḷattēyiṭappaṭṭāṉeṉpatūum, piṉ ac ciṟikkaḷattiṉiṉṟun tappippōyt taṉ aracurimiyiṉi eytip piṉpu appakivarmēṟ paṭiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟu avari yellām veṉṟā
ṉeṉpatūum ip paṭṭiṉap pāliyiṉ iṟutip pakutiyāl naṉku pulappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉṉum ivaṉ iḷiñaṉāyiruntapōtu orukāl ivaṉ pakivar ivaṉik kollumporuṭṭu ivaṉirunta illattit tīkkoḷuva, ivaṉ ataṟku añcātu attīyiṉaṭuvē pōy appāṟpaṭṭu uyir piḻittāṉeṉpatum, aṅṅaṉam at tīyik kaṭantu celluṅkāl ivaṉ kāl karintupōyiṉami paṟṟiyē ivaṉ karikāṟ cōḻaṉeṉṉum peyar peṟuvāṉāyiṉa ṉeṉpatum, ivaṉ taṉ aracurimiyiṉi eytuṅkāl ivaṉṟaṉ ammāṉāṉa irumpiṭarttaliyār eṉṉum nallicip pulavar ivaṟkup peruntuṇiyāyiruntaṉa reṉpatum, ivaṉ cēramāṉ peruñcēralātaṉoṭu veṇṇi yeṉṉum ūrppuṟattē pōrceytu veṟṟikoṇṭā ṉeṉpatum, aṅṅaṉam pōriyaṟṟuṅkāl ac cēralātaṉukkut tuṇiyāyvanta pāṇṭiyaṉ ōruvaṉiyun tōlvi peṟac ceytāṉeṉpatum, piṉṉorukāl imayamali variyiṟ ceṉṟu āriyavaracari yellām veṉṟu ammalik kuvaṭṭiṉmēl taṉ pulikkoṭiyi nāṭṭiṉā ṉeṉpatum, iruṅkō vēḷiṉ vaḻivanta cuṟṟattārkku ivaṉ perum pakivaṉeṉpatum, ivaṉ tamiḻppulavarkaḷi mikavum pātukāttu vantamiyāl avarkaḷāṟ peritum naṉku matikkappaṭṭu vantāṉeṉpatum paḻamoḻi, porunarāṟṟup paṭi, puṟanāṉūṟu mutaliya nūlkaḷāl iṉituviḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
9. pāṭṭiṉ nalam viyattal
iṉi, ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ uḷḷa poruḷamitiyiṉ nalattic ciṟitu ārāyvām. orupāṭṭiṉkaṇ aminta poruḷukkum ataṉiviṭṭut taṉiyē kiṭanta poruḷukkum vēṟupāṭu peritām. taṉiyē kiṭanta poruḷ aṟivukkup pulappaṭum avvaḷavēyaṉṟi vēṟu iṉpam payappataṉṟām. pāṭṭiṉkaṇ aminta poruḷō aṟiviṟkup pulappaṭuvatuṭaṉ nam maṉaniṉiviṉ uṇarcciyi eḻuvittu iṉpam payappatoṉṟām. tiṅkaḷ vāṉil viḷaṅkukiṉṟateṉṟu oruvaṉ urittāl avvuri nam aṟivukku ōruṇmiyi aṟivikkum avvaḷavēyaṉṟi ataṉmēṟ ṟaruvatu ciṟitumilli yaṉṟō? avvāṟaṉṟik,
koḻuntā rakimuki koṇṭalam
	pācaṭi viṇmaṭuvil,
eḻuntār matikkamalam eḻiltanteṉa.
eṉṉun tirucciṟṟampalak kōviyār tiruppāṭṭi oruvar urippak kēṭṭāl, virinta nīlavāṉam eṉṉum nīrmaṭuvil mukiṟkuḻāmākiya paccili paraviyiruppa iṭiyiṭiyē vāṉmīṉkaḷākiya veṇṭāmari mukikaḷ niṟintu viḷaṅka avaṟṟiṉ naṭuvil muḻu veṇṭiṅkaḷākiya oru veṇṭāmari alarntu aḻakuṭittāyt tōṉṟukiṉṟatu eṉṉum acceyyuṭporuḷ nam aṟivukkup pulaṉātaloṭu nam maṉaniṉiviṉ uṇarviṉiyum eḻuvittu iṉpantarutal kaṇṭāmaṉṟō? maṟṟu iṅṅaṉam iṉpam payattāl acceyyuṭporuḷukku maṭṭum evvāṟu poruntu
vatāyiṟṟu eṉṟu nuṇukippārkkumiṭattu, irākkālattē muḻunilavuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭṭa av vāṉiṉ tōṟṟatti nilattiṉkaṇṇuḷḷa oru maṭuviṉ ṟōṟṟattōṭu oppatākavittuk kāṭṭiṉamiyāl ataṟku avviṉpam viḷikkum āṟṟal poruntalāyiṟṟeṉpatu naṉku pulaṉām. ākavē, pāṭṭiṉkaṭ collappaṭum poruḷukku icinta vēṟoru tōṟṟattiṉi eḻupputal ceyyuḷiyaṟṟum nallicippulavarkku maṭṭum vāyntatoru tiṟamām. iṅṅaṉam avar ceyyum ceykittiṟattiṉiyē ilakkaṇa nūlār, uvami, uruvakam eṉa vēṟupeyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvar. poruḷkaḷik kiṭantavāṟē collik koṇṭupōm neṟi taṉminaviṟci eṉavum, aṅṅaṉañ collip pōtaṟku iṭiyiṭiyē uṇarvu caliyāmip poruṭṭuc cuvi vēṟupaṭutti apporuḷōṭu iyinta piṟaporuṭ ṭōṟṟatti eḻuppuneṟi uvami, uruvakam eṉavum peyarpeṟā niṟkum.
iṉṉum ivvuvami uruvakam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vēṟākap piṟar āyiram puṉinturikaḷ kūṟiṉārāyiṉum aviyellām ivvuvami uruvakam eṉṉum iraṇṭilē yaṭaṅkum. tammāṟ kūṟappaṭum poruḷkaḷiṉ vaḻiyē aṟivu celluṅkāṟ, pulavarkku iyaṟkiyāyt tōṉṟum oppumikaḷ uvami uruvakam eṉṉum ivviraṇṭē yākaliṉ, ivviyaṟki neṟi tiṟampi mikavum iṭarppaṭṭup palappalavāṉa puṉinturika ḷellām amittup poruḷuṇmi tirittuk kaṟpārtam maṉa uṇarvic citikkum piṟarellām pulavareṉap paṭutaṟkuc ciṟitum uriyar ākār eṉpatūum, avarāṉ amikkappaṭum avvaṇikaḷellām veṟṟāravāra veṟum pōlikaḷēyā meṉpatūum uṇmi yaṟivuṭiyārkkellām iṉitu viḷaṅkaṟpālaṉavēyām. ivvuṇmi kaṭippiṭittaṉṟē oppuyar villāc cennāvaṉmic cevvaṟivut teyvap peṟṟiyāḷarāṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār poruḷati kārattuḷ uvamaviyal eṉa oṉṟē vakuttu uvamam oṉṟiyē aṇinturiyāka vittu viḷakkiṉār. it teyva āciriyaroṭu tiṟampip piṟar tamakku vēṇṭiyavāṟellām aṇikaḷip perukki eḻutiṉārāyiṉum aviyellān tolkāppiyattiṉ muṉ talitūkkamāṭṭāvā yoḻital kāṇka.
iṉip paṭṭiṉappāli yeṉṉum ivvarumaruntaṉṉa nūli eḻutiya uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār eṉṉum nallicip pulavar itaṉkaṇ irupatu uvamikaḷ kāṭṭiyirukiṉṟār. ivvuvamikaḷi ōriṭattu orucērak kūṟātu poruḷkaḷik kiṭantavāṟē collik koṇṭu pōmvaḻi iṭiyiṭiyē cuvi vēṟupaṭuttik kaṟpārkku uṇarveḻucci uṇṭāmāṟu avi tammi ūṭē ūṭē iṉitamittiṭukiṉṟār. uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉārum poruḷkaḷik kiṭantavāṟē kūṟiṉarāyiṉ, ataṉāṉ maṉa uṇarviṉik kavartal ēlātāleṉiṉ; aṟṟaṉṟu; ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷil iyaṟkiyaḻakuḷḷaṉavum aḵtillaṉavumeṉa iru pālaṉa uḷa. āciriyar uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār icceyyuṭkaṇ ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷil aḻakāṟ ciṟantu makkaḷ maṉuṇarvi eḷitilē kavartaṟ payattavāṉa ariya periya poruḷkaḷik kūṟum vaḻiyellān taṉmi naviṟciyum, avaṟṟiṭiyē iyaṟkiyaḻakup poruḷiṉ vēṟāvaṉa cila vantāl, avaṟṟi aḻakupeṟak kūṟal vēṇṭi uvamiyum vitturikkum nuṭpam mikavum pārāṭṭaṟ pālatoṉṟām.
iṉik, kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattu uppaṅkaḻiyil nellēṟṟik koṇṭuvanta paṭakukaḷ taṟikaṭōṟum kaṭṭappaṭṭiruttaṟku ilāyattilē variciyākak kaṭṭap paṭṭirukkum kutiri variciyiyum, cuṟṟik kari eḻuppi viḷaṅkum malarp poykikaḷukku maka(m)mīṉāṟ cūḻappaṭṭa matiyiṉiyum, aṅkuḷḷa cōṟākkuñ cālikaḷiliruntu perukiyōṭuṅ kañcikku yāṟṟiṉ veḷḷattiyum akkañci ularntu puḻutiyāy mēṟpaṭinta araṇmaṉi vīṭukaḷukku veṇṇīṟṟiṟ puraṇṭeḻunta kaḷiṟṟi yāṉikaḷiyum, kaṭaṟkariyiṉ mēṭṭuk kuppattilē uḷḷa valiya cempaṭavar karaṭikkūṭattil oruvarōṭoruvar piṇintu maṟpōr puritalukku nīlavāṉil valappuṟamāyc cuḻaṉṟu vāṉmīṉkaḷoṭu kalantu celluṅ kōḷkaḷiyum, ac cempaṭavar tammuṭiya kuṭici vīṭukaḷil tūṇṭiṟ kōliyum mīṉ iṭum puṭṭiliyuñ cārtti vittiruttaṟkup pōrkkaḷattiliṟanta maṟavaṉukku niṟuttiya kalliṉ etirilē ūṉṟiya vēliyuṅ kēṭakattiyum, avartam kuṭici vīṭṭu muṟṟattil veṇmaṇalilē valiyi ulara vittiruttaṟku nilaviṉ iṭiyilē cērnta iruḷiyum cekkac civanta nīriyuṭiya kāviriyāṟu kaṭaloṭu kalattaṟkuk kariyamaliyic cērnta cekkar vāṉiyum tāyiṉ koṅkiyit taḻuvik kiṭanta makaviyum, cuṅkac cāvaṭiyiluḷḷa kāvalāḷar iravum pakalum oḻiyātu taṅkaṭami ceytiruttaṟkuk katiravaṉ ṟēriṟ pūṭṭappaṭṭu iṭiyaṟātu cellum kutiriyiyum, marakkalattil vanta carakkik kaṭaliṉiṉṟu kariyilēṟṟu taṟkum kariyiṟ kiṭantavaṟṟik kaṭaṉmēṟ kappalilēṟṟutaṟkum mukil kaṭalil mukanta niri maliyiṟ poḻitaliyum aṅṅaṉam maliyiṟ poḻinta nīr piṉ kaṭaliṟ cēṟaliyum, kaṭaṟkariyiṟ paṇṭacāli muṟṟaṅkaḷilē aṭukkik kiṭakkum mūṭṭikaḷiṉmēl āṇnāykaḷum āṭṭuk kiṭāykaḷum ēṟik kutittu viḷiyāṭutaṟku malip pakkaṅkaḷilē ēṟik kutikkum varuṭi māṉkaḷiyum, mātarkaḷ murukavēḷit toḻutaṟ poruṭṭākat talimēṟ kuvitta toṭikkikaḷukkuk kāntaṭ ceṭiyiṉ kaṇukkaḷiṟ kiḷitta kavinta mukikkulikaḷiyum, terukkaḷiṉ irupuṟattum nāṭṭappaṭṭa tukiṟ koṭikaḷukku yāṟṟiṉ irukariyilumuḷḷa karumpiṉ pūviyum, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉak kaṭaṟṟuṟi mukattil acintu koṇṭirukkum marakkalaṅkaḷukkuk kaṭṭut taṟiyi acikkuṅ kaḷiṟṟiyāṉikaḷiyum, vēḷāḷaratu naṭuvu nilimikku nukattaṭiyiṟ ṟitta pakalāṇi yiṉiyum, karikāṟcōḻaṉ iḷiñaṉāyiruntapōtu pakivaratu ciṟikkaḷattil aṭikkappaṭ ṭiruntamikkuk kūṭṭilaṭikkap
paṭṭa pulikkuṭṭiyiṉiyum, piṉ avvaraciḷiñaṉ acciṟik kaḷattiṟ kāvalari yellāṅ kaṭantu pōyt taṉ aracurimi yiṉik kippaṟṟiyataṟkut kuḻiyil vīḻnta āṇyāṉi ak kuḻiyiṉit tūrntu mēlēṟippōyt taṉ peṭṭiyāṉiyic cērntataṉiyum, pōrkkaṭiyāḷamāṉa pūḷippūvum uḻiñippūvuñ cūṭivanta paṭikkuk kuṟṟucceṭi pampiya karuṅkaṟ pāṟiyiṉiyum, pōr muraciṉ vāykkup pituṅkiṉa pēyiṉ kaṇṇiyum āciriyar uruttiraṅkaṇṇār āṇṭāṇṭu uvamikūṟi ippāṭṭi mikavun tiṟampaṭa amittatu kāṇka.
iṉṉum ivar ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷik kiṭantavāṟē vittu urikkumiṭattum, kuṭakamaliyiṟ ṟōṉṟiya kāviriyāṟu cōḻaṉāṭṭiṉkaṇ eṉṟum nīr aṟātu nirampip poṉ koḻittu oḻukutalum, kaḻaṉikaḷellām eppōtum viḷintu koṇṭiruppak karumpālikaḷiṟ karumpi naṟukkip piḻintu pāku kāyccutalum, erumik kaṉṟukaḷ neṟkūṭukaḷiṉ niḻalilē āṅkāṅku uṟaṅkik kiṭattalum, teṉṉi vāḻi kamuku mā paṉi mañcaḷ iñci cēmpu mutaliyaṉa mikavuṅ koḻumiyāy neṭuka vaḷarntu aṭarntiruttalum, ciṟu peṇkaḷ vīṭṭu muṟṟattil nellulara vittuk kāvalā yiruppac ciṟār ciṟutēr celutti viḷiyāṭutalum, kaṭaṟkari uppaṅkaḻikaḷilē paṭakukaḷ variciyāka niṟṟalum, kaṭaṟkariyiṟ kāṉaṟcōli vaḷañ ciṟantu tōṉṟutalum, malarvāvikaḷ ērikaḷ aṭiciṟcālikaḷ araṇmaṉikaḷ māṭṭuk koṭṭilkaḷ puttar ciṉar maṭaṅkaḷ kāḷikōyilkaḷ mutaliyaṉa āṅkāṅkiruttalum, cempaṭavar tam kuppaṅkaḷilē iṟāmīṉ cuṭṭut tiṉṟum vayalāmiyip puḻukki uṇṭum aṭampu āmpal mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ pūkkaḷ cūṭiyum oruvarō ṭoruvar cerukkuṟṟuk kavaṇkal vīca avaṟṟiṟku añcip paṉimaraṅ kaḷiluḷḷa puṭkaḷ paṟantupōtalum, ac cempaṭavaratu puṟaccēriyil paṉṟikaḷuṅ kōḻikaḷum ulāva āṭṭukkiṭāy kaḷum kutārippaṟavikaḷum viḷiyāṭa uṟikkiṇaṟukaḷ pala iruttalum, ciṟu kuṭici vīṭukaḷiṉuḷḷē tūṇṭiṟkōlum mīṉiṭum puṭṭiluñ cārttivikkappaṭṭiruttalum, avvīṭṭu muṟṟaṅkaḷiṉ maṇalilē valikaḷ ulartalum, aṅkuḷḷa paratavarum parattiyarum muḻunilavu nāḷilē cuṟāmīṉ kompi naṭṭuk kaṭaṟṟeyvattiṟkut tiruviḻāk koṇṭāṭutalum, nakarattuḷḷa māntar mēṉmāṭaṅkaḷilē yāmattiṟ palavakiyāṉa iṉpantuyttu uṟaṅkiṉamiyāl aviyātuviṭṭa viḷakkaṅkaḷik kaṭṭumarattil mīṉpiṭikkappōy vikaṟiyil vanta paratavar eṇṇip pārttalum, kaṭi yāmattil ap paratavar kaṭaṟkari ekkar maṇalilē uṟaṅkik kiṭattalum, cuṅkaccāvaṭiyiṟ cuṅkaṅ koḷḷuṅ kāvalar aḷaviṟanta paṇṭaṅkaḷi yellām tam aṟivāl aḷantu pārttuc cōḻaṉukkuriya puli muttiri yiṭṭup pōkkit taṅkaṭami vaḻātu ceytalum, paṇṭacāli muṟṟaṅkaḷilē malipōl aṭukkappaṭṭa mūṭṭikaḷiṉmēl nāyum āṭṭukkiṭāyum ēṟi viḷiyāṭutalum, nakarattiluḷḷa makaḷir murukavēḷ tiruviḻāvik kaṇṭu toḻutaṟ poruṭṭu mēl māṭaṅkaḷiṟ cāḷaravāyilip porunti niṉṟu ki kūpputalum, an nakaramellām palatiṟappaṭṭa koṭikaḷ nāṭṭappaṭṭu viḷaṅkutalum, parumāviliruntu vanta kutirikaḷ maliyamānāṭṭiliruntu vanta miḷaku potikaḷ imayamaliyi liruntu vanta palavaki maṇikaḷ potiyamaliyiliruntu vanta cantaṉa akiṟkaṭṭikaḷ ilaṅkik kaṭalil eṭutta muttukaḷ kīḻkaṭaliṉiṉṟuṅ koṇarnta pavaḷaṅkaḷ kaṅki kāviri īḻam mutaliya viṭaṅkaḷiliruntu vanta uṇavup poruḷkaḷ mutaliyaṉavellām kaṭitteruvukaṭōṟun tokukkap paṭṭiruttalum, pulāṉ maṟuttup poyyāvoḻukka mēṟkoṇṭuvāḻum vēḷāḷar palatiṟappaṭṭārkkum utavi puritalum, palatēyattiṉiṉṟum vanta makkaḷ oruvarōṭoruvar aḷavaḷāviyuṟitalum, karikāṟ cōḻaṉ pakivariṭṭa ciṟikkaḷattiṉiṉṟun tappippōyp piṉ ap pakivarmēṟ paṭi tiraṭṭi vantu vantu avar nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷip pāḻpaṭuttiṉamiyāl avaruṭiya poykikaḷ maṉṟaṅkaḷ araṇmaṉikaḷ nakaraṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉavellam veṟum pāḻāyk kiṭattalum, aṅṅaṉam veṟṟiyiṟ ciṟantu pōntu taṉ nakarattil attāṇi maṇṭapattē avaṉ aracu vīṟṟirukkuṅkāṟ palatēya maṉṉarum pōntu avaṉ maruṅkiruntu avaṉēviya toḻil kēṭṭu niṟṟalum, pakivarkku iṉṉaṉāyiṉum taṉṉōṭorumiyuṭiyāriṭattu avaṉ mikka aṉpuṭiyaṉā yoḻukutalum piṟavum nam maṉak kaṇṇetirē arumpeṟal ōviyameḻutik kāṭṭiṉāṟpōl aḻaku kaṉiya viḷakkik kāṭṭiyavāṟuṅ kāṇka. iṅṅaṉam ulakaviyaṟkip poruḷ
kaḷuḷḷum aḻakāṉ mikac ciṟantavaṟṟiyē terinteṭuttu avi tammit tamatu nuṇṇaṟivāl poruntak kuḻittut tam arumit tirumoḻikaḷāl nam neñcappaṭāttil amiti peṟa varintu paṭṭiṉappāli iyaṟṟiya āciriyar uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉāratu nallicip pulami centamiḻp pukaḻmikku oru nantā maṇiviḷakkāmeṉka.
iṉi, ivvāciriyar ulaka viyaṟkipporuṭ ṭōṟṟaṅkaḷi iṭiyaṟātu tirintu kaṇṭu viyantu kaḻiperumakiḻc ciyeyti vantaṉareṉpatu ip pāṭṭiṉ kaṭṭeṟṟeṉap pulappaṭukiṉṟatu. vāṉampāṭip paṟavi maḻittuḷiyi uṇṭu  uyirvāḻumeṉpatum, kuyiṟpaṟaviyun tūtuṇam puṟāvum taṉittuḷḷa kāḷikōṭṭattil otuṅkiyirukku meṉpatum, cempaṭavar cēriyiṟ kōḻi kutāri mutaliya puṭkaḷirukku meṉpatum, pāḻpaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷilē kūki kōṭṭāṉkaḷ uṟiyumeṉpatum ivarāl mikavum naṉṟākac collappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉum erumikkaṉṟukaḷ marutanilattiṟ kiṭattalum, kaḷiṟṟiyāṉikaḷ veṇṇīṟṟiṟ puraḷalum, māṭukaḷ koṭṭilkaḷil tokuti tokutiyāka niṟṟalum, iṟāmīṉkaḷ āmiyiṟiccikaḷ cempaṭavarāl tiṉṉappaṭutalum, paṉṟiyuṅkuṭṭikaḷum avar cēriyil mikutiyāyiruttalum, cuṟāmīṉ kompukaḷi avar vaḻipaṭutalum, naṇṭip piṭittu āṭṭutalum, malippakkaṅkaḷil varuṭi eṉṉum oruvaki māṉkaḷ tuḷḷik kutittalum, āṭṭukkiṭāykaḷ paṇṭacāli muṟṟaṅkaḷiṟ ṟiritalum, kaṭṭuttaṟiyiṟ piṇikkappaṭṭu niṉṟa kaḷiṟṟiyāṉikaḷ avaṟṟi yacittalum, paratavartaṅ kuṭicivīṭukaḷil mīṉkaṟiyum āṭṭiṟicciyum porittalum, kuḻiyil vīḻnta yāṉi ak kuḻiyit tūrttalum, poykikaḷil mutalikaḷ kiṭantulāvutalum, nīrvaṟṟiya vāvikaḷil aṟalpaṭṭa kompukaḷuṭiya māṉkaḷ tuḷḷutalum aṅṅaṉamē collappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa; maṟṟum neytal aṭampu āmpal tāḻi veṇkūtāḷam kāntaḷ karumpu pūḷi uḻiñi kuvaḷi mutaliya pūkkaḷum, teṅku vāḻi kamuku mā paṉi mutaliya maraṅkaḷum, mañcaḷ cēmpu iñci putavam cerunti mutaliya ceṭikaḷum ivarāṟ puṉintu kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi tammāl ivvāciriyar ulakaviyaṟkiyiṉi vaḻātu tirintukaṇṭu viyakkumiyal puṭiyā reṉpatu teṟṟeṉap pulappaṭukiṉṟataṉṟō?
iṉi, ivar iyaṟṟamiḻvalla nallicip pulavarātaloṭu vāṉa nūliṉum1 vallavarākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟār. ivar, tām iyaṟkip poruḷkaḷi moḻintu celluṅkāl avi tamakku vāṉmīṉ ṟōṟṟaṅkaḷi uvamiyāka eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟār; maḻi peyyātu vaṟattaṟku veḷḷi eṉṉuṅ kōḷ taṉṉili tirintu teṉṟicip pakkamāyc cellutalē kāraṇameṉkiṉṟār; tiṅkaḷic cūḻnta makamīṉi nīr nirampiya vāvikkum ataṉ karikkum uvamiyākak kūṟukiṉṟār; vāṉmīṉkaḷum kōḷkaḷum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu viravutali oruvarō ṭoruvar piṇintu maṟpōr iyaṟṟum cempaṭavarkku uvamiyāka eṭukkiṉṟār.
aṟṟēl, akkālattu vāṉ nūlārāycci uṇṭōveṉiṉ; iṟṟikku āyirattu nāṉūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉiruntu āriyapaṭṭarāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa vāṉ nūliṟ piṟṟiñāṉṟi vāṉnūlārāṟ collappaṭukiṉṟa in nilamaṇṭilam taṉṉittāṉē cuṟṟutalum, ñāyiṟṟiṉ mēlum tiṅkaḷiṉ mēlum paṭum niḻalkaḷ nilamaṇṭilattiṉ cāyalātalum mutalāṉa ariya periya uṇmikaḷ iṉitu viḷakkappaṭṭiruttalāṉum, avari aṭuttut tōṉṟiya varāka mikirarāl mēṟkōḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭa cūriya cittāntam eṉṉum vāṉ nūl mikappaḻiya toṉṟātal aṟivuṭiyārāl naṉku tuṇiyappaṭutalāṉum2 paṇṭināḷil vāṉ nūlārāycci mikutiyāyiruntateṉpatu tēṟṟamā meṉka. paṇṭik kālat tirunta tamiḻppulavar ilakkaṇa ilakkiyam vallārāta lōṭu ēṉi uṇminūṟpulamiyum uṭaṉ vāyppap peṟṟiruntār. orucārār icittamiḻum, piṟitorucārār nāṭakattamiḻum, maṟṟorucārār ōviya nūlum, vēṟoru cārār maruttu nūlum, piṉṉorucārār vāṉnūlum, maṟṟi orucārār camayanūlumākap palappala payiṉṟu viḷaṅkiṉār; avaravarkkuc ciṟappup peyarkaḷāy avaravariyaṟ peyarkaḷōṭu kūṭṭi vaḻaṅkappaṭum maruttuvaṉ ṟāmōtaraṉār, maturik kaṇakkāyaṉār eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉavē itaṟkuc cāṉṟā meṉka. iṉṉum ivi virikkiṟ perukum.
iṉi, ivvāciriyar ip pāṭṭi iyaṟṟumiṭattup payilvōruṇarvu calippaṭiyāvāṟu evviṭattil ep poruḷi evvaḷavu kūṟutal vēṇṭumō avvaḷavē kūṟippōtalāṉum, aṅṅaṉam apporuḷi aḻakupeṟak kūṟumiṭattum payilvōr iḵteṅṅaṉam muṭiyum iḵteṅṅaṉam muṭiyum eṉṟu mēṉmēl avāyccolla avarkku muṭivu teri vēṭkiyiṉi eḻuvittuk koṇṭu pōy ip pāṭṭi muṭikkumāṟu mutalilēyē iṉitu viḷakkappaṭṭamiyāṉum ivar nuṭpavaṟivum ivar tam uḷḷattiṉ uṇarccivaliyum aḷakkaṟpālaṉa vallaveṉka.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	Astronomy
2.	Dr. Kern
10. ippāṭṭiṉkaṭ ṭōṉṟiya paḻiyanāḷ vaḻakka voḻukka varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukaḷ
innūluṭ putta camaṇkurukkaḷ uṟiyum maṭaṅkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭiruttalāl, iḵtu eḻutappaṭṭa kālattup puttacamaṇmataṅkaḷ nirampavum paraviyiruntaṉaveṉpatu iṉitaṟiyappaṭum. in nūl eḻutap paṭutaṟku iru nūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ ceṅkōl occiya acōkaṉ eṉṉum puttavēntaṉ putta āciriyar palarip palaticikaḷukkum palatēyaṅkaḷukkuñ celaviṭuttu āṅkāṅkup puttacamaya aṟavuṇmikaḷi mikavirittu viḷakki avitammi nili
peṟuvittu vantāṉeṉpatum, cēracōḻapāṇṭiyamaṉṉar aracu purinta tamiḻnāṭṭilum aṅṅaṉamē accamayam nili nāṭṭappaṭṭateṉpatum avvacōkamaṉṉaṉ cetukkiya kalveṭṭukkaḷāl1 naṉku pulappaṭutalāṉum, in nūleḻutappaṭṭapiṉ nāṉūṟṟimpatu āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittu intiyāviṟku vanta pākiyāṉ eṉṉuñ cīṉa āciriyar kālattilellām putta
camayam mikavuñ ceḻippuṟṟiruntateṉpatu avvāciriyar varintu vitta vaḻinaṭik kuṟippukaḷāl iṉitu tuṇiyappaṭutalāṉum acōkaṉ kālattiṟkuc cila nūṟṟāṇṭu piṟṟōṉṟiya in nūliṉkaṇ ap putta maṭaṅkaḷ kuṟippikkappaṭṭami mikavum poruttamuṭittēyāmeṉka.
aṟṟēl, camaṇmatamum appōtirunta teṉak kūṟiya
teṉṉiyeṉiṉ; puttacamayam nilaviya kālattu ataṉoṭu camaṇmatamum uṭaṉ nilāviṟṟeṉpataṟku mikappaḻiyavāṉa ciṉa cūttiraṅkaḷum2 maṇimēkalik kāppiyamum, puttar aṟivurikaḷumē cāṉṟāmākaliṉ atuvum muṉṉiruntami tuṇiporuḷēyām.
iṉi, āriyamuṉivar palar pirāmaṇaṅkaḷiṟ collap
paṭṭa vēḷvikaḷ pala vēṭṭuvantaṉa reṉpatum itaṉkaṭ kuṟikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. pōrkkaḷattil añcātu etirniṉṟu pōrmalintu pakivaṉ vāḷāṟ pōḻappaṭṭu iṟanta ariya maṟavaṉi niṉivukūrtaṟ poruṭṭu avaṉip pōl uruttiraṭṭiya karuṅkalli naṭṭu ataṟketirilē avaṉuṭiya vēṟpaṭiyiṉi ūṉṟip pariciyit toṅkaviṭutal vaḻakkamāyiruntatu; āciriyar teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉārum eṉṉimuṉ nillaṉmiṉ ṟevvir palareṉṉi, muṉṉiṉṟu kaṉṉiṉ ṟavar eṉṟu ivvaḻakkattik kuṟittirukkiṉṟār; cilappatikārattiluñ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉṉuñ cēramaṉṉaṉ kaṇṇakikkuk kalniṟuttiṉami viritturikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.
iṉi, iṟṟiñāṉṟiṟ pōlavē, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉak kaṭaṟṟuṟi mukattil ēṟṟumati iṟakkumati ceyyum paṇṭaṅkaḷi yellām pātukāttu avaṟṟiṟkuk kaṭami ivvaḷaveṉṟu varampaṟuttuc cōḻavēntaṉ aṭiyāḷamāṉa pulimuttiri yiṭṭuc cuṅkaṅkoḷvōr tantoḻil māṟātu ceytu vantaṉar. itaṉāl iṟṟikku iraṇṭāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē tamiḻaracar celuttiya ceṅkōlaracu muṟikaḷ mikavun tiruttamākavum oḻuṅkākavum naṭipeṟṟu vantami naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟatu.
iṉi, akkālattu vīṭukaḷ paṭikaḷvittuk kaṭṭappaṭṭa uyarnta tiṇṇikaḷum palakaṭṭukkaḷuñ ciṟuvāyil peruvāyil iṭikaḻi mutaliyaṉavum amintaṉavāyum mēlmāṭaṅkaḷ pala uḷḷaṉavāyum iruntaṉa. mēṉmāṭaṅkaḷiṟ celvamakaḷir iruntami viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
iṉik kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam mutalāṉa nakaraṅkaḷilum nāṭukaḷilum murukakkaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭutalum avarkkut tiruviḻāk koṇṭāṭutalumē perumpāṉmiyā yiruntaṉa. itaṟkut tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi mutalāṉa paḻiya nulkaḷuñ cāṉṟām. eṉavē, murukakkaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu mikavun toṉṟutoṭṭu naṭipeṟuvateṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
iṉi, akkālik kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil vāṇikam perukuṅ kaṭitteruvukaḷ palaviruntaṉa. avaṟṟil aricip purikaḷ, carakku aṟikaḷ, pākku veṟṟilik kaṭikaḷ, meḻukkuppotinta pēḻikaḷ, kaḷḷuk kaṭikaḷ, maliyanāṭṭu miḷakupotikaḷ, mēru iyam mutalāṉa vaṭamalikaḷiṉiṉṟuṅ koṇarnta maṇikaḷ poṟkaṭṭikaḷ viṟkuṅ kaṭikaḷ, kuṭakumaliyiliruntu vanta cantaṉakkaṭṭi akiṟkaṭṭi kuvitta maḷikik kaṭikaḷ, ilaṅkikkaṭal muttu vaṅkāḷakkaṭaṟ pavaḷam kaṅkiyāṟṟup poruḷkaḷ kaṭāra tēyattu uṇāpporuḷkaḷ cīṉattup paṭṭukaḷ eṉa iviyellām niṟinta peruṅkaṭikaḷ ākiya palavum nirampi neruṅki iruntaṉa. iṉṉum parmātēyattiliruntu vanta kutirikaḷ pantiyiṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭu viliceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉṉum an nakarattil, pala nūlkaḷun tuṟipōkak kaṟṟa arumpeṟal āciriyar palar oruṅku cērntu vaḻakkāṭip poruḷārāyntu iṉpuṟuṅ kalikkaḻakaṅkaḷum3 iruntaṉa.
iṉik kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattiṟ, kolittoḻil nikaḻāvāṟu ceytu pulāṉmaṟuttu vāymipēcutaliyē oḻukalāṟāyk koṇṭu tēvarkku vaḻipāṭu iyaṟṟiyum, āriyarkkup poruḷutavi pala purintu avar vēṭkum vēḷvikaḷi naṭappittum, nāṉmaṟiyōtuvāri atu tiṟampātu ceyyumāṟu niliniṟuttiyum, ulaku nilipeṟutaṟkāṉa tam uḻavut toḻilic ciṟakka naṭipeṟuvittum, koḷvatum mikipaṭāmaṟ koṭuppatuṅ kuṟipaṭāmal vāṇikam naṭāttiyum pukaḻmēmpaṭṭa paḻiya vēḷāḷar kuṭiyiruppukaḷum iruntaṉa. itaṉāl, paṇṭināḷil vēḷāḷar peruñ ciṟappuṟṟirun tārkaḷeṉpatum, ēṉik kuṭimakkaḷellārum ivarkaḷuṭiya pērutaviyāl uyir vāḻntiruntaṉa reṉpatum naṉkaṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉit turukkitēcattuc cōṉakar cīṉar yavaṉar mutalāṉa pala tēyamakkaḷum tamakkuriya pala moḻikaḷum vaḻaṅkikkoṇṭu kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil vantu vāṇikam naṭātti vāḻntaṉar. ivarellām iṅṅaṉam innāṭṭiṟ kuṭiyēṟi vāḻntapōtu tamakkuḷ māṟiṉṟi orumiyuṟṟiruntaṉa reṉpatum itaṉāṟ ṟerikiṉṟatu.
iṉi, muṉṉāḷil ōr aracaṉ piṟaṉōr aracaṉoṭu porutu avaṉi veṉṟāl avaṉ nāṭṭiluḷḷa makaḷiric ciṟipiṭittu varuta luṇṭeṉpatum, aṅṅaṉam piṭittavariṉum avarik kaṟpaḻittu māṉañ citittu varuttāmal pātukāttuk kōyilkaḷiṟ kaṭavuḷit toḻutu koṇṭu aṅku kaṭavuṭṭiruppaṇi ceyyumpaṭi iruttuvar eṉpatum aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāṟ paṇṭi nāṭ ṭamiḻaracariṉ ceṅkōṉmi yaracum uḷḷap perumiyum iṉitu viḷaṅkaṟ pālaṉa.
iṉi, muṉṉāḷil kaṭavuḷit toḻutal vēṇṭic camikkappaṭṭa kōyilkaḷ ampalaṅkaḷ eṉṉum peyaruṭiyavāyiruntaṉa. avaṟṟiṉkaṭ kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭutaṟkut toḻukuṟiyāka niṟuttappaṭṭa uru kantu eṉap peyarpeṟṟatu. avvuruviṉiyē ik kālattiṟ civaliṅkam eṉa vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. in nāḷiṟpōla annāḷil makkaḷuruvi yoppac ceyta tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ perumpāluṅ kiṭiyā. civaliṅkam eṉappaṭukiṉṟa kantu uruviyē vaḻipaṭṭuvantaṉar. ic civaliṅka uruttāṉ mikap paḻiyanāṭ ṭoṭṭu ellāc cātiyārāṉum ellāt tēyattārāṉum vaṇaṅkappaṭṭu vantateṉa varalāṟṟu nūlārāycci valla pulavōrkaḷ uriniṟuvukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉālaṉṟē civapirāṉ ṟirukkōyil kaṭōṟum mutaṉmiyāṉa karu illil ic civaliṅka aruṭkuṟiyum, mutaṉmi yillā ēṉi iṭaṅkaḷil ēṉi uruvaṅkaḷum niṟuttappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. nacciṉārkkiṉiyaruṅ kantu eṉpataṟkut teyvamuṟiyun taṟi eṉṟuri kūṟiṉārākaliṉ īṇṭu atu civaliṅkamēyātal teṟṟeṉat tuṇiyappaṭum. tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭiyilum nakkīraṉār kantuṭi niliyiṉum eṉṟu aruḷic ceytami kāṇka. aṟṟēl, nākarikam nirampiya paṇṭināḷil makkaḷuruppōl tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ camittu vaḻipaṭāmi eṉṉiyeṉiṉ; kāṇavuṅ karutavum paṭāta mutalvaṉukkut tāntām niṉintavāṟu uruvaṅkaḷ camittu vaṇaṅkal poruntātākaliṉ, am mutaṟporuḷi oru piḻampākak koṇṭu vaḻipaṭutaṟku ōr aṟikuṟiyākap piḻampu vaṭivāṉa kanturu niṟuttiṉāreṉka. piḻampuruviṉ vaḻipāṭupōl ēṉi uruviṉ vaḻipāṭukaḷ ciṟavāmi uṇmi nūlkaḷuṭ kaṇṭuteḷika.
iṉi, muṉṉāḷil vēḷāḷar aracarāyiruntu ceṅkōl celuttiṉārātalum, avar aracupurinta nāṭu oḷi nāṭu ātalum itaṉkaṇ viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum oḷiyarāvār maṟṟi maṇṭalattiṟku aracarātaṟkuriya vēḷāḷar eṉṟuri virittār. iṉṉum, mulli nila makkaḷāṉa iṭic cātiyārkkuḷḷum palar aracarāyiruntaṉa reṉpatūum itaṉkaṭ pulappaṭukiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam patiṟṟuppattil āṉ payam vāḻnar kaḻuvuḷ talimayaṅka eṉṟu pōnta pakutiyiṉāl kaḻuvuḷ eṉṉum ōr aracaṉ iṭic cātiyāṉeṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
iv viruvakic cātiyārōṭu aruvāḷanāṭṭiṉ aracarum vaṭa nāṭṭiluḷḷa aracarum kuṭakunāṭṭiṉaracarum taṉṉiṭattu vantu paṇintukiṭappap karikāṟ cōḻaṉ vīṟṟiruntāṉ. pāṇṭiya aracaṉ oruvaṉi ivaṉ veṉṟamiyum, iruṅkōvēḷ eṉṉum aracaṉiṉ vaḻic cuṟṟamāvāri aḻittamiyum ik karikālaṉukkup peruveṉṟiyākac collappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. iḵtoḻiya, iv vēntaṉ pala nāṭukaḷi yellām aḻittu nakaraṅkaḷ putukkik kuḷaṅkaḷ akaḻvittu iḷamarakkākkaḷ viḷivittu nākarikam perukkiṉāṉeṉpatum, ivaṉukku mutalil aracirukkiyāyiruntatu uṟiyūr ākumeṉpatum, piṉ ataṉi viṭuttuppōṉālum atu pāḻpaṭāmal pala naṟkuṭikaḷi āṅku irutti māṭaṅkaḷ māḷikikaḷ mutalāṉa putuk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ amippittā ṉeṉpatum ip pāṭṭiṉkaṇ viḷakkamākac collappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	Asoka’s edict V, XIII
2.	Prof. Jacobi’s introduction to jina Sutras. Asoka’s edict, VIII, XI.
3.	Debating Halls
11. pāvum pāṭṭiṉ naṭiyum
tamiḻmoḻiyiluḷḷa ceyyuḷiṉ ōci amitiyē pā eṉṟu peyarpeṟuvatām. oruvaṉ. toliviṭattiliruntu oru pāṭṭup pāṭa ap pāṭṭiṉ coṟporuḷ iviyeṉṟu pulappaṭāvāyiṉum, avvōci varumāṟṟi uyttuṇarntu kāṇpāṉukku avaṉ pāṭuñ ceyyuḷ iṉṉa pāveṉṟu aṟiyak kiṭakkum. iṅṅaṉam nuṇṇiya vicumpiṉkaṇ alialiyāy eḻumpi ōr oḻuṅkāka varum ociyē pāvākumeṉat tolkāppiyar ceyyuḷiya luriyilē pērāciriyarum naṉku viḷakkiṉār. ivvāṟu tōṉṟip parampuñ ceyyuḷiṉ oci muṟiyit tamiḻāciriyar veṇpā akavaṟpā kalippā vañcippā eṉa nālvakippaṭuttiṉār. piṉṉum in nālvakip pāviṉ oci neṟiyi nōkkumiṭattu veṇpāviṟ kalippāvum akavaṟpāvil vañcippāvum aṭaṅkuvaṉavām. iḵtu āciriya naṭittē vañci yēṉi, veṇpā naṭittē kaliyeṉa moḻipa1 eṉṟum, pāviri maruṅkiṉip paṇpuṟat tokuppiṉ, āciriyappā veṇpā veṉṟāṅ kāyiru pāviṉu ḷaṭaṅku meṉpa2 eṉṟum āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟumāṟṟāṉ naṉku viḷaṅkum.
ivi tammuḷ āciriyap pāviṟkuriya  akavalōci eṉpatu oruvaṉ tāṉ karutiyaṉavellām varampupaṭātu collikkoṇṭa pōmvaḻi iyaṟkiyē toṉṟun toṭarpu oliyākum; veṇpāviṟkuriya ceppalōci eṉpatu oruvaṉ kēṭpap piṟaṉoruvaṉ ataṟku māṟucolla naṭipeṟuñ coṉmuṟiyil iyaṟkiyē tōṉṟi iṭiyiṭiyē niṟṟalpeṟṟuc celluvatām. maṟṟum ivaṟṟil aṭaṅkum vañcippāviṟkuriya tūṅkalōci eṉpatu ov or aṭitoṟum toṭarpuṟṟu niṟintu cellum ēṉi ocikaḷ pōlātu ovvoru cīrtoṟu niṟintu teṟṟuppaṭṭu niṉṟu celvatākum; kalippāviṟkuriya tuḷḷalōci eṉpatu ici vēṟupāṭu tōṟṟi ocikaṉintu tuḷḷittuḷḷic celvatām.
iṉip paṭṭiṉappāli eṉṉumippāṭṭu akavalōci taḻuvi vanta āciriyappāviṉāl amikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. ic ceyyuḷiṉkaṉ āciriyar uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār tām urippak karutiyaṉavellām oru toṭarpāka vittu munnūṟṟōraṭikāṟu murittārākaliṉ ip pāviṉkaṇ iyaṟkiyāy eḻuvatu akavalōciyē yāyiṟṟu. aṟṟēl, vaciyil pukaḻ vayaṅku veṇmīṉ, tici tirintu teṟkē kiṉum eṉpavi pōṉṟa vañcippā aṭikaḷ iṭiyiṭiyē kalakkat tūṅkalōci mayaṅkivantavā ṟeṉṉiyeṉiṉ; ippāṭṭiṉ mutaṟṟoṭṭu muṭivukāṟum akavalōciyē vantatāyiṉ iṭiyiṭiyē oci vēṟupaṭāmal orōciyāy naṭantu kēṭpārkku veṟupput tōṟṟuvikkumākaliṉ, aṅṅaṉam ākāmipporuṭṭu vañcikkuriya tūṅkalōciyiṉi āṅkāṅku vēṟupaṭu māṟṟāl nayamuṟa icivittup pāṭuvārāyiṉar. nāḷum pālamiḻtiṉiyē uṇpāṉukku ataṉ cuvi uvarppākat tōṉṟumākaliṉ aṅṅaṉam aḵtu ākāmip poruṭṭu iṭiyiṭiyē puḷiṅkaṟiyuñ cuviyāṟ palatiṟappaṭṭa mā palā vāḻi mutaliya paḻaṅkaḷum uṇṭu, ap pāṟcuvi iṉitu nikaḻumāṟu ceyvittuk kōṭalpōla, ip pāviṉ akavalōciyum iṉpaṅ kuṟiyāmip poruṭṭu āciriyar uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār tūṅkalōciyiṉiyum āṅkāṅkuc ceṟivittum pāṭuvārāyiṉar.
iṉi, ivvakavaṟpāviṉ aṭikaḷ ovvoṉṟum nāṉku cīrkaḷāṟ kaṭṭappeṟuvaṉām. in nāṟcīraṭiyi āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār aḷavaṭi eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. iṉi, pāṭṭiṉ iṭiyē mayaṅkiya vañcippāṭṭiṉ aṭikaḷ ovvoṉṟum iraṇṭu cīrkaḷāṟ kaṭṭappaṭuvaṉavām. ivviru cīraṭiyi avar kuṟaḷaṭi eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. ip paṭṭiṉappāliyiṉ mutaliṉiṉṟu irupattōraṭi kāṟum, irupattēḻu mutal irupatteṭṭu kāṟum, muppattiraṇṭu mutal aimpattu mūṉṟukāṟum, aimpattoṉpatu mutal aṟupattāṟukāṟum, eḻupattoṉṟu mutal eḻupattāṟukāṟum, eḻupatteṭṭu mutal eṇpattu nāṉku kāṟum, eṇpattāṟu mutal toṇṇuṟṟoṉṟu kāṟum, toṇṇuṟṟu mūṉṟu mutal toṇṇuṟṟu nāṉkukāṟum, toṇṇuṟṟeṭṭumutal nūṟukāṟum, nūṟṟāṟumutal nūṟṟeṭṭukāṟum, nūṟṟuppatiṉāṉku mutal nūṟṟirupattēḻukāṟum, nūṟṟirupattoṉpatu mutal nūṟṟu muppattēḻukāṟum, nūṟṟunāṟpattiraṇṭu mutal nūṟṟimpat toṉṟukāṟum, nūṟṟimpattāṟu mutal nūṟṟimpat teṭṭukāṟum, nūṟṟaṟupattumūṉṟu mutal nūṟṟeḻupatukāṟum, nūṟṟeḻupattiraṇṭu mutal nūṟṟeṇpattoṉṟukāṟum, nūṟṟuttoṇṇuṟṟintu mutal nūṟṟut toṇṇuṟ ṟoṉpatukāṟum, irunūṟṟoṉṟu mutal iru nūṟṟiraṇṭu kāṟum, iru nūṟṟintu mutal irunūṟṟeṭṭu kāṟum, iru nūṟṟup patiṉoṉṟumutal irunūṟṟup patiṉmūṉṟukāṟum, irunūṟṟeḻupattu mūṉṟu mutal irunūṟṟeḻupattintu kāṟum, iru nūṟṟeḻupat toṉpatu mutal irunūṟṟeṇpattu nāṉkukāṟum, irunūṟṟeṇpattāṟu mutal irunuṟṟut toṇṇuṟ ṟoṉṟukāṟun tūṅkalōci taḻīi vanta vañciyaṭikaḷ vantaṉa. ēṉi nūṟṟumuppatteṭṭu aṭikaḷum akavalōci taḻīi vanta āciriyappāvāmeṉka. eṉavē, nūṟṟaṟupattu mūṉṟaṭikaḷ vañcippāviṟkuriya vāyiṉum avi tamakkuriya tūṅkalōci āciriyappāviṟkuriya akavalōciyiṉ iṭiyiṭiyē cīrtoṟun teṟṟuppaṭṭuc cella varuvatē yallatu ataṉiṉ vēṟallāmiyāl avvōciyāṉ naṭakkum av vañciyaṭikaḷ ivvāciriyappāṭṭiṉkaṇ varutaṟkup peritum iyipuṭiyavām eṉka. iṅṅaṉam akavalōciyun tūṅkalōciyum pāluntēṉuṅ kalantāṟpōlak kalantu cellumāṟu ip pāṭṭiṉi iyaṟṟip payilvārkkut tōṉṟum ociyiṉpam iṭiyaṟātu cella naṭāttiya āciriyar uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār iciyaṟivu māṭci mikavum viḷakkaṟpālatoṉṟām.
iṉi, vañci aṭikaḷ akavalaṭikaḷi āṇṭāṇṭu orucērak kūṟumiṭattum avi orōciyāyc cella voṭṭāmal etuki mōṉi mutaliya eḻuttamiti kaḷāṉuñ ciṟitu ciṟitu ōci vēṟupaṭuttup pōkiṉṟār. peruñciṉattāṟ puṟakkoṭāa, tiruñceruvi ṉikaṉ moympiṉōr eṉpuḻippōlac cilaviṭattu ivviraṇṭaṭikaḷ mutaṟcīr etuki oṉṟap poruttu kiṉṟār. vēṟu cilaviṭaṅkaḷil cōṟuvākkiya koḻuṅkañci, yāṟupōlap parantoḻuki, yēṟuporac cēṟākit tērōṭat tukaḷ keḻumi, nīṟāṭiya kaḷiṟupōla, vēṟupaṭṭa viṉiyōvattu eṉpuḻippōla iraṇṭaṭikaḷiṉ mēlum mutaṟcīr etukiporunta amit
tiṭukiṉṟār. piṉṉuñcilaviṭattu vāṉpoyppiṉun tāṉ poyyā, koḻumpalkuṭic ceḻumpākkattu,  nelloṭu vanta valvāyp paḵṟi, paṇinilip puraviyiṉaṇimutaṟ piṇikkum eṉṟāṟpōla ōraṭi yiṉuḷḷēyē mutaṟcīrum iraṇṭāñcīrum, mutaṟcīrum mūṉṟāñcīrum etuki poruntap pāṭukiṉṟār. orōviṭattu akavalaṭiyil vārēṉ vāḻiya neñcē kūrukir, nuṇṇiti ṉuṇara nāṭi naṇṇār peṟṟavi makiḻtal ceyyāṉ ceṟṟōr eṉṟāṟ pōla mutaṟcīrum nāṉkāñcīrum etuki porunta amittiṭu kiṉṟār. maṟṟum orōviṭattu māttāṉi maṟamoympiṟ, ceṅkaṇṇāṟ ceyirttu nōkki eṉpuḻippōla etukiyiṉṟi mutaṟcīr iraṇṭāñcīr mōṉi oṉṟavum, vicipiṇimuḻaviṉ vēntar cūṭiya eṉpuḻippōla mutaṟcīrum mūṉṟāñcīrum mōṉi oṉṟavum iyittiṭukiṉṟār.
iṉi āciriyar tammāṟ pāṭappaṭum poruḷvaḻiyē tam aṟivi vittu moḻintu pōkuṅkāl iṭarppaṭātu tōṉṟum etuki mōṉikaḷiyē amittiṭukiṉṟār. poruḷurikkēṟṟa etuki mōṉi eḷitiṟ ṟōṉṟā
viṭattu etukipōla ōci poruntuñ coṟkaḷip patittiṭukiṉṟār; itaṟkuk kārkkarumpiṉ kamaḻālit tītteṟuviṟ kaviṉvāṭi, iṉamāvi ṉiṇarp peṇṇi, mutaṟcēmpiṉ muḷiyiñci, muṉikeṭac ceṉṟu muṉcama murukkit, talitavac ceṉṟu taṇpaṇiyeṭuppi eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattu aṭikaḷ eṭuttuk kāṭṭām; ivaṟṟuḷ, kārk karumpu; tītteṟuvu, iṉamāviṉ; mutaṟcēmpiṉ, muṉikeṭa: talitava eṉpaṉa uṇmi etuki yallāviṭiṉum atupōl oci oṉṟiyiruttal kāṇka. iṉṉum orōviṭattu ivvōcip poruttamuṭiya coṟkaṭāṉum eṭutta poruḷukku aṭuttunillāviṭiṉ etukimōṉi ciṟitumiṉṟi yuñcoṟkaḷi yamittuc celkiṉṟār; iḵtu, uṇavil vaṟuṅkūṭ ṭuḷḷakat tiruntu, vaḷivāyk kūki naṇpakaṟ kuḻaṟavum tērpūṇṭamāa pōla, vikaṟōṟu maciviṉṟi, ulakuceyak kuṟipaṭātu eṉpavaṟṟiṟ kāṇappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam poruḷukku iciyac coṟ poruttum muṟi ivvāciriyarōṭoru kālattiṉarum ivarkku muṉṉōrumāṉa paṇṭic centamiḻt taṇṭā nallicip pulavar tamakkellām potuvilakkaṇamām. piṟṟi ñāṉṟit tamiḻppōlip pulavarō poruṭciṟappuc ciṟitumiṉṟi veṟuñ collāravāra amippilēyē tam aṟivik kaḻittut teḷitamiḻukkun tamakkum iḻukkan tēṭuvārāyiṉar; aṅṅaṉam avar ceytal uyirakat tillāp piṇavuṭampip palavakiyāl oppittuk kaṇṭu makiḻtalō ṭokkumākaliṉ, avar iciyāppulami aṟivuṭiyārk kellā vaciyāyt tuyarantarum nīrmit tāmeṉṟoḻika.
iṉi, ip pāṭṭiṉ naṭiyaḻaku mikavum pōṟṟaṟpāla toṉṟām. iṉiya teḷitamiḻccuvi yūṟiya muḻumuḻuc coṟkaḷāl ip pāṭṭu muṟṟum amintirukkiṉṟatu. avaṟṟiṉkaṭ ṭoṭarpupaṭṭu eḻūum iṉṉōci kuḻal yāḻ mutaliya karuvikaḷiṉiṉṟu pōtarum olipōl mikavunti kutiyāniṉṟatu. ivaṟṟip payiluṅkālattu nam aṟivu am mellōci vaḻicceṉṟu uruki mikavun tūyatāṉa mēlulakattil uyarappōkiṉṟatu. nīr maṭiyilē teḷḷatteḷinta aruvinīr tiraṇṭu oḻukutalpōl ic ceyyuḷōciyum melleṉṟu moḻumoḻuveṉac celkiṉṟatu. mati niṟinta malipaṇṭam, potimūṭip pōrēṟi, mayiliyaṉ māṉōkkiṟ, kiḷimaḻali meṉcāyalōr, vaḷinuḻiyum vāy porunti, kuḻalakava yāḻmurala, muḻavatira muraciyampa, viḻavaṟā viyalāvaṇattu māyitaḻk kuvaḷiyoṭu neytalu mayaṅkik, karāaṅ kalitta kaṇṇakaṉ poykik, koḻuṅkāṟ putavamoṭu cerunti nīṭic, ceṟuvum vāviyu mayaṅki nīraṟ, ṟaṟukōṭṭiraliyoṭu māṉpiṇi yukaḷavuṅ, koṇṭi makaḷiruṇṭuṟi mūḻki, yanti māṭṭiya nantā viḷakkiṉ,  malaraṇi meḻukka mēṟip palartoḻa, vampalar cēkkuṅ kantuṭip potiyil eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattuc cila aṭikaḷi nōkkiṉum ic ceyyuḷiṉ melleṉṟa ōciyiṉimi naṉkuviḷaṅkum. māmpaḻaccāṟu peyta kuṭuviyiṉ eppakkattē pottiṭṭu nāvinīṭṭiṉum ataṉvaḻiyē yoḻukum ac cāṟu tittittalpōla, ivvariya ceyyuḷiṉ evviṭattē aṟivu tōyiṉum āṇṭu iṉimiyē viḷiyāniṟkiṉṟatu. ātalāl, eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya ivvaṭikaḷ maṭṭumē cuvikaṉin tiruppaṉa veṉṟu niṉital iḻukkāmeṉka.
itaṉkaṇṇuḷḷa ovvoru collum poruṭkaru nirampik koḻuntīyiṟṟeṟitta poṟipōl nammaṟivik koḷuttip palaporuṭ ṭōṟṟaṅ kāṭṭi viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. payaṉpaṭātu niṟkuñ col oṉṟāyiṉum itaṉkaṭ kāṇap paṭuvatilli. vilivarampaṟiyāp paṭṭāṭiyiṉ kaṇ ovvōr iḻiyum piṇintuniṉṟu avvāṭi yiṉi ākkutalpōla, itaṉkaṇ ovvoru collum oṉṟiyoṉṟu kuvik koṇṭu icceyyuḷi ākkukiṉṟatu. peyarviṉic coṟkaḷi aṭuttuniṟkum aṭimoḻit toṭarkaḷellām uṭampoṭukūṭiya uṟuppuppōl icivu peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉum ippāṭṭiṉ coṉṉayam poruṇayaṅkaḷ virippiṟ perukum.
iṉi, ippāṭṭiṉkaṭ ciṟitēṟakkuṟiya āyirattu munnūṟṟupat toṉpatu (1319) coṟkaḷirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuṭ patiṉorucoṟkaḷ (11) vaṭacoṟkaḷām: avi makam, aṅki, āvuti, pūtam, matu, pali, patāki, amarar, kaṅki, puṇṇiyam, camam eṉpaṉavām, ñamali eṉṉum oru col pūḻi nāṭṭiṟkuriya tikiccollākum. ākavē ip pāṭṭil nūṟṟukku oṉṟu viḻukkāṭu  (1%) piṟanāṭṭuc coṟkaḷ kalantaṉa veṉpatu aṟiyaṟpāṟṟu. itaṉāl ivvāciriyar kālattiṟ ṟamiḻ mikavum tūyatāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu vantateṉpatu pulappaṭum eṉka.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	tolkāppiyam, ceyyuḷiyal 108
2.	tolkāppiyam, ceyyuḷiyal 107
12. viḷakka urikkuṟippukaḷ
ip paṭṭiṉappāliyiṉ urikkuṟippukaḷ cila īṇṭeḻututaṟkumuṉ, itaṉ poruḷkōḷ muṟiyiṉic ciṟitu viḷakkutal iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭuvatākaliṉ ataṉi iṅku oruciṟitu teḷitturikkiṉṟām.
māṭṭu : pattuppāṭṭukkaḷil oṉṟākiya ippaṭṭiṉappāli yum itaṉoṭu cērnta ēṉi oṉpatu pāṭṭukkaḷum poruṭṭoṭarpuṟṟu mikanīṇṭu amintaṉavāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. oru caṅkiliyiṟ palakaṇkaḷ iruttalpōl ip pāṭṭukkaḷiṭattum akaṉṟu niṉṟu poruḷ poruntukiṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷ pala uḷavām. oruporuḷ ōriṭattuc  ceṉṟu oruvāṟṟāṉ muṭintum muṭiyāmalum niṟpap, piṟitoru poruḷ avviṭattiṉiṉṟuntōṉṟi naṭantupōyp piṟitōriṭattu muṭintum muṭiyāmalum niṟpa, iṅṅaṉamē iṭiyil varuvaṉavellām amiya, kaṭicip paṭiyāka appāṭṭiṉ poruḷ muṟṟup peṟuvatākum. ivvāṟu iṭiyiṭiyē muṭintum muṭiyāmalum niṟkum poruḷkaḷ kaṭiciyāka ap pāṭṭiṉ kaṭ karukkoṇṭa mutaṟporuḷic cārntu muṭiyumākaliṉ cārpu poruḷkaḷ eṉṟu peyarpeṟuvaṉa vākum. ic cārpu poruḷkaḷōṭu kūṭic ciṟantuviḷaṅkum poruḷ mutaṟporuḷ eṉappaṭum.
muṭiyaṇi vēntaṉoruvaṉ arumaṇi kuyiṟṟiya ceṅkōl kippaṟṟi ariyaṇi vīṟṟiruppa, avaṉic cārntuniṉṟu avaṉ ṟaṉ araciyaṟ cuṟṟamellām viḷaṅkiṉāṟpōla, oru pāṭṭiṉkaṭ karukkoṇṭa mutaṟporuḷ ataṟku ōruyirāyc ciṟantu niṟpa ēṉic cārpuporuḷ kaḷellām ataṉic cūḻntukoṇṭu ataṉic ciṟappittu avvāṟṟāl tāmum viḷaṅkāniṟkum.
cilloḷi vīcum vāṉmīṉkaḷ cūḻa iruntālallatu palloḷivīcum pāṉmativiḷakkam ciṟavātākaliṉ, cārpu poruṭ cērkkiyiṉṟi mutaṟporuḷ ciṟappuṭittāyc cellāteṉka. eṉavē, oru pāṭṭiṉ mutaṟporuḷic ciṟappittuk koṇṭu amintu kiṭakkuñ cārpuporuḷkaḷ iṉṉōraṉṉa perumpāṭṭukaḷil akaṉṟu niṟkumākaliṉ, uri kūṟaluṟum āciriyaṉ avi tammiyellām aṇukiya niliyil vittup poruḷurikkum neṟiyiṉiyē tollāciriyar māṭṭu eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉār, āṅkilattilum miliṭṭaṉ eṉṉum nallicip pulavar tuṟakka iḻappu eṉṉum tamatu arumperuṅkāppiyattiṟ pāṭṭukaḷi akaṉṟu poruḷkiṭappavit teḻutiṉār. ataṟkuri kaṇṭārum piṟarum1 an nuṇukkamaṟintu avitammiyellām aṇukiya niliyil vittu uriyurittār. iṅṅaṉamē iṉṉōraṉṉa pāṭṭukaḷukku uri urikkavēṇṭumeṉpatu āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār akaṉṟu poruḷ kiṭappiṉum aṇukiya niliyiṉum, iyaṉṟu poruṇmuṭiyat tantaṉaruṇarttal, māṭṭeṉa moḻipa pāṭṭiyal vaḻakkiṉ eṉṟu āṇitantaruḷiya vāṟṟāṉum, ataṟkurikaṇṭa pērāciriyar uritta uri viḷakkattāṉum teṟṟeṉat tuṇiyappaṭum.
iṉi, māṭṭu eṉṉum poruḷ kōḷmuṟi ituvēyātal aṟiyamāṭṭāta nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, ippattuppāṭṭukaṭkum orumuṟiyu miṉṟi ōriṭattu niṉṟa oru colliyum piṟitōriṭattu niṉṟa piṟitoru colliyum eṭuttu iṇittu uri urikkiṉṟār. aṅṅaṉa muṟikūṟutal nūlāciriyaṉ karuttukku muṟṟum muraṇātalāṉum, ivarkku muṉṉirunta nakkīraṉāri yuḷḷiṭṭa tollāciriyarevarum ivvāṟu uri urippak kāṇāmiyāṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urimuṟi koḷḷaṟpālataṉṟeṉa maṟukka.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyaruri kūṟāviṭiṉ ip pattuppāṭṭuk kaḷiṉ poruḷ viḷaṅkuta laritēyātal paṟṟiyum, ivar tolkāppiyam, kalittoki mutaliya paṇṭic centamiḻ nūlkaṭku ariyaperiya taṇṭamiḻuri vakuttatu paṟṟiyum ivari  neñcāravaḻutti ivarkkut toṇṭu pūṇṭoḻukuṅ kaṭappāṭumikavuṭiyē māyiṉum, ivar vaḻuviyaviṭaṅkaḷilum ivarippiṉ paṟṟi ulaki ēmāṟṟutal naṭuvuniliyākātākaliṉ ivar tam vaḻū ukkaḷik kaḷintu tiruttip piṉ ivaric centamiḻmāmukil vaḷḷalāyk koṇṭu vaḻipaṭuvāmeṉka.
iṉi, ip pāṭṭiṉappāliyiṉkaṭ pōnta poruḷ icivupeṟukiṉṟa poruttuvāykaḷ irupattēḻu uḷḷaṉa. avi tammiyellām iṟutiyil viṉimuṭittut tokuttuk kāṭṭumiṭattuk kāṇka. itaṉkaṭ karukkoṇṭa mutaṟporuḷ, 299 vatu varimutalāka muṭinta tirumāvaḷavaṉ tevvarkkōkkiya, vēliṉum veyya kāṉamavaṉ, kōliṉun taṇṇiya taṭameṉṟōḷē eṉpatu 218 mutal 220 āvatu varikāṟumuḷḷa muṭṭāc ciṟappiṟ paṭṭiṉam peṟiṉum, vāriruṅ kūntal vayaṅkiḻi yoḻiya, vārēṉ vāḻiya neñcē eṉpataṉoṭu poruntiniṉṟu muṭivatākum. ēṉiya vellām cārpuporuḷkaḷāy im mutaṟporuḷi aṇinturittaṟ poruṭṭu vantaṉaveṉka.
kuṟippu : iṅkē kuṟiyātu viṭappaṭṭavaṟṟiṟ kellām uri poruṭpākupāṭṭilum, piṉṉēyuḷḷa aruñcoṟporuḷ variciyiluṅ kāṇka.

(40 - 50) pulipoṟip... mācūṭṭum

(53-58) tavappaḷḷit.... cēkku
muṉivar vēṭkum pukiyi veṟuttalāṟ kuyilkaḷ cōliyiviṭṭuk kāḷikōṭṭattiṟpōyt tūtuṇam puṟākkaḷōṭum orupuṟattē taṅkum eṉa nērē poruḷ paṭutali viṭuttu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kāḷikōṭṭattip puṟākkaḷukku aṭimoḻiyākkip piṉ ap puṟākkaḷōṭuṅ kuyilkaḷ kāvilē ceṉṟirukkumeṉak kāvuṭaṉē cērttup poruḷurikkiṉṟār. aṅṅaṉam poruḷurippiṉ muṉivar vēḷvi vēṭkumiṭam ituveṉpatu peṟappaṭāmiyāṉum, kuyilkaḷ evviṭattiviṭṭu eṅkē pōyiruntaṉaveṉṟu viṉāvuvārkku iṟukkalākāmiyāṉum aḵturi yaṉṟeṉa maṟukka. iḷamarakkāvilē muṉivar vēṭkiṉṟāreṉṟum, aṅkeḻum pukiyi veṟuttu ac cōliyi nīṅkippōyk kuyilkaḷ kāḷikōṭṭattiṟ ṟaṅkukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum poruḷ kūṟutalē porutta muṭittātal kāṇka. kāḷikōṭṭam nakarppuṟattē uḷḷatu; muṉivar vikuṅ kā ataṟkuñ cēyttāy uḷḷateṉka. akni, ākuti eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷ aṅki āvuti eṉat tirintaṉa. kaṭinakar - accantaruṅ kāḷikōṭṭam. tūtu - ciṟukal.
(59 -74)mutumaratta.....pukarppōnti
karuntoḻil - kolittoḻil. kolit toḻililum, irumpu mutalāṉa valiya ulōkaṅkaḷ aṭittut tiraṭṭun toḻiliṉum paḻakiya kiyik karuṅki eṉpar; koṉṟu vāḻ toḻiliṉum vaṉpaṇit toḻiliṉuṅ, kaṉṟiya toḻiṟki karuṅki eṉap paṭumē (tivākaram, 16) eṉpatūuṅ
eṉpataṉoṭu cērttut tirumpavum 70 il uḷḷa tīṇṭi mutaliya viṉikaḷoṭu cērttup, piṟaku ataṉi 27 il uḷḷa pākkattōṭu iyittup periya tōr iṭarviḷittup pāṭṭiṉ poruṇayañ citittār. ivarpōl iṅṅaṉam urikkuḻappañ ceyvār piṟari vēṟu yāṇṭuṅ kaṇṭilam. ciṟār viḷiyāṭṭiyum, valiya perumakkaḷ pōr viḷiyāṭṭiyun  taṉittaṉiyē kūṟa vēṇṭumeṉak karutiya taṅkarutti, ak karuttukkuc ciṟitum iṭantarāta ākkiyōṉ tiruppāṭṭil iyippāṉvēṇṭi ataṉi aṅṅaṉamellām alittu varampiṉṟi yurikūṟat tuṇintatu avarkkup peritum ētamām eṉka.
iṉik karuntoḻiṉ mākkaḷ eṉpataṟku mīṉpiṭikkuṅ kolit toḻiliṟ paḻakiya valiya cempaṭavar eṉa vittu nērē poruḷurittuc ceṉṟāl ip pakuti āṟṟoḻukkāyp poruḷ payantu cevvitiṉ muṭiyumāṟṟip poruṭ
pākupāṭṭil varinta poḻippurikkaṭ kāṇka. kalimākkaḷ eṉpataṟkuc ciṟār eṉṟu poruḷurittaṟkup pāṭṭiṉ poruḷiyipu iṭantarāmiyōṭu accoṟṟoṭarum iṭantaruvatiṉ ṟām; eṉṉi? ciṟu makārkkuk karuntoḻil, valiya, cerukkiya mutalāṉa aṭimoḻikaḷ ēlāmiyāṉum, mākkaḷ eṉṉuñcol apporuṭarāmiyāṉu meṉpatu. maṟṟuk kiṭantavāṟē vittuk kalimākkaḷ cempaṭavar eṉapporuḷurippiṉ, ikaṉmoympiṉōr eṉpatum avariyē kuṟittuniṉṟu kūṟiyatu kūṟalāmeṉiṉ; aṟṟaṉṟu, cempaṭavar mēṟcoṉṉavāṟu kiyiṉuṅ kalattiṉum meyyuṟat tīṇṭip pōr iyaṟṟiyum ataṉāṉum tam vali yaṭaṅkāmiyiṉ māṟupaṭṭa tiṟaluṭiyarāyk kavaṇkal vīcuvārāyiṉareṉṟu uri kūṟak kiṭattalāl atu kūṟiyatu kūṟa lākāteṉka.
vaṟaḷ aṭampu - vaṟaṇṭamaṇaliṟ paṭarnta aṭappaṅ
koṭi ; ikkoṭi kaṭaṟkari maṇaliṟ paṭarumeṉpatu neytaṟkali (10) yuḷḷum, patiṟṟup pattiṉuḷḷuṅ (30 ,51) kūṟappaṭutaliṉ itu vaṟaḷ eṉṉum aṭiyaṭuttuvantatu. nāḷ mīṉ - naṭcattiram; kōḷmīṉ - kirakam.
(75-77) paṟaḻppaṉṟip.... viḷiyāṭa
uṟi eṉpatu maṇṇāṟ cuvarpōṟ cūḻa vaṉintu ōraṭikkumēl uyaramākkic cuḷḷiyil vittuc cuṭṭeṭuppatu. ivvāṟu camitta uṟikaḷi nilitti akaḻntu oṉṟaṉmēl oṉṟāka aṭukkicceyvatu uṟikkiṇaṟu eṉṟu collappaṭum. mēḻakattakar - turuvāṭṭiṉ āṇ; itaṉi ēḻakam eṉṟum vaḻaṅkupa; ivviru collun turuvāṭṭiṉ ēṟṟukkup peyarātal tivākarattuṅ kāṇka. cival - kutārippaṟavi. puṟac cēri - iḻi toḻilāḷarāṉa cempaṭavar nakarkkup puṟattēyirukkum ūr; itu kuppatti aṭuttuḷḷatākum.

piṉmiyāṉum, āciriyar karuttukku māṟāy uvamamiṉṟip piṟaḻtalāṉum avaruri poruntāteṉa maṟukka.
neṭuttūṇṭiliṟkāḻ eṉṟamiyāṉ ataṉōṭu iṉamāṉa mīṉiṭum puṭṭilum īṇṭuk koḷḷappaṭum. kēṭakam mīṉiṭum puṭṭilukkum, vēl tūṇṭiṟkōlukkum uvamikaḷām.
ciṉiccuṟaviṉ kōṭunaṭṭu maṉiccērttiya vallaṇaṅ kiṉāl - karukkoṇṭa cuṟāmīṉ kompi naṭṭu ataṉiyē tāṉ irukkumiṭamākac cērtta valiya kaṭaṟ ṟeyvattiṉ poruṭṭu. maṉi - irukkumiṭam; iccol ipporuḷ paṭumākavum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar itaṉik koṉṉē vēṟupirittup pōy 83 - vatu variyiluḷḷa maṇaṉmuṉṟil eṉpataṉoṭu kūṭṭiṉār. neytaṉilattil nuḷiyarkku valivaḷan tappiṉ am makaḷir kiḷiyuṭaṉ kuḻīic cuṟavukkōṭu nāṭṭip paravukkaṭaṉ koṭuttaliṉ āṇṭu varuṇaṉ veḷippaṭum eṉpatu tolkāppiyap poruḷati kārattil nacciṉārkkiṉiya rurittavuri.
maṭital - tantoḻil ceyyātu cōmputal. kāvirit tuṟikku uvamiyāy nērē ceṉṟiyivatākiya peṟaṟ karun tolcīrt tuṟakkam eṉṉum aṭiyi 111 - vatu aṭiyiluḷḷa neṭuṅkālmāṭam eṉpataṉōṭu iyittu iṭarppaṭu
kiṉṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. kavaliyiṉṟip pala payaṉ nukarntu paratavar tam makaḷirōṭum iṉitu viḷiyāṭṭayarkiṉṟa kāvirit tuṟiyit, tēvar arampi mātarōṭum viḷiyāṭu
